          stage: (frameDataUrl || backgroundDataUrl) ? ARTWORK_STAGE.textureInvalid : ARTWORK_STAGE.textureMissing,
          hasFrame: Boolean(frameDataUrl),
          hasBackground: Boolean(backgroundDataUrl),
        });
        return null;
      }

      const spec = Object.freeze({ frameDataUrl, backgroundDataUrl });
      historicalUnitFrameSpecCache.set(cacheKey, spec);
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.unitQualityFrame, diagnosticSubject, {
        stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.specReady,
      });
      return spec;
    } catch (error) {
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.unitQualityFrame, diagnosticSubject, {
        stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.resolverError,
        errorCode: error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error',
      });
      warn('HISTORICAL_UNIT_NATIVE_FRAME_SKIPPED', error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  // Resolved while the attack slot is still live, for the same reason the current term
  // and Main Pet are: the value objects belong to the attack popup, and the Reference
  // outlives it. Failure stays local to one unit and leaves that unit unframed.
  function captureUnitNativeFrames(units) {
    const byId = new Map();
    if (!Array.isArray(units)) return byId;
    for (const unit of units) {
      const numericId = Number(callSemantic(unit, 'get_id', { optional: true }));
      if (!Number.isFinite(numericId) || numericId <= 0 || byId.has(numericId)) continue;
      try {
        const spec = getUnitNativeFrameSpec(unit, {
          family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.unitQualityFrame,
          subject: numericId,
        });
        if (spec) byId.set(numericId, spec);
      } catch (error) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.unitQualityFrame, numericId, {
          stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.resolverError,
          errorCode: error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error',
        });
        warn(`UNIT_NATIVE_FRAME_SKIPPED_${numericId}`, error);
      }
    }
    return byId;
  }

  function captureMainPetNativeFrame(slot) {
    try {
      const pet = callSemantic(slot, 'get_pet', { optional: true });
      const petId = Number(callSemantic(pet, 'get_id', { optional: true }));
      const subject = Number.isFinite(petId) && petId > 0 ? petId : 'main_pet';
      if (!pet) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.unitQualityFrame, subject, { stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.assetMissing });
        return null;
      }
      return getUnitNativeFrameSpec(pet, { family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.unitQualityFrame, subject });
    } catch (error) {
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.unitQualityFrame, 'main_pet', {
        stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.resolverError,
        errorCode: error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error',
      });
      warn('MAIN_PET_NATIVE_FRAME_SKIPPED', error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  function findObjectByClassLimited(root, targetClass, { maxDepth = 4, maxNodes = 80 } = {}) {
    if (!root || typeof root !== 'object') return null;
    const queue = [{ value: root, depth: 0 }];
    const seen = new Set();
    let scanned = 0;
    while (queue.length && scanned < maxNodes) {
      const { value, depth } = queue.shift();
      if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || seen.has(value)) continue;
      seen.add(value);
      scanned += 1;
      if (className(value) === targetClass) return value;
      if (depth >= maxDepth) continue;
      for (const child of Object.values(value)) {
        if (!child || typeof child !== 'object' || child instanceof Node || ArrayBuffer.isView(child) || child instanceof ArrayBuffer) continue;
        if (Array.isArray(child)) {
          for (const nested of child) if (nested && typeof nested === 'object') queue.push({ value: nested, depth: depth + 1 });
        } else {
          queue.push({ value: child, depth: depth + 1 });
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  function getImageFileUrl(file) {
    if (!file) return '';
    const url = callSemantic(file, 'get_url', { optional: true });
    return url == null ? '' : String(url);
  }

  function getRsxImageDependency(file) {
    if (!file) return null;

    // Current build exposes the loaded RSX dependencies through JK(); this is the
    // same path that was live-confirmed for pet_icons0.png. Prefer it over source probing.
    if (typeof file.JK === 'function') {
      try {
        const dependencies = file.JK();
        if (Array.isArray(dependencies)) {
          const hit = dependencies.find(item => className(item) === 'engine.core.assets.file.ImageFile');
          if (hit) return hit;
        }
      } catch (error) {
        warn('RSX_DEPENDENCY_JK_FAILED', error);
      }
    }

    for (const value of Object.values(file)) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const hit = value.find(item => className(item) === 'engine.core.assets.file.ImageFile');
        if (hit) return hit;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  function findUniqueSourceMethodOrNull(valueOrClass, predicate) {
    const matches = prototypeMethodsDeep(valueOrClass).filter(row => {
      try { return predicate(row.source, row.fn, row.name); } catch { return false; }
    });
    return matches.length === 1 ? matches[0].name : null;
  }

  function getIconAssetLifecycle(asset) {
    if (!asset) return null;

    // IconAsset itself does not expose lifecycle names semantically. Match only the
    // tiny, known wrapper methods used by AtlasTextureIconAsset / RsxIconAsset.
    // The patterns cover the current build (Ie/pl/Dk) and the two recently observed
    // variants (Je/ol/Dk and Je/ql/Jk), plus the current Je/ql/Ek wrappers.
    // If the game changes again, fail closed.
    const ready = findUniqueSourceMethodOrNull(
      asset,
      (source, fn) => fn.length === 0 &&
        /return this\.[A-Za-z0-9_$]+(?:\(\))?\.(?:Ie|Je)\(\)/.test(source)
    );
    const acquire = findUniqueSourceMethodOrNull(
      asset,
      source => /this\.[A-Za-z0-9_$]+(?:\(\))?\.(?:pl|ol|ql)\((?:this|[A-Za-z0-9_$]+)\)/.test(source)
    );
    const release = findUniqueSourceMethodOrNull(
      asset,
      source => /this\.[A-Za-z0-9_$]+(?:\(\))?\.(?:Dk|Jk|Ek|Bk)\((?:this|[A-Za-z0-9_$]+)\)/.test(source)
    );

    return ready && acquire && release ? { ready, acquire, release } : null;
  }

  async function ensureIconAssetReady(asset, numericId) {
    const lifecycle = getIconAssetLifecycle(asset);
    if (!lifecycle) return { ready: false, release: null };

    try {
      if (Boolean(asset[lifecycle.ready]())) return { ready: true, release: null };
    } catch (error) {
      warn(`UNIT_ICON_READY_CHECK_FAILED_${numericId}`, error);
    }

    let acquired = false;
    try {
      const acquireFn = asset[lifecycle.acquire];
      if (typeof acquireFn !== 'function') return { ready: false, release: null };
      if (acquireFn.length === 0) acquireFn.call(asset);
      else acquireFn.call(asset, asset);
      acquired = true;
    } catch (error) {
      warn(`UNIT_ICON_ACQUIRE_FAILED_${numericId}`, error);
      return { ready: false, release: null };
    }

    const release = () => {
      if (!acquired) return;
      acquired = false;
      try {
        const releaseFn = asset[lifecycle.release];
        if (typeof releaseFn === 'function') {
          if (releaseFn.length === 0) releaseFn.call(asset);
          else releaseFn.call(asset, asset);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        warn(`UNIT_ICON_RELEASE_FAILED_${numericId}`, error);
      }
    };

    const started = Date.now();
    while (Date.now() - started <= 3000) {
      try {
        if (Boolean(asset[lifecycle.ready]())) return { ready: true, release };
      } catch {}
      await sleep(50);
    }

    release();
    return { ready: false, release: null };
  }

  // A subject that is not already an asset-identifier token is rejected outright
  // rather than punched into one. Mangling "Guild <Name> 12" into "Guild__Name__12"
  // would keep the identity while looking sanitized; `unknown` keeps neither.
  function artworkDiagnosticSubject(subject) {
    if (subject === null || subject === undefined) return ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_UNKNOWN;
    const text = String(subject).trim();
    return ARTWORK_SUBJECT_PATTERN.test(text) ? text : ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_UNKNOWN;
  }

  // Only a declared family may be recorded, so a stray string cannot open a new
  // bucket that no documented family owns.
  function artworkDiagnosticFamily(family) {
    const value = String(family ?? '');
    return Object.values(ARTWORK_FAMILY).includes(value) ? value : ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_UNKNOWN;
  }

  function artworkDiagnosticToken(value) {
    if (typeof value !== 'string') return undefined;
    const text = value.trim();
    if (!text) return undefined;
    return ARTWORK_TOKEN_PATTERN.test(text) ? text : ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_REDACTED;
  }

  // Bounded structural copy for the evidence the resolvers already derive
  // mechanically: finite numbers, booleans, and identifier-shaped tokens such as
  // class names, method names, stage names and source-shape expressions. Native
  // geometry (`region`, `frame`, `textureFlatten`) arrives as client value objects
  // whose own enumerable numbers are exactly what the record wants, so plain
  // containers are walked. Keys that are not identifier-shaped, values that are not
  // one of those kinds, functions, symbols, DOM nodes, cycles, and anything past the
  // depth / breadth budget are dropped; free text is redacted.
  function artworkDiagnosticStructure(value, depth = 0, seen = new Set()) {
    if (value === undefined) return undefined;
    if (value === null) return null;
    const type = typeof value;
    if (type === 'boolean') return value;
    if (type === 'number') return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : String(value);
    if (type === 'string') return artworkDiagnosticToken(value);
    if (type !== 'object') return undefined;
    if (depth >= ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_LIMITS.depth || seen.has(value)) return undefined;
    if (typeof Node === 'function' && value instanceof Node) return undefined;
    seen.add(value);
    try {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const out = [];
        for (const item of value.slice(0, ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_LIMITS.arrayItems)) {
          const clean = artworkDiagnosticStructure(item, depth + 1, seen);
          if (clean !== undefined) out.push(clean);
        }
        return out;
      }
      const out = {};
      let kept = 0;
      let keys = [];
      try { keys = Object.keys(value); } catch { return out; }
      for (const key of keys) {
        if (kept >= ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_LIMITS.objectKeys) break;
        if (!ARTWORK_STRUCTURE_KEY_PATTERN.test(key)) continue;
        let raw = undefined;
        try { raw = value[key]; } catch { continue; }
        const clean = artworkDiagnosticStructure(raw, depth + 1, seen);
        if (clean === undefined) continue;
        out[key] = clean;
        kept += 1;
      }
      return out;
    } finally {
      seen.delete(value);
    }
  }

  // The payload is read through the field allowlist, never enumerated from the
  // caller's object, so an undeclared field cannot reach the record at all.
  function sanitizeArtworkDiagnostic(diagnostic) {
    const out = {};
    if (!diagnostic || typeof diagnostic !== 'object') return out;
    for (const [field, kind] of Object.entries(ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_FIELDS)) {
      let raw = undefined;
      try { raw = diagnostic[field]; } catch { continue; }
      if (raw === undefined || raw === null) continue;
      let clean;
      if (kind === 'token') clean = artworkDiagnosticToken(raw);
      else if (kind === 'flag') clean = typeof raw === 'boolean' ? raw : undefined;
      else if (kind === 'count') clean = Number.isFinite(raw) ? raw : undefined;
      else clean = artworkDiagnosticStructure(raw);
      if (clean !== undefined) out[field] = clean;
    }
    return out;
  }

  function artworkDiagnosticKey(family, subject) {
    return `${artworkDiagnosticFamily(family)}:${artworkDiagnosticSubject(subject)}`;
  }

  // The one diagnostic sink for every Reference artwork family. Recording never
  // changes what a resolver returns: callers record and then keep their existing
  // fail-soft result.
  function recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, subject, diagnostic = {}) {
    const familyKey = artworkDiagnosticFamily(family);
    const subjectKey = artworkDiagnosticSubject(subject);
    const key = `${familyKey}:${subjectKey}`;
    const numericId = Number(subjectKey);
    const row = {
      family: familyKey,
      subject: subjectKey,
      // Kept for the Issue #44 unit rows, whose consumers already read `id`.
      ...(Number.isFinite(numericId) ? { id: numericId } : {}),
      ...sanitizeArtworkDiagnostic(diagnostic),
    };
    const previous = artworkDiagnostics.get(key);
    artworkDiagnostics.set(key, row);
    if (artworkDiagnostics.size > ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_LIMITS.rows) {
      artworkDiagnostics.delete(artworkDiagnostics.keys().next().value);
    }
    if (JSON.stringify(previous) === JSON.stringify(row)) return row;
    if (ARTWORK_SUCCESS_STAGES.includes(row.stage)) log('ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC', row);
    else warn('ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC', row);
    return row;
  }

  function getArtworkDiagnostic(family, subject) {
    return artworkDiagnostics.get(artworkDiagnosticKey(family, subject)) ?? null;
  }

  // Resolving a spec and the browser actually loading that image are two different
  // steps, and the second one happens in a shared sprite helper long after the
  // resolver returned. Before this, a non-unit family whose image failed fell back to
  // its placeholder while its diagnostic row still said the resolver had succeeded.
  //
  // This only updates the row - it returns nothing the callers act on - so rendering
  // and fail-soft behaviour are unchanged. The previous row is carried forward so the
  // route and asset class the resolver established are not lost.
  function recordArtworkImageStage(family, subject, stage) {
    if (!family) return null;
    return recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, subject, {
      ...(getArtworkDiagnostic(family, subject) ?? {}),
      stage,
    });
  }

  function getNativeIconRenderMethod(asset, diagnostic) {
    const Provider = findClass(CLASS.iconContentProvider);
    const methods = prototypeMethodsDeep(Provider);
    const shapeExpressions = new Set(['this.shape']);
    const shapeGetter = findGetter(Provider, 'get_shape', { optional: true });
    if (shapeGetter) {
      shapeExpressions.add(`this.${shapeGetter}()`);
      const getter = methods.find(row => row.name === shapeGetter);
      const field = getter?.source.replace(/\s+/g, '').match(/^function\(\)\{returnthis\.([$\w]+);?\}$/)?.[1];
      if (field) shapeExpressions.add(`this.${field}`);
    }
    const shapeSetter = findGetter(Provider, 'set_shape', { optional: true });
    const setter = methods.find(row => row.name === shapeSetter)?.source.replace(/\s+/g, '');
    const argument = setter?.match(/^function\(([$\w]+)\)/)?.[1];
    if (argument) {
      const fields = [...setter.matchAll(new RegExp(`this\\.([$\\w]+)=${argument.replace(/\$/g, '\\$')}(?=[,;}])`, 'g'))]
        .map(match => match[1]);
      if (new Set(fields).size === 1) shapeExpressions.add(`this.${fields[0]}`);
    }
    const lifecycle = getIconAssetLifecycle(asset);
    const excluded = new Set(['dispose', ...Object.values(lifecycle ?? {})]);
    const sources = new Map();
    const shapeCalls = new Map();
    for (const row of methods) {
      // String/comment contents are not evidence of an executable call.
      const source = row.source.replace(/"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|'(?:\\.|[^'\\])*'|`(?:\\.|[^`\\])*`|\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|\/\/[^\r\n]*/g, ' ');
      sources.set(row.name, source);
      const matches = [];
      const calls = source.matchAll(/\b((?:this\s*\.\s*)?[$\w]+(?:\s*\(\s*\))?)\s*\.\s*([$\w]+)\s*\(\s*(this\s*\.\s*[$\w]+(?:\s*\(\s*\))?)\s*\)/g);
      for (const match of calls) {
        const name = match[2];
        const receiver = match[1].replace(/\s+/g, '');
        const shapeExpression = match[3].replace(/\s+/g, '');
        if (receiver === 'this' || !shapeExpressions.has(shapeExpression) || excluded.has(name)) continue;
        const fn = asset[name];
        if (typeof fn !== 'function' || fn.length > 1) continue;
        matches.push({ method: name, consumer: row.name, receiver, shapeExpression });
      }
      shapeCalls.set(row.name, matches);
    }

    // The native entry checks readiness, uses a preview while incomplete, and
    // registers a provider callback. Only that callback's shape call is final.
    // All names below come from the live methods, including the ready wrapper.
    const entries = [];
    diagnostic.unresolvedCompletionEntries = [];
    for (const [name, source] of sources) {
      if (!lifecycle) break;
      const compact = source.replace(/\s+/g, '');
      const previews = shapeCalls.get(name).filter(call => compact.includes(`${call.receiver}.${lifecycle.ready}()`));
      if (!previews.length) continue;
      const callbacks = new Set();
      // Haxe bind-helper / callback registration arguments; no helper name assumed.
      for (const match of source.matchAll(/(?:\(|,)\s*this\s*\.\s*([$\w]+)\s*(?=[,)])/g)) {
        if (sources.has(match[1]) && match[1] !== name) callbacks.add(match[1]);
      }
      for (const match of source.matchAll(/\bthis\s*\.\s*([$\w]+)\s*\.\s*bind\s*\(\s*this\s*\)/g)) {
        if (sources.has(match[1]) && match[1] !== name) callbacks.add(match[1]);
      }
      if (!callbacks.size) continue;
      // Prefer the callback shared by the already-ready branch and registration.
      // Without that evidence, multiple registered callbacks are not interchangeable.
      const readyCallbacks = new Set();
      for (const preview of previews) {
        const readyCall = `${preview.receiver}.${lifecycle.ready}()`.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
        const readyBranch = new RegExp(`(?:if\\(${readyCall}\\)\\{?(?:return)?|(?<![!$\\w.])${readyCall}(?:\\?|&&))this\\.([$\\w]+)\\(`, 'g');
        for (const match of compact.matchAll(readyBranch)) {
          if (callbacks.has(match[1])) readyCallbacks.add(match[1]);
        }
      }
      if (readyCallbacks.size) {
        entries.push({ name, previews, callbacks: [...readyCallbacks], evidence: 'ready_branch_and_registration' });
      } else if (callbacks.size === 1) {
        entries.push({ name, previews, callbacks: [...callbacks], evidence: 'single_registered_callback' });
      } else {
        diagnostic.unresolvedCompletionEntries.push({ entry: name, callbacks: [...callbacks] });
      }
    }
    const previewMethods = new Set(entries.flatMap(entry => entry.previews.map(call => call.method)));
    const entryNames = new Set(entries.map(entry => entry.name));
    const candidates = new Map();
    diagnostic.readyMethod = lifecycle?.ready ?? null;
    diagnostic.previewCandidates = [...previewMethods];
    diagnostic.completionRoutes = [];
    for (const entry of entries) {
      for (const callback of entry.callbacks) {
        if (entryNames.has(callback)) continue;
        const finals = shapeCalls.get(callback).filter(call => !previewMethods.has(call.method));
        diagnostic.completionRoutes.push({ entry: entry.name, callback, evidence: entry.evidence, finalMethods: finals.map(call => call.method) });
        for (const call of finals) candidates.set(call.method, { ...call, entryMethod: entry.name, completionCallback: callback });
      }
    }
    diagnostic.candidates = [...candidates.values()];
    if (!entries.length) {
      diagnostic.stage = !lifecycle ? 'native_lifecycle_unresolved'
        : diagnostic.unresolvedCompletionEntries.length ? 'native_completion_route_ambiguous' : 'native_completion_route_not_found';
      return null;
    }
    if (candidates.size !== 1) {
      diagnostic.stage = candidates.size ? 'native_final_render_ambiguous' : 'native_final_render_not_found';
      return null;
    }
    const contract = [...candidates.values()][0];
    diagnostic.renderMethod = contract.method;
    diagnostic.consumerMethod = contract.consumer;
    diagnostic.entryMethod = contract.entryMethod;
    return contract.method;
  }

  function readIconTextureProperty(texture, semantic) {
    let method = null;
    try {
      method = findGetter(texture?.__class__, semantic, { optional: true });
      if (!method) return { status: 'missing', method: null };
      if (typeof texture[method] !== 'function') return { status: 'not_callable', method };
      return { status: 'ok', method, value: texture[method]() };
    } catch (error) {
      return { status: 'threw', method, errorCode: error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error' };
    }
  }

  function inspectIconTextureTransform(texture) {
    const nodes = [];
    const seen = new Map();
    let current = texture;
    let end = 'depth_limit';
    let cycleTo = null;
    // Follow only the semantic parent chain, never an arbitrary game object graph.
    while (current && typeof current === 'object' && nodes.length < 8) {
      if (seen.has(current)) {
        end = 'cycle';
        cycleTo = seen.get(current);
        break;
      }
      seen.set(current, nodes.length);
      const node = { value: current, reads: new Map() };
      nodes.push(node);
      if (className(current) !== CLASS.subTexture) {
        end = className(current) ? 'root_reached' : 'parent_class_unresolved';
        break;
      }
      const parent = readIconTextureProperty(current, 'get_parent');
      node.reads.set('get_parent', parent);
      if (parent.status !== 'ok') { end = 'parent_unresolved'; break; }
      if (!parent.value || typeof parent.value !== 'object') { end = 'parent_invalid'; break; }
      current = parent.value;
    }

    const scalar = value => {
      if (value == null) return { type: value === null ? 'null' : 'undefined', value: null };
      if (typeof value === 'number') return { type: 'number', value: Number.isFinite(value) ? value : String(value) };
      if (typeof value === 'boolean') return { type: 'boolean', value };
      return { type: typeof value, value: null };
    };
    const fields = (value, keys) => {
      if (value == null) return null;
      const values = {}, invalidFields = [];
      for (const key of keys) {
        let field;
        try { field = value[key]; } catch { invalidFields.push(key); values[key] = null; continue; }
        values[key] = scalar(field).value;
        if (typeof field !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(field)) invalidFields.push(key);
      }
      return { values, invalidFields };
    };
    const rectangles = ['region', 'frame'];
    const matrices = ['transformationMatrix', 'transformationMatrixToRoot'];
    const numbers = ['width', 'height', 'scale', 'nativeWidth', 'nativeHeight'];
    const chain = nodes.map((node, depth) => {
      const report = { depth, className: className(node.value), getters: {} };
      const names = [...rectangles, 'rotated', ...numbers, ...matrices];
      for (const name of names) {
        const semantic = `get_${name}`;
        // Derived getters may follow parents recursively. Do not call them on a
        // broken/cyclic chain; retain the safe leaf geometry and the stop reason.
        const read = end !== 'root_reached' && (numbers.includes(name) || matrices.includes(name))
          ? { status: 'skipped_incomplete_chain', method: null }
          : readIconTextureProperty(node.value, semantic);
        node.reads.set(semantic, read);
        report.getters[name] = {
          status: read.status, method: read.method,
          ...(read.status === 'ok' ? { valueType: read.value === null ? 'null' : typeof read.value } : {}),
          ...(read.errorCode ? { errorCode: read.errorCode } : {}),
        };
        if (read.status !== 'ok') { report[name] = null; continue; }
        if (rectangles.includes(name)) report[name] = fields(read.value, ['x', 'y', 'width', 'height']);
        else if (matrices.includes(name)) report[name] = fields(read.value, ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'tx', 'ty']);
        else report[name] = scalar(read.value);
      }
      const parent = node.reads.get('get_parent');
      report.parentClass = parent?.status === 'ok' ? className(parent.value) : null;
      if (parent) report.getters.parent = { status: parent.status, method: parent.method, ...(parent.errorCode ? { errorCode: parent.errorCode } : {}) };
      return report;
    });
    const leaf = nodes[0]?.reads ?? new Map();
    const rotated = leaf.get('get_rotated') ?? { status: 'missing', method: null };
    const parent = leaf.get('get_parent') ?? { status: 'missing', method: null };
    const rotatedTruthy = rotated.status === 'ok' ? Boolean(rotated.value) : null;
    const parentIsSubTexture = parent.status === 'ok' ? className(parent.value) === CLASS.subTexture : null;
    const reason = rotatedTruthy && parentIsSubTexture ? 'rotated_and_parent_subtexture'
      : rotatedTruthy ? 'rotated' : parentIsSubTexture ? 'parent_subtexture' : null;
    return {
      rotated, parent,
      region: leaf.get('get_region'), frame: leaf.get('get_frame'),
      report: {
        rotatedTruthy, rotated: scalar(rotated.value), parentIsSubTexture,
        parentClass: parent.status === 'ok' ? className(parent.value) : null,
        reason, chainEnd: end, cycleTo, chain,
      },
    };
  }

  function flattenIconTextureTransform(report, diagnostic) {
    const chain = report.chain;
    const detail = diagnostic.textureFlatten = {
      stage: 'validating', coordinateSpace: 'root_pixels', chainLength: chain.length,
    };
    const reject = (stage, depth, reason) => {
      diagnostic.stage = stage;
      Object.assign(detail, { stage, depth, reason });
      return null;
    };
    if (report.chainEnd !== 'root_reached' || chain.length < 2 || chain.length > 8) {
      return reject('texture_parent_chain_invalid', null, report.chainEnd);
    }
    const rootDepth = chain.length - 1;
    if (chain[rootDepth].className !== CLASS.concreteTexture) {
      return reject('texture_root_invalid', rootDepth, 'concrete_texture_required');
    }

    // Only floating-point roundoff is tolerated, never a visible rotation/skew
    // or an arbitrary crop adjustment. Matrix values below are numeric copies.
    const near = (a, b) => Number.isFinite(a) && Number.isFinite(b) &&
      Math.abs(a - b) <= 64 * Number.EPSILON * Math.max(1, Math.abs(a), Math.abs(b));
    const snap = value => near(value, Math.round(value)) ? Math.round(value) : value;
    const positive = value => Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0;
    const inside = (start, length, limit) => positive(length) && positive(limit) &&
      (start >= 0 || near(start, 0)) && (start + length <= limit || near(start + length, limit));
    const numericFields = (node, name, keys) => {
      const field = node[name];
      return node.getters[name]?.status === 'ok' && field && !field.invalidFields.length &&
        keys.every(key => Number.isFinite(field.values[key])) ? field.values : null;
    };
    const rectKeys = ['x', 'y', 'width', 'height'];
    const matrixKeys = ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'tx', 'ty'];
    const sizes = [];
    for (let depth = 0; depth < chain.length; depth++) {
      const node = chain[depth], size = {};
      for (const name of ['width', 'height', 'nativeWidth', 'nativeHeight', 'scale']) {
        const field = node[name];
        if (node.getters[name]?.status !== 'ok' || field?.type !== 'number' || !positive(field.value)) {
          return reject('texture_dimensions_invalid', depth, name);
        }
        size[name] = field.value;
      }
      if (!near(size.width * size.scale, size.nativeWidth) || !near(size.height * size.scale, size.nativeHeight)) {
        return reject('texture_dimensions_invalid', depth, 'scale_mismatch');
      }
      sizes.push(size);
    }
    const root = sizes[rootDepth];
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(root.nativeWidth) || !Number.isSafeInteger(root.nativeHeight)) {
      return reject('texture_root_invalid', rootDepth, 'native_pixel_dimensions');
    }
    detail.rootSize = { width: root.nativeWidth, height: root.nativeHeight, scale: root.scale };
    const matrices = [];
    for (let depth = 0; depth < rootDepth; depth++) {
      const node = chain[depth], size = sizes[depth], parent = sizes[depth + 1];
      if (node.className !== CLASS.subTexture || node.getters.parent?.status !== 'ok' ||
          node.parentClass !== chain[depth + 1].className) {
        return reject('texture_parent_chain_invalid', depth, 'parent_unverified');
      }
      if (node.getters.rotated?.status !== 'ok' || node.rotated?.type !== 'boolean') {
        return reject('texture_transform_unresolved', depth, 'rotation_unverified');
      }
      if (node.rotated.value) return reject('texture_transform_unsupported', depth, 'rotation');
      const region = numericFields(node, 'region', rectKeys);
      if (!region || !inside(region.x, region.width, parent.width) || !inside(region.y, region.height, parent.height)) {
        return reject('texture_parent_region_invalid', depth, 'region_outside_parent');
      }
      if (node.getters.frame?.status !== 'ok' || node.getters.frame.valueType === 'undefined') {
        return reject('texture_geometry_unresolved', depth, 'frame_unverified');
      }
      if (node.frame !== null) {
        const frame = numericFields(node, 'frame', rectKeys);
        if (!frame || !inside(-frame.x, size.width, frame.width) || !inside(-frame.y, size.height, frame.height)) {
          return reject('frame_invalid', depth, 'frame_does_not_contain_texture');
        }
      }
      const matrix = numericFields(node, 'transformationMatrix', matrixKeys);
      if (!matrix) return reject('texture_matrix_invalid', depth, 'matrix_unverified');
      if (matrix.b !== 0 || matrix.c !== 0) return reject('texture_transform_unsupported', depth, 'rotation_or_skew');
      if (!positive(matrix.a) || !positive(matrix.d)) return reject('texture_matrix_invalid', depth, 'nonpositive_scale');
      if (!near(matrix.a * parent.width, region.width) || !near(matrix.d * parent.height, region.height) ||
          !near(matrix.tx * parent.width, region.x) || !near(matrix.ty * parent.height, region.y) ||
          !near(region.width * parent.scale, size.nativeWidth) || !near(region.height * parent.scale, size.nativeHeight)) {
        return reject('texture_matrix_geometry_mismatch', depth, 'parent_coordinates');
      }
      matrices.push(matrix);
    }

    const multiply = (parent, child) => ({
      a: parent.a * child.a + parent.c * child.b,
      b: parent.b * child.a + parent.d * child.b,
      c: parent.a * child.c + parent.c * child.d,
      d: parent.b * child.c + parent.d * child.d,
      tx: parent.a * child.tx + parent.c * child.ty + parent.tx,
      ty: parent.b * child.tx + parent.d * child.ty + parent.ty,
    });
    let composed = { a: 1, b: 0, c: 0, d: 1, tx: 0, ty: 0 };
    // M(root-parent) * ... * M(leaf) maps the leaf's unit UV square to root UVs.
    for (let depth = rootDepth - 1; depth >= 0; depth--) {
      composed = multiply(composed, matrices[depth]);
      if (!matrixKeys.every(key => Number.isFinite(composed[key])) || !positive(composed.a) || !positive(composed.d)) {
        return reject('texture_matrix_invalid', depth, 'composition_invalid');
      }
      const node = chain[depth];
      if (node.getters.transformationMatrixToRoot?.status !== 'missing') {
        const nativeRoot = numericFields(node, 'transformationMatrixToRoot', matrixKeys);
        if (!nativeRoot || !positive(nativeRoot.a) || !positive(nativeRoot.d)) {
          return reject('texture_matrix_invalid', depth, 'native_root_matrix_unverified');
        }
        if (nativeRoot.b !== 0 || nativeRoot.c !== 0 || !matrixKeys.every(key => near(composed[key], nativeRoot[key]))) {
          return reject('texture_matrix_geometry_mismatch', depth, 'native_root_matrix_mismatch');
        }
      }
    }
    detail.matrix = composed;
    const left = snap(composed.tx * root.nativeWidth), top = snap(composed.ty * root.nativeHeight);
    const right = snap((composed.tx + composed.a) * root.nativeWidth);
    const bottom = snap((composed.ty + composed.d) * root.nativeHeight);
    const region = { x: left, y: top, width: snap(right - left), height: snap(bottom - top) };
    if (!rectKeys.every(key => Number.isFinite(region[key])) ||
        !inside(region.x, region.width, root.nativeWidth) || !inside(region.y, region.height, root.nativeHeight)) {
      return reject('texture_root_region_out_of_bounds', 0, 'root_crop');
    }
    if (!near(region.width, sizes[0].nativeWidth) || !near(region.height, sizes[0].nativeHeight)) {
      return reject('texture_matrix_geometry_mismatch', 0, 'root_crop_size');
    }
    const leafFrame = chain[0].frame?.values;
    const frame = leafFrame ? Object.fromEntries(rectKeys.map(key => [key, snap(leafFrame[key] * sizes[0].scale)])) : null;
    if (frame && (!rectKeys.every(key => Number.isFinite(frame[key])) || !positive(frame.width) || !positive(frame.height))) {
      return reject('frame_invalid', 0, 'root_pixel_frame');
    }
    Object.assign(detail, { stage: 'ready', region, frame });
    return { region, frame };
  }

  function getValidatedIconTexture(rendered, diagnostic) {
    delete diagnostic.textureFlatten;
    const queue = [{ value: rendered, depth: 0 }];
    const seen = new Set();
    const textures = new Set();
    while (queue.length && seen.size < 100) {
      const { value, depth } = queue.shift();
      if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || seen.has(value)) continue;
      seen.add(value);
      if (className(value) === CLASS.subTexture) {
        textures.add(value);
        continue;
      }
      if (value instanceof Node || ArrayBuffer.isView(value) || value instanceof ArrayBuffer) continue;
      // A semantic texture getter takes precedence over unrelated cached fields.
      const texture = callSemantic(value, 'get_texture', { optional: true });
      if (texture) {
        queue.push({ value: texture, depth: depth + 1 });
        continue;
      }
      if (depth >= 4) continue;
      for (const descriptor of Object.values(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(value))) {
        if (descriptor.value && typeof descriptor.value === 'object') queue.push({ value: descriptor.value, depth: depth + 1 });
      }
    }
    if (queue.length || textures.size !== 1) {
      diagnostic.stage = queue.length ? 'texture_scan_limit' : textures.size ? 'subtexture_ambiguous' : 'subtexture_not_found';
      return null;
    }
    const texture = [...textures][0];
    diagnostic.textureClass = className(texture);
    const transform = inspectIconTextureTransform(texture);
    diagnostic.textureTransform = transform.report;
    diagnostic.transformReason = transform.report.reason;
    const valid = value => value && Object.values(value).every(Number.isFinite) && value.width > 0 && value.height > 0;
    const region = transform.report.chain[0]?.region?.values ?? null;
    const frame = transform.report.chain[0]?.frame?.values ?? null;
    diagnostic.region = region;
    diagnostic.frame = frame;
    if (transform.region?.status !== 'ok' || transform.frame?.status !== 'ok') {
      diagnostic.stage = 'texture_geometry_unresolved';
      return null;
    }
    if (!valid(region) || region.x < 0 || region.y < 0) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'region_invalid';
      return null;
    }
    if (frame && !valid(frame)) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'frame_invalid';
      return null;
    }
    if (transform.rotated.status !== 'ok' || typeof transform.rotated.value !== 'boolean' ||
        transform.parent.status !== 'ok' || !transform.parent.value || typeof transform.parent.value !== 'object') {
      diagnostic.stage = 'texture_transform_unresolved';
      return null;
    }
    if (transform.report.chainEnd !== 'root_reached') {
      diagnostic.stage = 'texture_parent_chain_invalid';
      return null;
    }
    const flattened = flattenIconTextureTransform(transform.report, diagnostic);
    if (!flattened) return null;
    diagnostic.region = flattened.region;
    diagnostic.frame = flattened.frame;
    return { texture, ...flattened };
  }

  function extractUnitIconSpriteSpec(asset, { nativeAtlasFallback = false, diagnostic = {} } = {}) {
    diagnostic.stage = 'render_method';
    diagnostic.route = 'legacy';
    let renderMethod = null;
    try {
      renderMethod = findMethodBySource(
        asset,
        (source, fn) => fn.length <= 1 && (
          (source.includes('switch(a)') && source.includes('this.rc()')) ||
          (source.includes('switch(a)') && source.includes('this.sc()')) ||
          source.includes('.data.jk(') ||
          source.includes('.data.kk(') ||
          source.includes('.data.qk(')
        ),
        'ICON_RENDER_METHOD_NOT_FOUND'
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (!nativeAtlasFallback || className(asset) !== CLASS.atlasTextureIconAsset) throw error;
      diagnostic.route = 'native_consumer';
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_contract';
      renderMethod = getNativeIconRenderMethod(asset, diagnostic);
      if (!renderMethod) return null;
      const lifecycle = getIconAssetLifecycle(asset);
      diagnostic.stage = 'asset_ready';
      if (!lifecycle || !asset[lifecycle.ready]()) return null;
    }
    diagnostic.stage = 'render_call';
    // Preserve HWCT's existing shape argument; only method discovery changes.
    const rendered = asset[renderMethod](0);
    try {
      diagnostic.stage = 'texture';
      const validated = diagnostic.route === 'native_consumer' ? getValidatedIconTexture(rendered, diagnostic) : null;
      if (diagnostic.route === 'native_consumer' && !validated) return null;
      const texture = validated?.texture ?? findSubTexture(rendered, { maxDepth: 4 });
      if (!texture) return null;
      const region = validated ? validated.region : callSemantic(texture, 'get_region', { optional: true });
      const frame = validated ? validated.frame : callSemantic(texture, 'get_frame', { optional: true });
      if (!region) return null;

      diagnostic.stage = 'atlas_url';
      let imageFile = null;
      let url = '';
      if (className(asset) === 'game.assets.icon.AtlasTextureIconAsset') {
        let atlas = callSemantic(asset, 'get_file', { optional: true }) ?? null;
        if (!atlas) {
          try {
            const atlasMethod = findMethodBySource(
              asset,
              (source, fn) => fn.length === 0 && /return [A-Za-z0-9_$.]+\.(?:zpb|wpb|bpb)\(this\./.test(source),
              'ICON_ATLAS_METHOD_NOT_FOUND'
            );
            atlas = asset[atlasMethod]();
          } catch {}
        }
        imageFile = atlas?.image ?? findObjectByClassLimited(
          atlas,
          'engine.core.assets.file.ImageFile',
          { maxDepth: 3, maxNodes: 50 }
        );
        url = getImageFileUrl(imageFile);

        // The current client no longer exposes the Hero atlas through the old
        // get_file()/ImageFile route. The same AtlasTextureIconAsset still exposes
        // its IconAtlasAsset bundle, so recover the production PNG URL from that
        // read-only bundle exactly as Pattern icons already do.
        if (!url) {
          diagnostic.urlRoute = 'generic_atlas';
          url = getGenericAtlasInfo(asset)?.url ?? '';
        } else {
          diagnostic.urlRoute = 'atlas_imagefile';
        }
      } else if (className(asset) === 'game.assets.RsxIconAsset') {
        const file = callSemantic(asset, 'get_file', { optional: true });
        imageFile = getRsxImageDependency(file);
        url = getImageFileUrl(imageFile);
      } else {
        imageFile = findObjectByClassLimited(
          asset,
          'engine.core.assets.file.ImageFile',
          { maxDepth: 4, maxNodes: 80 }
        );
        url = getImageFileUrl(imageFile);
      }

      if (!url) {
        diagnostic.stage = 'atlas_url_unresolved';
        return null;
      }
      diagnostic.stage = 'sprite_spec_ready';
      return {
        url,
        region: {
          x: Number(region.x), y: Number(region.y),
          width: Number(region.width), height: Number(region.height),
        },
        frame: frame ? {
          x: Number(frame.x ?? 0), y: Number(frame.y ?? 0),
          width: Number(frame.width), height: Number(frame.height),
        } : null,
      };
    } finally {
      try {
        if (diagnostic.route !== 'native_consumer' ||
            (rendered !== asset && className(rendered) !== CLASS.subTexture &&
              !callSemantic(rendered, 'get_parent', { optional: true }))) rendered?.dispose?.();
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  // The resolution metadata for one unit ID, kept so a later cache hit can still be
  // attributed. It is bounded exactly like the diagnostic store it feeds.
  function rememberUnitIconResolution(numericId, diagnostic) {
    unitIconResolutionById.set(numericId, { ...diagnostic });
    if (unitIconResolutionById.size > ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_LIMITS.rows) {
      unitIconResolutionById.delete(unitIconResolutionById.keys().next().value);
    }
  }

  // A cache hit resolves nothing, so it must not pretend to. It replays the stored
  // resolver metadata under the family that asked. Without this, the Hero Reference
  // preload - which warms Hero, Main Pet and Patron Pet IDs together - would leave
  // the later Main Pet and Patron renders with no row of their own, and their image
  // failures would be invisible.
  function projectUnitIconDiagnostic(family, numericId) {
    const stored = unitIconResolutionById.get(Number(numericId));
    if (!stored) return null;
    return recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, numericId, stored);
  }

  // Only inspect own data fields at the two proven storage levels. Do not invoke
  // arbitrary storage methods/accessors or require StringMap to be in $haxe.
  function getNativeUnitDescription(id, Description) {
    const DataStorage = findClass(CLASS.dataStorage);
    const Storage = findClass(CLASS.heroDescriptionStorage);
    const Pet = findClass(CLASS.petDescription);
    const Titan = findClass(CLASS.titanDescription);
    const storages = uniqueRefs(Object.values(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(DataStorage))
      .map(field => field.value).filter(value => value instanceof Storage));
    const maps = [];
    for (const storage of storages) {
      for (const field of Object.values(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(storage))) {
        const candidate = field.value;
        try {
          if (candidate && typeof candidate.get === 'function' &&
              candidate.get('6001') instanceof Pet && candidate.get('4000') instanceof Titan) {
            maps.push(candidate);
          }
        } catch { /* A rejected candidate cannot prevent another validated map. */ }
      }
    }
    const validated = uniqueRefs(maps);
    if (validated.length !== 1) fail(validated.length ? 'UNIT_DESCRIPTION_MAP_AMBIGUOUS' : 'UNIT_DESCRIPTION_MAP_NOT_FOUND');
    const description = validated[0].get(String(id));
    if (!description) fail('UNIT_DESCRIPTION_MISSING');
    if (!(description instanceof Description)) fail('UNIT_DESCRIPTION_CLASS_MISMATCH');
    return description;
  }

  function getNativeUnitIconMethod(entry) {
    const keys = new Set();
    // Metadata can belong to UnitEntryValueObject while the implementation is
    // overridden by Pet/Titan. Collect semantic keys, never prototype functions.
    for (let proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(entry); proto; proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(proto)) {
      const properties = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto, '__properties__')?.value;
      if (!properties) continue;
      for (const key in properties) if (properties[key] === 'get_icon') keys.add(key);
    }
    if (keys.size !== 1) fail(keys.size ? 'UNIT_ICON_GETTER_AMBIGUOUS' : 'UNIT_ICON_GETTER_NOT_FOUND');
    const [method] = keys;
    if (typeof entry[method] !== 'function') fail('UNIT_ICON_GETTER_MISSING');
    return method;
  }

  // Hero and Titan portraits can be a SubTexture inside an atlas-page SubTexture.
  // Geometry validation is shared, while pixel-source ownership remains family-local:
  // Titan keeps the existing borrowed-texture crop and Hero resolves the loaded atlas
  // through the game's dependency interfaces below.
  function getNestedUnitIconGeometry(texture, diagnostic) {
    const transform = inspectIconTextureTransform(texture);
    diagnostic.textureTransform = transform.report;
    diagnostic.transformReason = transform.report.reason;
    const flattened = flattenIconTextureTransform(transform.report, diagnostic);
    if (!flattened) return null;
    diagnostic.region = flattened.region;
    diagnostic.frame = flattened.frame;
    return { ...flattened, transformReport: transform.report };
  }

  // Once flattenIconTextureTransform() has accepted the full chain, derive the
  // same leaf rectangle in each validated ancestor's native pixel space. The source
  // PNG may correspond to an atlas-page SubTexture rather than the power-of-two
  // ConcreteTexture backing allocation. Browser natural dimensions select the one
  // exact coordinate space later; ambiguous matches fail closed.
  function getAtlasCoordinateCandidates(report, frame = null) {
    const chain = report?.chain ?? [];
    if (report?.chainEnd !== 'root_reached' || chain.length < 2 || chain.length > 8) return [];

    const near = (a, b) => Number.isFinite(a) && Number.isFinite(b) &&
      Math.abs(a - b) <= 64 * Number.EPSILON * Math.max(1, Math.abs(a), Math.abs(b));
    const snap = value => near(value, Math.round(value)) ? Math.round(value) : value;
    const positive = value => Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0;
    const matrix = depth => {
      const field = chain[depth]?.transformationMatrix;
      const values = field?.values;
      if (chain[depth]?.getters?.transformationMatrix?.status !== 'ok' || !values) return null;
      const out = {};
      for (const key of ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'tx', 'ty']) {
        if (!Number.isFinite(values[key])) return null;
        out[key] = values[key];
      }
      return out;
    };
    const multiply = (parent, child) => ({
      a: parent.a * child.a + parent.c * child.b,
      b: parent.b * child.a + parent.d * child.b,
      c: parent.a * child.c + parent.c * child.d,
      d: parent.b * child.c + parent.d * child.d,
      tx: parent.a * child.tx + parent.c * child.ty + parent.tx,
      ty: parent.b * child.tx + parent.d * child.ty + parent.ty,
    });
    const candidates = [];
    for (let ancestorDepth = 1; ancestorDepth < chain.length; ancestorDepth += 1) {
      const ancestor = chain[ancestorDepth];
      const width = Number(ancestor?.nativeWidth?.value);
      const height = Number(ancestor?.nativeHeight?.value);
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(width) || !Number.isSafeInteger(height) || width <= 0 || height <= 0) continue;

      let composed = { a: 1, b: 0, c: 0, d: 1, tx: 0, ty: 0 };
      let valid = true;
      for (let depth = ancestorDepth - 1; depth >= 0; depth -= 1) {
        const local = matrix(depth);
        if (!local) { valid = false; break; }
        composed = multiply(composed, local);
      }
      if (!valid || composed.b !== 0 || composed.c !== 0 ||
          !positive(composed.a) || !positive(composed.d)) continue;

      const left = snap(composed.tx * width);
      const top = snap(composed.ty * height);
      const right = snap((composed.tx + composed.a) * width);
      const bottom = snap((composed.ty + composed.d) * height);
      const region = { x: left, y: top, width: snap(right - left), height: snap(bottom - top) };
      if (![region.x, region.y, region.width, region.height].every(Number.isFinite) ||
          region.x < 0 || region.y < 0 || !positive(region.width) || !positive(region.height) ||
          region.x + region.width > width || region.y + region.height > height) continue;

      candidates.push({
        depth: ancestorDepth,
        className: ancestor.className,
        width,
        height,
        region,
        frame,
      });
    }

    const unique = new Map();
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      const key = JSON.stringify({
        width: candidate.width, height: candidate.height,
        region: candidate.region, frame: candidate.frame,
      });
      if (!unique.has(key)) unique.set(key, candidate);
    }
    return [...unique.values()];
  }

  function cropNestedTitanIconTexture(texture, diagnostic) {
    const flattened = getNestedUnitIconGeometry(texture, diagnostic);
    if (!flattened) return null;
    const url = cropSubTextureToDataUrl(texture, flattened);
    if (!url) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'texture_crop_failed';
      return null;
    }
    return { url, ...flattened };
  }

  // Resolve an implemented Haxe interface from the concrete object's prototype
  // chain, rather than assuming interface objects are exported as values in
  // window.$haxe. Only static metadata is read; no candidate method is invoked.
  function getImplementedInterfaceByName(object, fullName) {
    if (!object || typeof object !== 'object') fail('ASSET_DEPENDENCY_OBJECT_INVALID');
    const matches = [];
    let proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(object);
    for (let depth = 0; proto && depth < 12; depth += 1, proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(proto)) {
      const ClassObject = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto, '__class__')?.value;
      if (typeof ClassObject !== 'function') continue;
      const interfaces = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(ClassObject, '__interfaces__')?.value;
      if (!Array.isArray(interfaces)) continue;
      for (const Interface of interfaces) {
        if (typeof Interface === 'function' && Interface.j === fullName) matches.push(Interface);
      }
    }
    const unique = uniqueRefs(matches);
    if (unique.length === 0) fail('ASSET_DEPENDENCY_INTERFACE_NOT_FOUND', fullName);
    if (unique.length !== 1) fail('ASSET_DEPENDENCY_INTERFACE_AMBIGUOUS', fullName);
    return unique[0];
  }

  // IAssetWithDependencies has one dispatch slot in the current Haxe contract.
  // Resolve that slot from the exact implemented-interface metadata instead of
  // naming the minified method, parsing source, or requiring interface export in
  // window.$haxe. If the interface shape broadens, fail closed instead of guessing.
  function getAssetDependencyMethod(asset) {
    const Interface = getImplementedInterfaceByName(asset, CLASS.assetWithDependencies);
    const slots = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(Interface.prototype ?? {}).filter(name =>
      name !== 'constructor' &&
      !name.startsWith('__') &&
      typeof asset[name] === 'function'
    );
    if (slots.length === 0) fail('ASSET_DEPENDENCY_METHOD_NOT_FOUND');
    if (slots.length !== 1) fail('ASSET_DEPENDENCY_METHOD_AMBIGUOUS', String(slots.length));
    const method = slots[0];
    if (asset[method].length !== 0) fail('ASSET_DEPENDENCY_METHOD_SIGNATURE');
    return method;
  }

  function getAssetDependenciesByInterface(asset) {
    const method = getAssetDependencyMethod(asset);
    const dependencies = asset[method]();
    if (!Array.isArray(dependencies)) fail('ASSET_DEPENDENCY_RESULT_INVALID');
    return dependencies.filter(Boolean);
  }

  function getExactHaxeClassByName(value, fullName) {
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return null;
    const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
    const ClassObject = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(proto ?? {}, '__class__')?.value;
    return typeof ClassObject === 'function' && ClassObject.j === fullName ? ClassObject : null;
  }

  // Return only own *data* fields whose value is an exact Haxe class by runtime
  // class-name metadata. Accessor descriptors are never read, and the class does
  // not need to be exported through window.$haxe.
  function getOwnExactClassValuesByName(object, fullName) {
    if (!object || typeof object !== 'object') return [];
    const matches = [];
    for (const descriptor of Object.values(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(object))) {
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(descriptor, 'value')) continue;
      const value = descriptor.value;
      if (getExactHaxeClassByName(value, fullName)) matches.push(value);
    }
    return uniqueRefs(matches);
  }

  // Bind semantic get_icon geometry to the exact decoded TextureAtlas owned by the
  // exact IconAtlasAsset dependency. No candidate method/getter is invoked:
  //
  //   AtlasTextureIconAsset.get_textureIdent()
  //     -> exact IconAtlasAsset dependency
  //     -> one exact own TextureAtlas data field
  //     -> one exact own StringMap data field
  //     -> known read-only StringMap.get(textureIdent)
  //     -> exact SubTexture object identity with semantic get_icon
  //
  // Any missing/ambiguous class field or identity mismatch fails closed.
  function validateNativeHeroTextureBinding(bundle, asset, texture, diagnostic) {
    if (!getExactHaxeClassByName(asset, CLASS.atlasTextureIconAsset) ||
        !getExactHaxeClassByName(bundle, CLASS.iconAtlasAsset) ||
        !getExactHaxeClassByName(texture, CLASS.subTexture)) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_texture_mismatch';
      return false;
    }

    const textureIdent = callSemantic(asset, 'get_textureIdent', { optional: true });
    if (typeof textureIdent !== 'string' || !textureIdent) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_texture_ident_invalid';
      return false;
    }

    const atlases = getOwnExactClassValuesByName(bundle, CLASS.textureAtlas);
    diagnostic.atlasTextureAtlasCandidates = atlases.length;
    if (atlases.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.stage = atlases.length
        ? 'native_atlas_texture_atlas_ambiguous'
        : 'native_atlas_texture_atlas_missing';
      return false;
    }

    const maps = getOwnExactClassValuesByName(atlases[0], CLASS.stringMap);
    diagnostic.atlasTextureMapCandidates = maps.length;
    if (maps.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.stage = maps.length
        ? 'native_atlas_texture_map_ambiguous'
        : 'native_atlas_texture_map_missing';
      return false;
    }

    const StringMap = getExactHaxeClassByName(maps[0], CLASS.stringMap);
    const get = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(StringMap?.prototype ?? {}, 'get')?.value;
    if (typeof get !== 'function' || get.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_texture_map_contract_invalid';
      return false;
    }

    let resolved = null;
    try {
      resolved = get.call(maps[0], textureIdent);
    } catch {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_texture_map_lookup_failed';
      return false;
    }
    diagnostic.atlasTextureBindingCandidates =
      className(resolved) === CLASS.subTexture ? 1 : 0;
    if (className(resolved) !== CLASS.subTexture || resolved !== texture) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_texture_mismatch';
      return false;
    }
    diagnostic.atlasTextureBinding = 'exact_identity';
    return true;
  }

  function getNativeHeroAtlasInfo(entry, texture, diagnostic) {
    diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_asset';
    const asset = callSemantic(entry, 'get_iconAsset', { optional: true });
    diagnostic.atlasAssetClass = className(asset);
    if (!asset || className(asset) !== CLASS.atlasTextureIconAsset) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_asset_invalid';
      return null;
    }

    let assetDependencies = null;
    try {
      assetDependencies = getAssetDependenciesByInterface(asset);
    } catch (error) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_dependency_invalid';
      diagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
      return null;
    }
    const bundles = uniqueRefs(assetDependencies.filter(value => className(value) === CLASS.iconAtlasAsset));
    if (bundles.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.stage = bundles.length ? 'native_atlas_bundle_ambiguous' : 'native_atlas_bundle_missing';
      return null;
    }
    if (!validateNativeHeroTextureBinding(bundles[0], asset, texture, diagnostic)) return null;

    let bundleDependencies = null;
    try {
      bundleDependencies = getAssetDependenciesByInterface(bundles[0]);
    } catch (error) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_file_dependency_invalid';
      diagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
      return null;
    }
    const images = uniqueRefs(bundleDependencies.filter(value => className(value) === CLASS.imageFile));
    if (images.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.stage = images.length ? 'native_atlas_image_ambiguous' : 'native_atlas_image_missing';
      return null;
    }

    const url = callSemantic(images[0], 'get_url', { optional: true });
    if (typeof url !== 'string' || !/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_url_invalid';
      return null;
    }
    diagnostic.atlasUrlSource = 'native_dependency_interface';
    return { url, asset, bundle: bundles[0], imageFile: images[0] };
  }

  function getNativeUnitIconSpriteSpec(id, family, diagnostic) {
    diagnostic.route = 'native_unit_entry';
    diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.descriptionLookup;
    const isHero = family === ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait;
    const isTitan = family === ARTWORK_FAMILY.titanPortrait;
    const Description = findClass(
      isHero ? CLASS.heroDescription : isTitan ? CLASS.titanDescription : CLASS.petDescription
    );
    const description = getNativeUnitDescription(id, Description);
    diagnostic.descriptionClass = className(description);
    diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.entryConstruction;
    const Entry = findClass(
      isHero ? CLASS.heroEntryVO : isTitan ? CLASS.titanEntryVO : CLASS.petEntryVO
    );
    const entry = new Entry(description, null);
    diagnostic.entryClass = className(entry);
    diagnostic.stage = 'native_contract';
    const method = getNativeUnitIconMethod(entry);
    diagnostic.entryMethod = method;
    diagnostic.stage = 'native_icon';
    const texture = entry[method](); // Virtual dispatch through the concrete object.
    diagnostic.textureClass = className(texture);
    if (!texture) {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.textureMissing;
      return null;
    }
    if (className(texture) !== CLASS.subTexture) {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.textureInvalid;
      return null;
    }
    // Borrowed textures stay game-owned. Hero uses the already-loaded atlas URL plus
    // validated root-pixel geometry, so it never needs a browser CanvasImageSource
    // from Starling's GPU texture. Pet/Titan retain their existing crop behavior.
    diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.textureInvalid;
    const region = callSemantic(texture, 'get_region', { optional: true });
    const frame = callSemantic(texture, 'get_frame', { optional: true });
    if (!region || Number(region.x) < 0 || Number(region.y) < 0) return null;

    const parent = callSemantic(texture, 'get_parent', { optional: true });
    if (isHero) {
      // Run the same strict transform validator for direct and nested Hero textures.
      // This yields both the root-space geometry and a fully validated chain from
      // which possible source-PNG coordinate spaces can be derived.
      const geometry = getNestedUnitIconGeometry(texture, diagnostic);
      if (!geometry) return null;
      const coordinateCandidates = getAtlasCoordinateCandidates(
        geometry.transformReport,
        geometry.frame
      );
      if (!coordinateCandidates.length) {
        diagnostic.stage = 'native_atlas_coordinate_unresolved';
        return null;
      }

      const atlas = getNativeHeroAtlasInfo(entry, texture, diagnostic);
      if (!atlas) return null;
      const rootSize = diagnostic.textureFlatten?.rootSize ?? null;
      diagnostic.atlasRootSize = rootSize;
      diagnostic.atlasCoordinateCandidates = coordinateCandidates.map(candidate => ({
        depth: candidate.depth,
        className: candidate.className,
        width: candidate.width,
        height: candidate.height,
      }));
      diagnostic.region = geometry.region;
      diagnostic.frame = geometry.frame;
      diagnostic.stage = 'sprite_spec_ready';
      return {
        url: atlas.url,
        region: geometry.region,
        frame: geometry.frame,
        nativeAtlasCandidates: coordinateCandidates,
      };
    }

    let crop = null;
    if (className(parent) === CLASS.concreteTexture) {
      const url = cropValidatedNativeTexture(texture);
      if (url) crop = { url, region, frame };
    } else if (isTitan && className(parent) === CLASS.subTexture) {
      crop = cropNestedTitanIconTexture(texture, diagnostic);
    }
    if (!crop) return null;
    diagnostic.stage = 'sprite_spec_ready';
    return {
      url: crop.url,
      region: { x: 0, y: 0, width: Math.max(1, Number(crop.frame?.width ?? crop.region.width)),
        height: Math.max(1, Number(crop.frame?.height ?? crop.region.height)) },
      frame: null, // Native crop already applied the frame offset.
    };
  }

  // Hero, Main Pet, Patron Pet and Titan portrait now use the semantic/native
  // EntryVO route exclusively after Issue #47 Phase 3 Batches 1-4. Shared unit
  // cache reuse is therefore valid only for successful native provenance.
  function canReuseUnitIconSpecCache(family, numericId) {
    if (!unitIconSpecCache.has(numericId)) return false;
    const stored = unitIconResolutionById.get(Number(numericId));
    return stored?.route === 'native_unit_entry' && stored?.fallbackUsed === false;
  }

  async function getUnitIconSpriteSpec(id, family = ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait) {
    const numericId = Number(id);
    if (!Number.isFinite(numericId)) return null;
    if (canReuseUnitIconSpecCache(family, numericId)) {
      projectUnitIconDiagnostic(family, numericId);
      return unitIconSpecCache.get(numericId);
    }
    const pending = unitIconSpecPromiseCache.get(numericId);
    if (pending) {
      const shared = await pending.promise;
      // Same-family callers can return the exact in-flight result directly. The
      // resolving attempt already recorded that family's diagnostic; in particular,
      // a null result must not project unrelated successful cache provenance.
      if (pending.family === family) return shared;

      // Cross-family reuse must return the same cache entry whose *current*
      // provenance passed the native-only policy. Do not approve an older pending
      // result by inspecting metadata that may have been replaced by a later resolution.
      if (canReuseUnitIconSpecCache(family, numericId)) {
        projectUnitIconDiagnostic(family, numericId);
        return unitIconSpecCache.get(numericId);
      }
    }

    const promise = (async () => {
      // Started before the first client call, so a throw anywhere below still leaves
      // a row naming the exact native step it reached.
      const diagnostic = { stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.descriptionLookup };
      const recordDiagnostic = ({ cacheResolution = false } = {}) => {
        // unitIconResolutionById describes provenance of a reusable successful spec,
        // not merely the most recent family-specific attempt.
        if (cacheResolution) rememberUnitIconResolution(numericId, diagnostic);
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, numericId, diagnostic);
      };
      try {
        const primary = {};
        let nativeSpec = null;
        try {
          nativeSpec = getNativeUnitIconSpriteSpec(numericId, family, primary);
        } catch (error) {
          primary.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
        }

        Object.assign(diagnostic, primary);
        diagnostic.route = 'native_unit_entry';
        diagnostic.primaryRoute = 'native_unit_entry';
        diagnostic.primaryStage = primary.stage;
        diagnostic.fallbackUsed = false;
        if (primary.errorCode) {
          diagnostic.errorCode = primary.errorCode;
          diagnostic.primaryErrorCode = primary.errorCode;
        }

        if (!nativeSpec) {
          recordDiagnostic();
          return null;
        }

        unitIconSpecCache.set(numericId, nativeSpec);
        recordDiagnostic({ cacheResolution: true });
        return nativeSpec;
      } catch (error) {
        // The stage is deliberately not overwritten: it is already the exact step the
        // resolver reached, which is more useful than a generic error stage.
        diagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
        recordDiagnostic();
        warn(`UNIT_ICON_SPEC_FAILED_${numericId}`, error);
        return null;
      }
    })();

    const pendingEntry = { promise, family };
    unitIconSpecPromiseCache.set(numericId, pendingEntry);
    try {
      return await promise;
    } finally {
      // A cross-family caller may replace an incompatible pending entry after awaiting
      // it. Only delete the entry this call actually installed.
      if (unitIconSpecPromiseCache.get(numericId) === pendingEntry) {
        unitIconSpecPromiseCache.delete(numericId);
      }
    }
  }

  function getImageSize(url) {
    if (imageSizeCache.has(url)) return imageSizeCache.get(url);
    const promise = new Promise(resolve => {
      const image = new Image();
      image.onload = () => resolve({ width: image.naturalWidth, height: image.naturalHeight });
      image.onerror = () => resolve(null);
      image.src = url;
    });
    imageSizeCache.set(url, promise);
    return promise;
  }

  async function createUnitSprite(id, size, classNameValue = 'unit-sprite', title = '', family = ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait) {
    const spec = await getUnitIconSpriteSpec(id, family);
    if (!spec) return null;
    const imageSize = await getImageSize(spec.url);
    const diagnostic = getArtworkDiagnostic(family, id) ?? projectUnitIconDiagnostic(family, id) ?? {};
    // Every route reports whether its image actually loaded. Only the native-consumer
    // route discards caches and re-checks atlas bounds afterwards: that is the route
    // whose region an atlas change can invalidate, and widening the rejection to the
    // legacy / RSX routes would risk removing artwork that renders correctly today.
    const nativeHeroCandidates = Array.isArray(spec.nativeAtlasCandidates)
      ? spec.nativeAtlasCandidates
      : null;
    const isNativeHeroAtlas = nativeHeroCandidates?.length > 0;
    if (!imageSize?.width || !imageSize?.height) {
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, id, { ...diagnostic, stage: 'image_load_failed' });
      if (diagnostic?.route === 'native_consumer' || isNativeHeroAtlas) {
        imageSizeCache.delete(spec.url);
        unitIconSpecCache.delete(Number(id));
      }
      return null;
    }

    let renderRegion = spec.region;
    let renderFrame = spec.frame;
    let imageDiagnostic = {
      ...diagnostic,
      ...(isNativeHeroAtlas ? {
        atlasImageSize: { width: imageSize.width, height: imageSize.height },
      } : {}),
    };
    if (isNativeHeroAtlas) {
      const matches = nativeHeroCandidates.filter(candidate =>
        Number(candidate?.width) === imageSize.width &&
        Number(candidate?.height) === imageSize.height
      );
      const unique = new Map();
      for (const candidate of matches) {
        const key = JSON.stringify({ region: candidate.region, frame: candidate.frame });
        if (!unique.has(key)) unique.set(key, candidate);
      }
      const resolved = [...unique.values()];
      if (resolved.length !== 1) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, id, {
          ...imageDiagnostic,
          stage: resolved.length ? 'atlas_coordinate_space_ambiguous' : 'atlas_size_mismatch',
        });
        unitIconSpecCache.delete(Number(id));
        return null;
      }
      const selected = resolved[0];
      renderRegion = selected.region;
      renderFrame = selected.frame;
      if (!renderRegion || renderRegion.x < 0 || renderRegion.y < 0 ||
          renderRegion.x + renderRegion.width > imageSize.width ||
          renderRegion.y + renderRegion.height > imageSize.height) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, id, { ...imageDiagnostic, stage: 'atlas_region_out_of_bounds' });
        unitIconSpecCache.delete(Number(id));
        return null;
      }
      imageDiagnostic = {
        ...imageDiagnostic,
        atlasCoordinateSpace: `depth_${selected.depth}_${selected.className === CLASS.concreteTexture ? 'root' : 'subtexture'}`,
        region: renderRegion,
        frame: renderFrame,
      };
    } else if (diagnostic?.route === 'native_consumer' &&
        (spec.region.x + spec.region.width > imageSize.width || spec.region.y + spec.region.height > imageSize.height)) {
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, id, { ...diagnostic, stage: 'atlas_region_out_of_bounds' });
      unitIconSpecCache.delete(Number(id));
      return null;
    }
    recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, id, { ...imageDiagnostic, stage: 'image_ready' });

    const logicalWidth = Number(renderFrame?.width ?? renderRegion.width) || 1;
    const logicalHeight = Number(renderFrame?.height ?? renderRegion.height) || 1;
    const scale = Math.min(size / logicalWidth, size / logicalHeight);
    const frameX = Number(renderFrame?.x ?? 0);
    const frameY = Number(renderFrame?.y ?? 0);

    const element = document.createElement('span');
    element.className = classNameValue;
    element.title = title || String(id);
    element.style.width = `${size}px`;
    element.style.height = `${size}px`;
    element.style.backgroundImage = `url("${spec.url}")`;
    element.style.backgroundRepeat = 'no-repeat';
    element.style.backgroundSize = `${imageSize.width * scale}px ${imageSize.height * scale}px`;
    element.style.backgroundPosition = `${(-renderRegion.x - frameX) * scale}px ${(-renderRegion.y - frameY) * scale}px`;
    return element;
  }

  // Some non-unit compatibility routes still use the shared generic IconAsset
  // extraction helpers. Unit portraits no longer enter this path after Issue #47
  // Phase 3 Batch 4. `family` only decides where the record lands; the resolution
  // itself is untouched, which is why an uninstrumented caller still passes no family.
  async function getIconAssetSpriteSpec(asset, diagnosticId = 'asset', { family = null, subject = diagnosticId } = {}) {
    if (!asset) return null;
    const diagnostic = { assetClass: className(asset) };
    const record = () => {
      if (family) recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, subject, diagnostic);
    };
    try {
      const immediate = extractUnitIconSpriteSpec(asset, { diagnostic });
      if (immediate) {
        record();
        return immediate;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
    }

    const prepared = await ensureIconAssetReady(asset, diagnosticId);
    if (!prepared.ready) {
      diagnostic.stage = 'asset_not_ready';
      record();
      return null;
    }
    try {
      const spec = extractUnitIconSpriteSpec(asset, { diagnostic });
      record();
      return spec;
    } finally {
      prepared.release?.();
    }
  }

  async function getTitanReferenceIconSpec(iconAsset, diagnosticId, { family = null, subject = diagnosticId } = {}) {
    if (!iconAsset) return null;
    try { return await getIconAssetSpriteSpec(iconAsset, diagnosticId, { family, subject }); }
    catch (error) {
      if (family) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(family, subject, {
          stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.resolverError,
          errorCode: error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error',
        });
      }
      warn(`TITAN_REFERENCE_ICON_FAILED_${diagnosticId}`, error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  function staticFunctionRows(ClassObject) {
    const out = [];
    for (const name of Object.getOwnPropertyNames(ClassObject ?? {})) {
      let fn = null;
      try { fn = ClassObject?.[name]; } catch { continue; }
      if (typeof fn !== 'function') continue;
      out.push({ name, fn, source: String(fn) });
    }
    return out;
  }

  function escapeSourcePattern(value) {
    return String(value).replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
  }

  function compactClientExecutableSource(value) {
    return String(value)
      .replace(/"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|'(?:\\.|[^'\\])*'|`(?:\\.|[^`\\])*`|\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|\/\/[^\r\n]*/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, '');
  }

  function findTitanTotemBodyConsumerContexts(route, diagnostic) {
    const matches = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    let classCount = 0;
    let staticCount = 0;
    let values = [];
    try { values = Object.values(getHaxeRoot()); } catch { return matches; }
    for (const Type of values) {
      if (typeof Type !== 'function') continue;
      classCount += 1;
      if (classCount > TOTEM_BODY_CONTEXT_CLASS_LIMIT) {
        diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_CONTEXT_CLASS_LIMIT';
        return [];
      }
      let descriptors = [];
      try { descriptors = Object.values(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(Type)); } catch { continue; }
      for (const descriptor of descriptors) {
        staticCount += 1;
        if (staticCount > TOTEM_BODY_CONTEXT_STATIC_LIMIT) {
          diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_CONTEXT_STATIC_LIMIT';
          return [];
        }
        if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(descriptor, 'value')) continue;
        const owner = descriptor.value;
        if (!owner || (typeof owner !== 'object' && typeof owner !== 'function') || seen.has(owner)) continue;
        seen.add(owner);
        let usages = null;
        let loader = null;
        try {
          usages = owner.usages;
          loader = owner[route.loaderField];
        } catch { continue; }
        if (!usages || !loader) continue;
        if (typeof usages[route.usagesAdd] !== 'function' || typeof usages[route.usagesRemove] !== 'function') continue;
        if (typeof loader[route.register] !== 'function' || typeof loader[route.unregister] !== 'function') continue;
        matches.push({ owner, usages, loader });
      }
    }
    return matches;
  }

  function findTitanTotemBodyUsageCountContract(usages, usagesAdd, usagesRemove) {
    const methods = prototypeMethodsDeep(usages).map(row => ({
      ...row,
      compact: compactClientExecutableSource(row.source),
    }));
    const add = methods.find(row => row.name === usagesAdd);
    const remove = methods.find(row => row.name === usagesRemove);
    if (!add || !remove) return null;
    const counterCalls = (row, delta) => {
      const argument = row.compact.match(/^function\(([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)(?:,[^)]*)?\)/)?.[1];
      if (!argument) return new Set();
      return new Set([...row.compact.matchAll(new RegExp(
        `this\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(${escapeSourcePattern(argument)},${delta}\\)`, 'g',
      ))].map(match => match[1]));
    };
    const increments = counterCalls(add, '(?:\\+?1)');
    const counters = [...counterCalls(remove, '-1')].filter(name => increments.has(name));
    if (counters.length !== 1) return null;
    const counter = methods.find(row => row.name === counters[0] && row.fn.length === 2);
    if (!counter) return null;
    const fields = [...new Set([...counter.compact.matchAll(/this\.([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)/g)]
      .map(match => match[1]))];
    const backings = fields.filter(field => {
      let value = null;
      try { value = usages[field]; } catch { return false; }
      return className(value) === CLASS.objectMap && typeof value.get === 'function';
    });
    return backings.length === 1 ? { usageCounter: counter.name, usageBacking: backings[0] } : null;
  }

  function readTitanTotemBodyUsageCount(contract, asset) {
    const backing = contract.usages[contract.usageBacking];
    if (!backing || className(backing) !== CLASS.objectMap || typeof backing.get !== 'function') {
      fail('TOTEM_BODY_USAGE_COUNT_INVALID');
    }
    const stored = backing.get(asset);
    const value = stored == null ? 0 : stored;
    if (typeof value !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(value) || !Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) {
      fail('TOTEM_BODY_USAGE_COUNT_INVALID');
    }
    return value;
  }

  // Derive the complete Totem-body lifecycle from one exact native consumer. The
  // method names are retained only as values reached through cross-method source
  // relationships; no observed minified spelling is a selector.
  function readTitanTotemBodyConsumerContract(diagnostic) {
    diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyConsumerContract;
    const Provider = findClass(CLASS.iconContentProvider, { optional: true });
    if (!Provider || Provider.j !== CLASS.iconContentProvider) {
      diagnostic.contractOutcome = 'missing';
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_PROVIDER_MISSING';
      return null;
    }
    const methods = prototypeMethodsDeep(Provider).map(row => ({
      ...row,
      compact: compactClientExecutableSource(row.source),
    }));
    const byName = new Map(methods.map(row => [row.name, row]));
    const candidates = [];
    for (const row of methods) {
      if (row.fn.length !== 1) continue;
      const usageCalls = row.compact.matchAll(
        /([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*(?:\.[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)*)\.usages\.([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\((this\.([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*))\)/g,
      );
      for (const usage of usageCalls) {
        const ownerExpression = usage[1];
        const usagesAdd = usage[2];
        const assetExpression = usage[3];
        const assetField = usage[4];
        const owner = escapeSourcePattern(ownerExpression);
        const asset = escapeSourcePattern(assetExpression);
        const register = row.compact.match(new RegExp(
          `${owner}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(${asset},(?:[A-Za-z_$][\\w$.]*\\(this,this\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\)|this\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*))\\)`,
        ));
        if (!register) continue;
        const loaderField = register[1];
        const registerMethod = register[2];
        const callbackMethod = register[3] || register[4];
        const callback = byName.get(callbackMethod);
        if (!callback) continue;
        const readyNames = new Set([
          ...row.compact.matchAll(new RegExp(`${asset}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(\\)`, 'g')),
        ].map(match => match[1]));
        if (readyNames.size !== 1) continue;
        const finalCalls = [...callback.compact.matchAll(new RegExp(
          `this\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)=${asset}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\((this\\.[A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\)`,
          'g',
        ))];
        const finals = [...new Map(finalCalls.map(match => [`${match[1]}:${match[2]}:${match[3]}`, {
          displayField: match[1],
          renderer: match[2],
          shapeExpression: match[3],
        }])).values()];
        if (finals.length !== 1) continue;

        const removeNames = new Set();
        const unregisterNames = new Set();
        const assetCleanupRows = [];
        const callbackPattern = `(?:[A-Za-z_$][\\w$.]*\\(this,this\\.${escapeSourcePattern(callbackMethod)}\\)|this\\.${escapeSourcePattern(callbackMethod)})`;
        for (const cleanup of methods) {
          if (cleanup.name === row.name) continue;
          let hasRemove = false;
          let hasUnregister = false;
          for (const match of cleanup.compact.matchAll(new RegExp(
            `${owner}\\.usages\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(${asset}\\)`, 'g',
          ))) {
            if (match[1] !== usagesAdd) {
              removeNames.add(match[1]);
              hasRemove = true;
            }
          }
          for (const match of cleanup.compact.matchAll(new RegExp(
            `${owner}\\.${escapeSourcePattern(loaderField)}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(${callbackPattern}\\)`, 'g',
          ))) {
            if (match[1] !== registerMethod) {
              unregisterNames.add(match[1]);
              hasUnregister = true;
            }
          }
          if (hasRemove && hasUnregister) assetCleanupRows.push(cleanup);
        }
        if (removeNames.size !== 1 || unregisterNames.size !== 1 || assetCleanupRows.length !== 1) continue;

        const displayField = finals[0].displayField;
        const displayCleanupRows = [];
        for (const cleanup of methods) {
          const match = cleanup.compact.match(new RegExp(
