            `this\\.${escapeSourcePattern(displayField)}&&this\\.${escapeSourcePattern(displayField)}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\((?:!0|true)\\)`,
          ));
          if (match) displayCleanupRows.push({ row: cleanup, teardown: match[1] });
        }
        if (displayCleanupRows.length !== 1) continue;

        const assetCleanup = assetCleanupRows[0];
        const displayCleanup = displayCleanupRows[0];
        const usagesRemove = [...removeNames][0];
        const unregister = [...unregisterNames][0];
        const removePosition = assetCleanup.compact.indexOf(`.usages.${usagesRemove}(${assetExpression})`);
        const unregisterPosition = assetCleanup.compact.indexOf(`.${loaderField}.${unregister}(`);
        if (removePosition < 0 || unregisterPosition < 0 || removePosition > unregisterPosition) continue;
        const resetRows = methods.filter(candidate =>
          candidate.compact.includes(`this.${assetCleanup.name}()`) &&
          candidate.compact.includes(`this.${displayCleanup.row.name}()`));
        if (resetRows.length !== 1) continue;
        const reset = resetRows[0];
        if (reset.compact.indexOf(`this.${assetCleanup.name}()`) >
            reset.compact.indexOf(`this.${displayCleanup.row.name}()`)) continue;

        const route = {
          entryMethod: row.name,
          callbackMethod,
          assetField,
          displayField,
          shapeExpression: finals[0].shapeExpression,
          loaderField,
          usagesAdd,
          usagesRemove,
          register: registerMethod,
          unregister,
          ready: [...readyNames][0],
          renderer: finals[0].renderer,
          displayTeardown: displayCleanup.teardown,
          cleanupOrder: ['usage_remove', 'loader_unregister', 'display_dispose'],
        };
        const contexts = findTitanTotemBodyConsumerContexts(route, diagnostic);
        for (const context of contexts) {
          const usageCount = findTitanTotemBodyUsageCountContract(
            context.usages,
            route.usagesAdd,
            route.usagesRemove,
          );
          if (usageCount) candidates.push({ ...route, ...context, ...usageCount });
        }
      }
    }

    const unique = [];
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      const duplicate = unique.some(row => row.owner === candidate.owner &&
        row.entryMethod === candidate.entryMethod && row.callbackMethod === candidate.callbackMethod &&
        row.renderer === candidate.renderer && row.ready === candidate.ready &&
        row.usagesAdd === candidate.usagesAdd && row.usagesRemove === candidate.usagesRemove &&
        row.usageCounter === candidate.usageCounter && row.usageBacking === candidate.usageBacking &&
        row.register === candidate.register && row.unregister === candidate.unregister &&
        row.displayTeardown === candidate.displayTeardown);
      if (!duplicate) unique.push(candidate);
    }
    diagnostic.contractOutcome = unique.length === 1 ? 'unique' : unique.length ? 'ambiguous' : 'missing';
    if (unique.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = unique.length ? 'TOTEM_BODY_CONSUMER_AMBIGUOUS' :
        (diagnostic.errorCode ?? 'TOTEM_BODY_CONSUMER_MISSING');
      return null;
    }
    return Object.freeze(unique[0]);
  }

  function getTitanTotemBodyConsumerContract(diagnostic) {
    const cached = titanTotemBodyConsumerContractCache;
    if (cached && typeof cached.usages?.[cached.usagesAdd] === 'function' &&
        typeof cached.usages?.[cached.usagesRemove] === 'function' &&
        typeof cached.usages?.[cached.usageCounter] === 'function' &&
        className(cached.usages?.[cached.usageBacking]) === CLASS.objectMap &&
        typeof cached.usages?.[cached.usageBacking]?.get === 'function' &&
        typeof cached.loader?.[cached.register] === 'function' &&
        typeof cached.loader?.[cached.unregister] === 'function') {
      diagnostic.contractOutcome = 'unique';
      return cached;
    }
    titanTotemBodyConsumerContractCache = null;
    const contract = readTitanTotemBodyConsumerContract(diagnostic);
    if (contract) titanTotemBodyConsumerContractCache = contract;
    return contract;
  }

  function getTitanArtifactIconAsset(description, diagnostic = null) {
    if (diagnostic) diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyMapper;
    if (!description || className(description) !== CLASS.titanArtifactDescription) {
      if (diagnostic) diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_DESCRIPTION_CLASS';
      return null;
    }
    const AssetUtil = findClass(CLASS.assetStorageUtil, { optional: true });
    if (!AssetUtil) {
      if (diagnostic) diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_MAPPER_CLASS_MISSING';
      return null;
    }

    const invoke = name => {
      if (!name || typeof AssetUtil[name] !== 'function') return null;
      try {
        const asset = AssetUtil[name](description) ?? null;
        return className(asset) === CLASS.rsxIconAsset ? asset : null;
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    };

    if (titanArtifactIconMapperMethodCache) {
      const cached = invoke(titanArtifactIconMapperMethodCache);
      if (cached) {
        if (diagnostic) {
          diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyAsset;
          diagnostic.assetClass = className(cached);
        }
        return cached;
      }
      titanArtifactIconMapperMethodCache = null;
    }

    // Harness v0.2.19 confirmed that the native generic description mapper is a
    // static one-argument AssetStorageUtil function. Its current implementation
    // starts with the description's explicit icon, then fans out through many
    // description classes. Do not bind the minified method name; validate the
    // result against a real TitanArtifactDescription instead.
    const structural = [];
    const candidates = [];
    for (const { name, fn, source } of staticFunctionRows(AssetUtil)) {
      if (fn.length !== 1) continue;
      if (!source.includes('.icon')) continue;
      if ((source.match(/instanceof/g) ?? []).length < 4) continue;
      structural.push(name);
      const asset = invoke(name);
      if (!asset) continue;
      candidates.push({ name, asset });
    }

    const unique = [...new Map(candidates.map(row => [row.name, row])).values()];
    if (unique.length !== 1) {
      if (unique.length > 1) warn('TITAN_TOTEM_ICON_MAPPER_AMBIGUOUS', unique.map(row => row.name));
      if (diagnostic) {
        diagnostic.stage = structural.length ? ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyAsset : ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyMapper;
        diagnostic.errorCode = unique.length > 1 ? 'TOTEM_BODY_MAPPER_AMBIGUOUS'
          : structural.length ? 'TOTEM_BODY_ASSET_INVALID' : 'TOTEM_BODY_MAPPER_MISSING';
      }
      return null;
    }
    titanArtifactIconMapperMethodCache = unique[0].name;
    if (diagnostic) {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyAsset;
      diagnostic.assetClass = className(unique[0].asset);
    }
    return unique[0].asset;
  }

  function titanTotemBodySemanticRows(Type, semanticName) {
    const rows = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    for (let current = Type; current && typeof current === 'function'; current = current.w ?? null) {
      const properties = current.prototype?.__properties__;
      if (!properties) continue;
      for (const runtimeKey in properties) {
        if (properties[runtimeKey] !== semanticName) continue;
        const key = `${current.j ?? ''}:${runtimeKey}`;
        if (seen.has(key)) continue;
        seen.add(key);
        rows.push({ Type: current, runtimeKey });
      }
    }
    return rows;
  }

  function getTitanTotemBodyTextureGeometry(texture, diagnostic) {
    diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyTextureValidation;
    diagnostic.textureClass = className(texture);
    if (!texture || className(texture) !== CLASS.subTexture) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_CLASS';
      return null;
    }
    const regionRead = readIconTextureProperty(texture, 'get_region');
    const frameRead = readIconTextureProperty(texture, 'get_frame');
    const parentRead = readIconTextureProperty(texture, 'get_parent');
    const rotatedRead = readIconTextureProperty(texture, 'get_rotated');
    if ([regionRead, frameRead, parentRead, rotatedRead].some(row => row.status !== 'ok')) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_GEOMETRY';
      return null;
    }
    const region = regionRead.value;
    const frame = frameRead.value ?? null;
    const parent = parentRead.value;
    const rotated = rotatedRead.value;
    if (className(parent) !== CLASS.concreteTexture) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_GEOMETRY';
      return null;
    }
    const parentWidth = Number(callSemantic(parent, 'get_width', { optional: true }));
    const parentHeight = Number(callSemantic(parent, 'get_height', { optional: true }));
    const values = [region?.x, region?.y, region?.width, region?.height].map(Number);
    const validRegion = values.every(Number.isFinite) && values[0] >= 0 && values[1] >= 0 &&
      values[2] > 0 && values[3] > 0;
    const insideParent = Number.isFinite(parentWidth) && Number.isFinite(parentHeight) &&
      parentWidth > 0 && parentHeight > 0 && values[0] + values[2] <= parentWidth &&
      values[1] + values[3] <= parentHeight;
    const validFrame = !frame || [frame.x, frame.y, frame.width, frame.height].map(Number)
      .every(Number.isFinite) && Number(frame.width) > 0 && Number(frame.height) > 0;
    diagnostic.region = region;
    diagnostic.frame = frame;
    if (rotated !== false || !validRegion || !insideParent || !validFrame) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_GEOMETRY';
      return null;
    }
    return { region, frame };
  }

  // Shape 0 returns RoundImage, whose current contract intentionally has a semantic
  // setter but no getter. Derive its storage from that setter and require native
  // render methods to read the same field; never scan the wrapper object graph.
  function getTitanTotemBodyRoundImageTexture(display, diagnostic) {
    diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyRoundImage;
    if (!display || className(display) !== CLASS.roundImage) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_ROUND_IMAGE_CLASS';
      return null;
    }
    diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyTextureStorage;
    const Type = display.__class__;
    const setters = titanTotemBodySemanticRows(Type, 'set_texture');
    if (setters.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.contractOutcome = setters.length ? 'ambiguous' : 'missing';
      diagnostic.errorCode = setters.length ? 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_SETTER_AMBIGUOUS' : 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_SETTER_MISSING';
      return null;
    }
    const setter = prototypeMethodsDeep(Type).find(row => row.name === setters[0].runtimeKey);
    if (!setter || setter.fn.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_SETTER_INVALID';
      return null;
    }
    const compact = compactClientExecutableSource(setter.source);
    const argument = compact.match(/^function\(([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\)/)?.[1];
    if (!argument) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_ARGUMENT_MISSING';
      return null;
    }
    const fields = [...compact.matchAll(new RegExp(
      `this\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)=${escapeSourcePattern(argument)}(?=[,;}])`, 'g',
    ))].map(match => match[1]);
    const uniqueFields = [...new Set(fields)];
    if (uniqueFields.length !== 1) {
      diagnostic.contractOutcome = uniqueFields.length ? 'ambiguous' : 'missing';
      diagnostic.errorCode = uniqueFields.length ? 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_STORAGE_AMBIGUOUS' : 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_STORAGE_MISSING';
      return null;
    }
    const storage = uniqueFields[0];
    const readers = prototypeMethodsDeep(Type).filter(row =>
      row.name !== setter.name && compactClientExecutableSource(row.source).includes(`this.${storage}`));
    if (!readers.length) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_READER_MISSING';
      return null;
    }
    diagnostic.contractOutcome = 'unique';
    diagnostic.textureMethod = setter.name;
    diagnostic.textureStorage = storage;
    let texture = null;
    try { texture = display[storage] ?? null; }
    catch {
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_TEXTURE_STORAGE_READ';
      return null;
    }
    return getTitanTotemBodyTextureGeometry(texture, diagnostic) ? texture : null;
  }

  async function getTitanTotemNativeIconSpec(asset, diagnostic) {
    diagnostic.route = 'native_consumer';
    diagnostic.primaryRoute = 'native_consumer';
    diagnostic.fallbackUsed = false;
    diagnostic.assetClass = className(asset);
    if (!asset || className(asset) !== CLASS.rsxIconAsset) {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyAsset;
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_RSX_ASSET_REQUIRED';
      return null;
    }
    let contract = null;
    try { contract = getTitanTotemBodyConsumerContract(diagnostic); }
    catch (error) {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyConsumerContract;
      diagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
      return null;
    }
    if (!contract) return null;
    let assetContractValid = false;
    try {
      diagnostic.renderMethod = contract.renderer;
      diagnostic.consumerMethod = contract.callbackMethod;
      diagnostic.readyMethod = contract.ready;
      diagnostic.cleanupOrder = contract.cleanupOrder;
      const readyMethod = asset[contract.ready];
      const renderer = asset[contract.renderer];
      diagnostic.rendererArity = typeof renderer === 'function' ? renderer.length : -1;
      assetContractValid = typeof readyMethod === 'function' &&
        typeof renderer === 'function' && renderer.length === 1;
    } catch (error) {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyConsumerContract;
      diagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
      return null;
    }
    if (!assetContractValid) {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyConsumerContract;
      diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_ASSET_CONTRACT_INVALID';
      return null;
    }

    let added = false;
    let registerAttempted = false;
    let usageCountBefore = null;
    let display = null;
    let spec = null;
    let completionCount = 0;
    const callback = () => { completionCount += 1; };
    try {
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyReady;
      diagnostic.readyBefore = Boolean(asset[contract.ready]());

      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyUsageRegistration;
      diagnostic.usageCountMethod = contract.usageCounter;
      diagnostic.usageBackingField = contract.usageBacking;
      usageCountBefore = readTitanTotemBodyUsageCount(contract, asset);
      diagnostic.usageCountBefore = usageCountBefore;
      let usageAddError = null;
      try { contract.usages[contract.usagesAdd](asset); }
      catch (error) { usageAddError = error; }
      const usageCountAfterAdd = readTitanTotemBodyUsageCount(contract, asset);
      diagnostic.usageCountAfterAdd = usageCountAfterAdd;
      added = usageCountAfterAdd === usageCountBefore + 1;
      if (usageCountAfterAdd !== usageCountBefore && !added) fail('TOTEM_BODY_USAGE_COUNT_MISMATCH');
      if (usageAddError) throw usageAddError;
      if (!added) fail('TOTEM_BODY_USAGE_NOT_ADDED');

      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyLoaderRegistration;
      registerAttempted = true;
      contract.loader[contract.register](asset, callback);

      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyReady;
      const started = Date.now();
      let ready = false;
      while (Date.now() - started <= TOTEM_BODY_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        ready = Boolean(asset[contract.ready]());
        if (ready || completionCount > 0) break;
        await sleep(TOTEM_BODY_LOAD_STEP_MS);
      }
      diagnostic.readyAfter = ready || Boolean(asset[contract.ready]());
      diagnostic.completionMode = completionCount > 0
        ? (diagnostic.readyBefore ? 'synchronous_completion' : 'completion_callback')
        : diagnostic.readyAfter ? 'ready_state' : 'timeout';
      if (!diagnostic.readyAfter && completionCount === 0) {
        diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyReadyTimeout;
        diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_READY_TIMEOUT';
        return null;
      }

      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyNativeRender;
      const rendered = asset[contract.renderer](0);
      if (className(rendered) === CLASS.roundImage) display = rendered;
      const texture = getTitanTotemBodyRoundImageTexture(rendered, diagnostic);
      if (!texture) return null;
      const region = callSemantic(texture, 'get_region', { optional: true });
      const frame = callSemantic(texture, 'get_frame', { optional: true }) ?? null;

      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyCrop;
      const url = cropSubTextureToDataUrl(texture);
      if (!url) {
        diagnostic.errorCode = 'TOTEM_BODY_CROP_FAILED';
        return null;
      }
      spec = {
        url,
        region: {
          x: 0,
          y: 0,
          width: Math.max(1, Number(frame?.width ?? region.width)),
          height: Math.max(1, Number(frame?.height ?? region.height)),
        },
        frame: null,
      };
      diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.specReady;
      diagnostic.primaryStage = ARTWORK_STAGE.specReady;
    } catch (error) {
      diagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
      return null;
    } finally {
      const cleanupErrors = [];
      if (added) {
        try {
          contract.usages[contract.usagesRemove](asset);
          const usageCountAfterRemove = readTitanTotemBodyUsageCount(contract, asset);
          diagnostic.usageCountAfterRemove = usageCountAfterRemove;
          if (usageCountAfterRemove !== usageCountBefore) fail('TOTEM_BODY_USAGE_CLEANUP_MISMATCH');
          diagnostic.cleanupUsageRemoved = true;
        } catch (error) { cleanupErrors.push(error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error'); }
      }
      if (registerAttempted) {
        try {
          contract.loader[contract.unregister](callback);
          diagnostic.cleanupLoaderUnregistered = true;
        } catch (error) { cleanupErrors.push(error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error'); }
      }
      if (display) {
        try {
          let teardownCalled = false;
          if (typeof display[contract.displayTeardown] === 'function') {
            display[contract.displayTeardown](true);
            teardownCalled = true;
          }
          const disposed = callSemantic(display, 'get_disposed', { optional: true });
          let directDisposeCalled = false;
          if (disposed !== true && typeof display.dispose === 'function') {
            display.dispose();
            directDisposeCalled = true;
          }
          if (!teardownCalled && !directDisposeCalled) fail('TOTEM_BODY_DISPLAY_TEARDOWN_MISSING');
          diagnostic.cleanupDisplayDisposed = true;
        } catch (error) { cleanupErrors.push(error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error'); }
      }
      diagnostic.cleanupStage = cleanupErrors.length ? 'failed' : 'complete';
      if (cleanupErrors.length) {
        diagnostic.cleanupErrorCode = cleanupErrors[0];
        diagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyCleanup;
        diagnostic.primaryStage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyCleanup;
        diagnostic.primaryErrorCode = cleanupErrors[0];
        spec = null;
      }
    }
    return spec;
  }

  // The Totem body and the Fusion Skill are both drawn from a deferred task that runs
  // after the resolver, so the resolver's key has to be reachable from the render site
  // too. One definition, used by both, keeps the later image stage on the same row.
  function titanTotemIconSubject(totem) {
    const numericId = Number(totem?.id);
    return Number.isFinite(numericId) && numericId > 0
      ? numericId
      : String(totem?.element ?? ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_UNKNOWN);
  }

  function titanSkillIconSubject(skill) {
    const numericId = Number(skill?.id);
    return Number.isFinite(numericId) && numericId > 0
      ? numericId
      : String(skill?.name ?? ARTWORK_DIAGNOSTIC_UNKNOWN);
  }

  async function getTitanTotemIconSpec(totem) {
    const cacheKey = titanTotemIconSubject(totem);

    if (titanTotemIconSpecCache.has(cacheKey)) return titanTotemIconSpecCache.get(cacheKey);
    if (titanTotemIconSpecPromiseCache.has(cacheKey)) return titanTotemIconSpecPromiseCache.get(cacheKey);

    const promise = (async () => {
      const primaryDiagnostic = {
        stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.descriptionLookup,
        route: 'native_consumer',
        primaryRoute: 'native_consumer',
        fallbackUsed: false,
      };
      const description = totem?.iconDescription
        ?? getTitanSpiritDescriptionInfoByElement(totem?.element).description
        ?? null;
      if (!description) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemBody, cacheKey, { stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.descriptionMissing });
        return null;
      }
      primaryDiagnostic.descriptionClass = className(description);
      if (className(description) !== CLASS.titanArtifactDescription) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemBody, cacheKey, {
          ...primaryDiagnostic,
          stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.descriptionClassMismatch,
          errorCode: 'TOTEM_BODY_DESCRIPTION_CLASS',
        });
        return null;
      }
      let asset = null;
      try { asset = getTitanArtifactIconAsset(description, primaryDiagnostic); }
      catch (error) {
        primaryDiagnostic.stage = ARTWORK_STAGE.totemBodyMapper;
        primaryDiagnostic.errorCode = error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error';
      }
      if (!asset) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemBody, cacheKey, primaryDiagnostic);
        return null;
      }
      const primarySpec = await getTitanTotemNativeIconSpec(asset, primaryDiagnostic);
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemBody, cacheKey, primaryDiagnostic);
      if (primarySpec) {
        titanTotemIconSpecCache.set(cacheKey, primarySpec);
        return primarySpec;
      }

      return null;
    })();

    titanTotemIconSpecPromiseCache.set(cacheKey, promise);
    try {
      return await promise;
    } finally {
      titanTotemIconSpecPromiseCache.delete(cacheKey);
    }
  }

  // Totem native artifact frame (Issue #69).
  //
  // The frame Issue #69 reproduces is the artifact frame from the native Titan /
  // Totem artifact UI, confirmed by eye against the game on 2026-09-06:
  // `artifact_big_purple` is that purple frame. The native item renderer builds the
  // key from the artifact colour:
  //
  //   PlayerTitanArtifactItemRenderer.get_frameAsset()
  //     -> new <wrapper>(<titan artifact storage>, "artifact_big_" + <artifact colour>)
  //
  // The colour comes from the live artifact through semantic `get_color()`, probed on
  // the opponent Current Defense objects HWCT already reads Lv / star from (a Lv 83
  // Totem returned `purple`). The Reference only ever shows opponent Totems, so that
  // is the production path, not a stand-in for it.
  //
  // The colour is never derived from level. HWCT owns a level -> tier mapping for its
  // own fallback frame, and reusing it here would state a native quality the client
  // did not report. No colour means no native frame.
  //
  // The battle route `border_totem_frame_big_<element>` was rejected on a live check:
  // it is the in-battle Totem frame, a different look. Do not reintroduce it here.
  function normalizeTotemColorKey(value) {
    const color = String(value ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    // Only a plain asset-key token may be concatenated into a native lookup key.
    return /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/.test(color) ? color : '';
  }

  // The storage holder field and its one-argument lookup method are read out of the
  // current native sources rather than bound to their minified spellings, which have
  // already moved between client builds. Two shapes carry the same route: the item
  // renderer passes the storage to an asset wrapper, and AssetStorageUtil looks the
  // key up on it directly.
  function getTitanTotemFrameRoute() {
    if (titanTotemFrameRouteCache) return titanTotemFrameRouteCache;

    const directPattern = new RegExp(
      `\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(["']${TOTEM_ARTIFACT_FRAME_KEY_PREFIX}["']`
    );
    const passedPattern = new RegExp(
      `\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*),["']${TOTEM_ARTIFACT_FRAME_KEY_PREFIX}["']`
    );
    const storageFields = new Set();
    const lookupMethods = new Set();
    const consider = source => {
      if (!source.includes(TOTEM_ARTIFACT_FRAME_KEY_PREFIX)) return;
      const compact = source.replace(/\s+/g, '');
      const direct = directPattern.exec(compact);
      if (direct) {
        storageFields.add(direct[1]);
        lookupMethods.add(direct[2]);
        return;
      }
      const passed = passedPattern.exec(compact);
      if (passed) storageFields.add(passed[1]);
    };

    const Renderer = findClass(CLASS.titanArtifactItemRenderer, { optional: true });
    if (Renderer) for (const { source } of prototypeMethodsDeep(Renderer)) consider(source);
    const AssetUtil = findClass(CLASS.assetStorageUtil, { optional: true });
    if (AssetUtil) for (const { source } of staticFunctionRows(AssetUtil)) consider(source);

    // Fail closed rather than pick one: a route that is not uniquely identified is
    // exactly the case the approved fallback exists for.
    if (storageFields.size !== 1 || lookupMethods.size !== 1) {
      if (storageFields.size > 1 || lookupMethods.size > 1) {
        warn(
          'TITAN_TOTEM_FRAME_ROUTE_AMBIGUOUS',
          `${[...storageFields].join('|')} . ${[...lookupMethods].join('|')}`,
        );
      }
      return null;
    }

    titanTotemFrameRouteCache = Object.freeze({
      storageField: [...storageFields][0],
      lookupMethod: [...lookupMethods][0],
    });
    return titanTotemFrameRouteCache;
  }

  // The holder that owns the registered asset storages is found by shape, not by its
  // minified identifier: any object or class exposing the route's storage field with
  // the route's one-argument lookup method on it is a candidate, and the lookup itself
  // decides which candidate actually answers for an artifact frame key.
  function collectTitanTotemFrameStorages(route) {
    const found = [];
    const consider = owner => {
      if (!owner || (typeof owner !== 'object' && typeof owner !== 'function')) return;
      let storage = null;
      try { storage = owner[route.storageField]; } catch { return; }
      if (!storage || (typeof storage !== 'object' && typeof storage !== 'function')) return;
      if (typeof storage[route.lookupMethod] !== 'function') return;
      if (!found.includes(storage)) found.push(storage);
    };

    // The Fusion Skill resolver already reads its own storage off this native holder.
    consider(findClass(CLASS.assetStorage, { optional: true }));

    let root = null;
    try { root = getHaxeRoot(); } catch { root = null; }
    if (!root) return found;

    const seen = new Set();
    const queue = [{ value: root, depth: 0 }];
    let scanned = 0;
    while (queue.length && scanned < TOTEM_FRAME_STORAGE_SCAN_LIMIT && found.length < TOTEM_FRAME_STORAGE_CANDIDATE_LIMIT) {
      const { value, depth } = queue.shift();
      if (!value || (typeof value !== 'object' && typeof value !== 'function') || seen.has(value)) continue;
      seen.add(value);
      scanned += 1;
      consider(value);
      if (depth >= 3) continue;
      if (typeof Node !== 'undefined' && value instanceof Node) continue;
      if (ArrayBuffer.isView(value) || value instanceof ArrayBuffer) continue;
      let children = [];
      try { children = Object.values(value); } catch { continue; }
      for (const child of children) {
        if (child && (typeof child === 'object' || typeof child === 'function')) queue.push({ value: child, depth: depth + 1 });
      }
    }
    return found;
  }

  function getTitanTotemFrameStorages(route) {
    if (titanTotemFrameStoragesCache?.length) return titanTotemFrameStoragesCache;
    const found = collectTitanTotemFrameStorages(route);
    // Only a non-empty scan is remembered. Before the artifact atlas is loaded the
    // holder can be absent, and that has to stay retryable.
    if (found.length) titanTotemFrameStoragesCache = found;
    return found;
  }

  // A cold artifact atlas does not return null here - it throws out of the native
  // lookup, because the storage RSX it reads through is still unloaded. Swallow that
  // in one place so the caller can treat "no texture yet" uniformly.
  function lookupTitanTotemFrameTexture(storage, route, key) {
    try { return storage[route.lookupMethod](key) ?? null; } catch { return null; }
  }

  // The Reference never opens the native Titan / Totem artifact screen - this account
  // may not even own a Totem - so on the first render that atlas is cold and the
  // direct lookup above throws rather than returning null.
  //
  // Getting it loaded took four live probes to pin down, and the answer is narrow:
  //
  //   * acquiring the asset (`ol()`) only moves a refcount;
  //   * `Kic()` inside that acquire is AssetDisposeManager registration;
  //   * the load is started by the loader the native item renderer registers with,
  //     which queues the RSX dependency.
  //
  // So HWCT drives the same three calls the native consumer makes, in the same order,
  // and undoes them afterwards. `Amc(0)` - the fourth call the consumer makes - is
  // deliberately not made: it creates the display object, which a crop does not need,
  // and a live run confirmed the texture becomes available without it.
  //
  // Every name below is read out of the native consumer source, anchored on the asset
  // expression that its usage registration is handed, so nothing is bound to the
  // current minified spelling of any of it.
  function findClassesDeclaringMethod(methodName, limit = 8) {
    const rows = [];
    if (!methodName) return rows;
    let values = [];
    try { values = Object.values(getHaxeRoot()); } catch { return rows; }
    for (const Type of values) {
      if (rows.length >= limit) break;
      if (typeof Type !== 'function' || typeof Type.j !== 'string') continue;
      let fn = null;
      try {
        if (Object.getOwnPropertyNames(Type.prototype ?? {}).includes(methodName)) fn = Type.prototype[methodName];
      } catch { continue; }
      if (typeof fn !== 'function') continue;
      rows.push({ Type, source: String(fn) });
    }
    return rows;
  }

  // The native builder reads the colour off its own data through an accessor chain.
  // Rather than imitate that chain - which would bind another set of minified names -
  // hand it a stand-in `this` that answers any chain and converts to the colour, so
  // the client builds its own asset with its own storage and its own key.
  function makeTotemFrameColorStandIn(color) {
    const proxy = new Proxy(function () {}, {
      get(target, property) {
        if (property === Symbol.toPrimitive) return () => color;
        if (property === 'toString' || property === 'valueOf') return () => color;
        if (property === Symbol.toStringTag) return 'String';
        return proxy;
      },
      apply() { return proxy; },
      construct() { return proxy; },
    });
    return proxy;
  }

  function readTitanTotemFrameConsumerRoute() {
    const Renderer = findClass(CLASS.titanArtifactItemRenderer, { optional: true });
    if (!Renderer) return null;
    const rendererMethods = prototypeMethodsDeep(Renderer);
    const build = rendererMethods.find(row =>
      row.fn.length === 0 && row.source.includes(TOTEM_ARTIFACT_FRAME_KEY_PREFIX));
    if (!build) return null;

    // The renderer hands the built asset to its consumer component.
    const handoff = new RegExp(
      `this\\.[A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(this\\.${build.name}\\(\\)\\)`,
    );
    let consumerMethod = '';
    for (const row of rendererMethods) {
      const match = row.source.replace(/\s+/g, '').match(handoff);
      if (match) { consumerMethod = match[1]; break; }
    }
    if (!consumerMethod) return null;

    // Among the classes declaring it, the consumer is the one registering a usage.
    const declaring = findClassesDeclaringMethod(consumerMethod);
    const consumer = declaring.find(row => row.source.includes('.usages.'));
    if (!consumer) return null;
    const setup = consumer.source.replace(/\s+/g, '');

    // Anchor everything on the asset expression the usage registration is given.
    const usagesCall = setup.match(/\.usages\.([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)\(([^)]*)\)/);
    if (!usagesCall) return null;
    const usagesAdd = usagesCall[1];
    const assetExpression = usagesCall[2];
    const asset = assetExpression.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');

    const register = setup.match(
      new RegExp(`\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(${asset},`),
    );
    const ready = setup.match(new RegExp(`${asset}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(\\)`));
    if (!register || !ready) return null;

    // The teardown calls live on another method of the same class.
    let usagesRemove = '';
    let unregister = '';
    for (const row of prototypeMethodsDeep(consumer.Type)) {
      if (row.name === consumerMethod) continue;
      const body = row.source.replace(/\s+/g, '');
      if (!usagesRemove) {
        const match = body.match(new RegExp(`\\.usages\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(${asset}\\)`));
        if (match && match[1] !== usagesAdd) usagesRemove = match[1];
      }
      if (!unregister) {
        const match = body.match(new RegExp(`\\.${register[1]}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\(`));
        if (match && match[1] !== register[2]) unregister = match[1];
      }
      if (usagesRemove && unregister) break;
    }
    // Without both teardown calls this would leak a usage and a callback, so it fails
    // closed instead: the approved fallback costs nothing.
    if (!usagesRemove || !unregister) return null;

    // The context owning both collections, by shape rather than by its identifier.
    const context = findTitanTotemFrameConsumerContext(usagesAdd, register[1], register[2]);
    if (!context) return null;

    return Object.freeze({
      build: build.fn,
      usages: context.usages,
      loader: context.loader,
      usagesAdd,
      usagesRemove,
      register: register[2],
      unregister,
      ready: ready[1],
    });
  }

  function findTitanTotemFrameConsumerContext(usagesAdd, loaderField, registerMethod) {
    const read = owner => {
      if (!owner || (typeof owner !== 'object' && typeof owner !== 'function')) return null;
      try {
        const usages = owner['usages'];
        if (!usages || typeof usages[usagesAdd] !== 'function') return null;
        const loader = owner[loaderField];
        if (!loader || typeof loader[registerMethod] !== 'function') return null;
        return { usages, loader };
      } catch { return null; }
    };

    let values = [];
    try { values = Object.values(getHaxeRoot()); } catch { return null; }
    for (const Type of values) {
      if (typeof Type !== 'function') continue;
      let names = [];
      try { names = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(Type); } catch { continue; }
      for (const name of names) {
        if (['length', 'name', 'prototype', 'caller', 'arguments'].includes(name)) continue;
        let value = null;
        try { value = Type[name]; } catch { continue; }
        const found = read(value);
        if (found) return found;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  function getTitanTotemFrameConsumerRoute() {
    const cached = titanTotemFrameConsumerRouteCache;
    if (cached
      && typeof cached.usages?.[cached.usagesAdd] === 'function'
      && typeof cached.loader?.[cached.register] === 'function') {
      return cached;
    }
    titanTotemFrameConsumerRouteCache = null;
    try {
      titanTotemFrameConsumerRouteCache = readTitanTotemFrameConsumerRoute();
    } catch (error) {
      warn('TITAN_TOTEM_FRAME_CONSUMER_ROUTE_FAILED', error);
    }
    return titanTotemFrameConsumerRouteCache;
  }

  // Registers the asset the way the native consumer does, waits for the load the
  // registration starts, reads while it is still registered, and always unwinds.
  async function withTitanTotemFrameAssetLoaded(consumer, asset, read) {
    let added = false;
    let registered = false;
    // The native consumer passes a bound handler here; HWCT has nothing to redraw, so
    // this is only an identity for the matching unregister call.
    const callback = () => {};
    try {
      try { consumer.usages[consumer.usagesAdd](asset); added = true; }
      catch { return null; }
      try { consumer.loader[consumer.register](asset, callback); registered = true; }
      catch { return null; }

      const started = Date.now();
      while (Date.now() - started <= TOTEM_FRAME_LOAD_TIMEOUT_MS) {
        let ready = false;
        try { ready = Boolean(asset[consumer.ready]()); } catch { return null; }
        if (ready) return read();
        await sleep(TOTEM_FRAME_LOAD_STEP_MS);
      }
      return null;
    } finally {
      if (registered) { try { consumer.loader[consumer.unregister](callback); } catch {} }
      if (added) { try { consumer.usages[consumer.usagesRemove](asset); } catch {} }
    }
  }

  async function resolveTitanTotemFrameDataUrl(storage, route, key, color) {
    // 1. the ordinary direct lookup, for an atlas that is already loaded.
    const direct = cropValidatedNativeTexture(lookupTitanTotemFrameTexture(storage, route, key));
    if (direct) return direct;

    // 2. otherwise drive the native consumer route, and only that route.
    const consumer = getTitanTotemFrameConsumerRoute();
    if (!consumer) return null;
    let asset = null;
    try { asset = consumer.build.call(makeTotemFrameColorStandIn(color)); }
    catch { return null; }
    if (!asset || typeof asset[consumer.ready] !== 'function') return null;

    // The crop runs inside, while the asset is still registered: the atlas goes cold
    // again once it is unwound, and the crop already yields a standalone PNG.
    return await withTitanTotemFrameAssetLoaded(consumer, asset, () =>
      cropValidatedNativeTexture(lookupTitanTotemFrameTexture(storage, route, key)));
  }

  async function getTitanTotemNativeFrameSpec(colorValue) {
    const color = normalizeTotemColorKey(colorValue);
    // The raw value is deliberately not echoed into the record: it comes straight
    // from the client and the colour key is the only shape allowed into a lookup.
    if (!color) {
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemFrame, 'unknown', { stage: 'color_unresolved' });
      return null;
    }
    if (titanTotemFrameSpecCache.has(color)) return titanTotemFrameSpecCache.get(color);
    // Two Totems can share a colour, and a cold atlas must be warmed once, not twice.
    if (titanTotemFrameSpecPromiseCache.has(color)) return titanTotemFrameSpecPromiseCache.get(color);

    const promise = (async () => {
      try {
        const route = getTitanTotemFrameRoute();
        if (!route) {
          recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemFrame, color, { stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.routeUnresolved });
          return null;
        }
        const key = `${TOTEM_ARTIFACT_FRAME_KEY_PREFIX}${color}`;
        let storageCount = 0;
        for (const storage of getTitanTotemFrameStorages(route)) {
          storageCount += 1;
          const frameDataUrl = await resolveTitanTotemFrameDataUrl(storage, route, key, color);
          if (!frameDataUrl) continue;
          const spec = Object.freeze({ frameDataUrl });
          // Only a resolved frame is cached. A miss can simply mean the artifact
          // atlas is still not loaded, and must never harden into "this colour has
          // no native frame" - that would also freeze out a colour added later.
          titanTotemFrameSpecCache.set(color, spec);
          recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemFrame, color, {
            stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.specReady,
            storageCount,
          });
          return spec;
        }
        // A miss can still mean "the artifact atlas is not loaded yet", which is why
        // it is not cached. The record says which of the two ends was reached.
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemFrame, color, {
          stage: storageCount ? ARTWORK_STAGE.textureMissing : ARTWORK_STAGE.routeUnresolved,
          storageCount,
        });
        return null;
      } catch (error) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemFrame, color, {
          stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.resolverError,
          errorCode: error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error',
        });
        warn(`TITAN_TOTEM_NATIVE_FRAME_FAILED_${color}`, error);
        return null;
      }
    })();

    titanTotemFrameSpecPromiseCache.set(color, promise);
    try {
      return await promise;
    } finally {
      titanTotemFrameSpecPromiseCache.delete(color);
    }
  }

  function getTitanSkillIconStorage() {
    if (titanSkillIconStorageCache && className(titanSkillIconStorageCache) === CLASS.skillIconAssetStorage) {
      return titanSkillIconStorageCache;
    }
    const AssetStorage = findClass(CLASS.assetStorage, { optional: true });
    if (!AssetStorage) return null;
    const matches = [];
    try {
      for (const key of Object.getOwnPropertyNames(AssetStorage)) {
        let value = null;
        try { value = AssetStorage[key]; } catch { continue; }
        if (className(value) === CLASS.skillIconAssetStorage) matches.push(value);
      }
    } catch {}
    const unique = uniqueRefs(matches);
    if (unique.length !== 1) return null;
    titanSkillIconStorageCache = unique[0];
    return titanSkillIconStorageCache;
  }

  function getTitanSkillIconResolver(description) {
    const storage = getTitanSkillIconStorage();
    if (!storage || !description) return null;

    const validate = resolver => {
      if (!resolver?.method || !resolver?.identField) return null;
      const ident = String(description?.[resolver.identField] ?? '').trim();
      if (!ident) return null;
      const fn = storage?.[resolver.method];
      if (typeof fn !== 'function') return null;
      try {
        const asset = fn.call(storage, description) ?? null;
        const assetClass = className(asset) ?? '';
        // Batch 7: the approved Totem Skill route is the current-client
        // ImageIconAsset contract. Arbitrary *IconAsset implementations belong to
        // the retired generic compatibility path and are not accepted here.
        if (assetClass !== 'game.assets.ImageIconAsset') return null;
        return { ...resolver, ident, asset };
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    };

    if (titanSkillIconResolverCache) {
      const cached = validate(titanSkillIconResolverCache);
      if (cached) return cached;
      titanSkillIconResolverCache = null;
    }

    // Resolve the inherited SkillIconAssetStorage method by source shape. The
    // minified method and SkillDescription backing field names are deliberately
    // not hard-coded. Current native code reads <textureIdent> and resolves
    // `<textureIdent>.png` through the asset manager.
    const candidates = [];
    for (const { name, fn, source } of prototypeMethodsDeep(storage)) {
      if (fn.length !== 1 || !source.includes('.png')) continue;
      const compact = source.replace(/\s+/g, '');
      const arg = compact.match(/^function\(([$\w]+)\)/)?.[1];
      if (!arg) continue;
      const escapedArg = arg.replace(/[$]/g, '\\$&');
      const identMatch = new RegExp(`${escapedArg}\\.([A-Za-z_$][\\w$]*)\\+["']\\.png["']`).exec(compact);
      if (!identMatch) continue;
      if (!/new\s+[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*\(/.test(source)) continue;
      const resolver = { method: name, identField: identMatch[1] };
      const resolved = validate(resolver);
      if (resolved) candidates.push(resolved);
    }

    const unique = [...new Map(candidates.map(row => [`${row.method}|${row.identField}`, row])).values()];
    if (unique.length !== 1) {
      if (unique.length > 1) warn('TITAN_SKILL_ICON_RESOLVER_AMBIGUOUS', unique.map(row => row.method));
      return null;
    }
    titanSkillIconResolverCache = { method: unique[0].method, identField: unique[0].identField };
    return unique[0];
  }

  function resourceUrlMatchesLogicalFile(urlValue, fileName) {
    const target = String(fileName ?? '').trim();
    const url = String(urlValue ?? '').trim();
    if (!target || !url) return false;
    let pathName = '';
    try { pathName = new URL(url, location.href).pathname; } catch { pathName = url.split('?')[0]; }
    let decoded = pathName;
    try { decoded = decodeURIComponent(pathName); } catch {}
    const base = decoded.split('/').pop() ?? '';
    if (base === target) return true;
    const dot = target.lastIndexOf('.');
    if (dot <= 0) return false;
    const stem = target.slice(0, dot);
    const ext = target.slice(dot);
    // Hero Wars often fingerprints resource filenames as <stem>.<hash><ext>.
    return base.startsWith(`${stem}.`) && base.endsWith(ext);
  }

  function findLoadedResourceUrl(fileName) {
    const target = String(fileName ?? '').trim();
    if (!target || typeof performance === 'undefined' || typeof performance.getEntriesByType !== 'function') return '';
    let entries = [];
    try { entries = performance.getEntriesByType('resource') ?? []; } catch { return ''; }
    const hits = [];
    for (const entry of entries) {
      const url = String(entry?.name ?? '');
      if (resourceUrlMatchesLogicalFile(url, target)) hits.push(url);
    }
    const unique = [...new Set(hits)];
    return unique.length === 1 ? unique[0] : '';
  }

  async function getStandaloneImageSpec(url) {
    if (!url) return null;
    const size = await getImageSize(url);
    if (!size?.width || !size?.height) return null;
    return {
      url,
      region: { x: 0, y: 0, width: size.width, height: size.height },
      frame: null,
    };
  }

  async function getImageIconAssetStandaloneSpec(asset, logicalFile) {
    if (!asset || className(asset) !== 'game.assets.ImageIconAsset') return null;

    // Harness v0.2.20 captured the native Fusion Skill route directly:
    // SkillDescription -> SkillIconAssetStorage -> ImageIconAsset -> get_image()
    // -> ImageFile. Use that exact native file instead of trying to extract the
    // preview SubTexture, which is a generic placeholder in the current client.
    const imageFile = callSemantic(asset, 'get_image', { optional: true })
      ?? findObjectByClassLimited(
        asset,
        'engine.core.assets.file.ImageFile',
        { maxDepth: 4, maxNodes: 60 }
      )
      ?? null;

    let url = getImageFileUrl(imageFile);
    if (url && logicalFile && !resourceUrlMatchesLogicalFile(url, logicalFile)) {
      url = '';
    }

    // If get_url is not exposed yet, use only the exact already-loaded logical
    // file from ResourceTiming. Never synthesize a CDN URL.
    if (!url) url = findLoadedResourceUrl(logicalFile);
    return url ? await getStandaloneImageSpec(url) : null;
  }

  async function getTitanSkillIconSpec(skill) {
    const cacheKey = titanSkillIconSubject(skill);

    if (titanSkillIconSpecCache.has(cacheKey)) return titanSkillIconSpecCache.get(cacheKey);
    if (titanSkillIconSpecPromiseCache.has(cacheKey)) return titanSkillIconSpecPromiseCache.get(cacheKey);

    const promise = (async () => {
      const description = skill?.iconDescription ?? null;
      const resolved = getTitanSkillIconResolver(description);
      if (!resolved) {
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemSkill, cacheKey, {
          stage: description ? ARTWORK_STAGE.assetMissing : ARTWORK_STAGE.descriptionMissing,
        });
        return null;
      }

      const logicalFile = `${resolved.ident}.png`;

      // Fast/current-client path confirmed by Harness v0.2.20.
      const imageSpec = await getImageIconAssetStandaloneSpec(resolved.asset, logicalFile);
      if (imageSpec) {
        titanSkillIconSpecCache.set(cacheKey, imageSpec);
        recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemSkill, cacheKey, {
          stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.specReady,
          route: 'image_icon_asset',
          assetClass: className(resolved.asset),
        });
        return imageSpec;
      }

      const url = findLoadedResourceUrl(logicalFile);
      const fallback = url ? await getStandaloneImageSpec(url) : null;
      if (fallback) titanSkillIconSpecCache.set(cacheKey, fallback);
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemSkill, cacheKey, {
        stage: fallback ? ARTWORK_STAGE.specReady : ARTWORK_STAGE.imageUrlUnresolved,
        route: 'resource_timing',
        assetClass: className(resolved.asset),
      });
      return fallback;
    })();

    titanSkillIconSpecPromiseCache.set(cacheKey, promise);
    try {
      return await promise;
    } finally {
      titanSkillIconSpecPromiseCache.delete(cacheKey);
    }
  }

  async function getWarFlagSpriteSpec(id) {
    const numericId = Number(id);
    if (!Number.isFinite(numericId) || numericId <= 0) return null;
    try {
      // rc18 incorrectly reused the Pattern-only mapper for BannerDescription.
      // The live client exposes War Flag bodies through InventoryAssetStorage's
      // BannerDescription -> atlas SubTexture wrapper instead. Crop that native
      // texture to a self-contained PNG, then feed the normal sprite renderer.
      const texture = getNativeWarFlagTexture(numericId);
      if (texture) {
        const region = callSemantic(texture, 'get_region', { optional: true });
        const frame = callSemantic(texture, 'get_frame', { optional: true });
        const dataUrl = cropSubTextureToDataUrl(texture);
        if (dataUrl) {
          const width = Math.max(1, Number(frame?.width ?? region?.width ?? 1));
          const height = Math.max(1, Number(frame?.height ?? region?.height ?? 1));
          recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.warFlag, numericId, {
            stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.specReady,
            route: 'native_subtexture',
          });
          return {
            url: dataUrl,
            region: { x: 0, y: 0, width, height },
            frame: null,
          };
        }
      }

      // Batch 8: the live-proven native body texture is the only War Flag artwork
      // route. Do not fall through to the Pattern mapper or generic IconAsset
      // lifecycle: that path is not an independent resilience route.
      const description = getBannerDescription(numericId);
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.warFlag, numericId, {
        stage: description
          ? (texture ? 'crop_failed' : ARTWORK_STAGE.textureMissing)
          : ARTWORK_STAGE.descriptionMissing,
        route: 'native_subtexture',
      });
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      recordArtworkDiagnostic(ARTWORK_FAMILY.warFlag, numericId, {
        stage: ARTWORK_STAGE.resolverError,
        errorCode: error?.code ?? error?.name ?? 'Error',
      });
      warn(`WAR_FLAG_SPRITE_FAILED_${numericId}`, error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  function getWarFlagName(id) {
    const numericId = Number(id);
    if (!Number.isFinite(numericId) || numericId <= 0) return '';
    try {
      const description = getBannerDescription(numericId);
      const name = description ? callSemantic(description, 'get_name', { optional: true }) : null;
      if (name != null && String(name).trim()) return String(name).trim();
    } catch {}
    return '';
  }

  function getWarFlagDisplayName(id) {
    const numericId = Number(id);
    if (!Number.isFinite(numericId) || numericId <= 0) return '';
    return getWarFlagName(numericId) || warFlagIdText(numericId);
  }

  function warFlagIdentityText(id) {
    return referenceIdentityText(getWarFlagName(id), id, warFlagIdText(id));
  }

  // `family` / `subject` are the diagnostic context of whichever family owns this
  // sprite. They change nothing about the sprite itself; an uninstrumented caller
  // passes none and records nothing, exactly as before.
  async function createSpriteFromSpec(spec, size, classNameValue = 'unit-sprite', title = '', { family = null, subject = null } = {}) {
    if (!spec?.url) return null;
    const imageSize = await getImageSize(spec.url);
    if (!imageSize?.width || !imageSize?.height) {
      recordArtworkImageStage(family, subject, 'image_load_failed');
      return null;
    }
    const logicalWidth = Number(spec.frame?.width ?? spec.region.width) || 1;
    const logicalHeight = Number(spec.frame?.height ?? spec.region.height) || 1;
    const scale = Math.min(size / logicalWidth, size / logicalHeight);
    const frameX = Number(spec.frame?.x ?? 0);
    const frameY = Number(spec.frame?.y ?? 0);
    const element = document.createElement('span');
    element.className = classNameValue;
    element.title = title;
    element.style.display = 'block';
    element.style.width = `${size}px`;
    element.style.height = `${size}px`;
    element.style.backgroundImage = `url("${spec.url}")`;
    element.style.backgroundRepeat = 'no-repeat';
    element.style.backgroundSize = `${imageSize.width * scale}px ${imageSize.height * scale}px`;
    element.style.backgroundPosition = `${(-spec.region.x - frameX) * scale}px ${(-spec.region.y - frameY) * scale}px`;
    element.style.flex = '0 0 auto';
    recordArtworkImageStage(family, subject, 'image_ready');
    return element;
  }

  function getRendererData(renderer) {
    const data = callSemantic(renderer, 'get_data', { optional: true });
    if (data) return data;
    const SlotVO = findClass(CLASS.cowSlotVO);
    return Object.values(renderer ?? {}).find(value => value instanceof SlotVO) ?? null;
  }

  function findCowSlotRendererFast(snapshot, slotNumber) {
    const Renderer = findClass(CLASS.cowAttackRenderer, { optional: true });
    if (!Renderer || !snapshot?.popup) return null;
    const queue = [{ value: snapshot.popup, depth: 0 }];
    const seen = new Set();
    let scanned = 0;
    while (queue.length && scanned < FLAG_SCAN_LIMIT) {
      const { value, depth } = queue.shift();
      if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || seen.has(value)) continue;
      seen.add(value);
      scanned += 1;
      if (value instanceof Renderer) {
        const data = getRendererData(value);
        if (Number(callSemantic(data, 'get_slotNumber', { optional: true })) === Number(slotNumber)) return value;
        continue;
      }
      if (depth >= 4) continue;
      for (const child of Object.values(value)) {
        if (!child || typeof child !== 'object' || child instanceof Node || ArrayBuffer.isView(child) || child instanceof ArrayBuffer) continue;
        if (Array.isArray(child)) {
          for (const nested of child) if (nested && typeof nested === 'object') queue.push({ value: nested, depth: depth + 1 });
        } else {
          queue.push({ value: child, depth: depth + 1 });
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  function captureCurrentFlagBody(snapshot, slotNumber) {
    const renderer = findCowSlotRendererFast(snapshot, slotNumber);
    if (!renderer) return null;
    const clip = renderer.clip ?? Object.values(renderer).find(value => value && typeof value === 'object' && className(value)?.endsWith('Clip'));
    const bannerComponent = clip?.He ?? Object.values(clip ?? {}).find(value => className(value) === 'game.view.gui.components.banner.MiniBannerClipWithTooltip');
    if (!bannerComponent) return null;
    const texture = findSubTexture(bannerComponent, { maxDepth: 7, preferSize: [84, 84] });
    return cropSubTextureToDataUrl(texture);
  }

  function findDefenseEditorOpenMethod(demo) {
    return findUniqueSourceMethodOrNull(
      demo,
      (source, fn) => {
        if (fn.length !== 1) return false;
        return (
          source.includes('this.player') &&
          /new [A-Za-z0-9_$]+\(this\.player,/.test(source) &&
          /\.open\(\)/.test(source) &&
          /\.close\(\)/.test(source) &&
          /null==[A-Za-z0-9_$]+&&\([A-Za-z0-9_$]+=!1\)/.test(source)
        );
      }
    );
  }

  async function openDefenseEditorFailSoft(demo) {
    try {
      await waitFor(() => hasOpenDemoBattle(), {
        timeout: 2500,
        step: 70,
        code: 'DEMO_POPUP_OPEN_TIMEOUT',
      });

      const methodName = findDefenseEditorOpenMethod(demo);
      if (!methodName || typeof demo?.[methodName] !== 'function') {
        warn('DEFENSE_EDITOR_METHOD_NOT_FOUND');
        return false;
      }

      log('Opening native Defense editor', { methodName });
      demo[methodName](false);

      // Confirm the same native editor that the pencil button opens.
      await waitFor(
        () => getOpenPopupsByClass(CLASS.demoDefenseGatherPopup).length === 1,
        { timeout: 2500, step: 70, code: 'DEFENSE_EDITOR_OPEN_TIMEOUT' }
      );
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      warn('DEFENSE_EDITOR_AUTO_OPEN_FAILED', error);
      return false;
    }
  }

  function formatDefenseLabel(value) {
    if (!value) return t('currentDefense');
    const building = value.building || t('defense');
    const slot = value.slotNumber != null ? ` #${value.slotNumber}` : '';
    const player = value.playerName ? ` · ${value.playerName}` : '';
    return `${building}${slot}${player}`;
  }

  function installEightWayResize({
    host,
    panel,
    shadow,
    minWidth,
    minHeight = PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT,
    getVisualScale = () => 1,
    onResizeStart,
    onResize,
    onResizeEnd,
  }) {
    const directions = ['n', 'ne', 'e', 'se', 's', 'sw', 'w', 'nw'];
    const handles = [];
    let active = null;

    for (const direction of directions) {
      const handle = document.createElement('div');
      handle.className = `resize-handle resize-${direction}`;
      handle.dataset.direction = direction;
      panel.appendChild(handle);
      handles.push(handle);

      handle.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
        if (event.button !== 0) return;
        const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
        const visualScale = Math.max(.01, Number(getVisualScale?.()) || 1);
        active = {
          id: event.pointerId,
          direction,
          startX: event.clientX,
          startY: event.clientY,
          left: rect.left,
          top: rect.top,
          width: rect.width,
          height: rect.height,
          right: rect.right,
          bottom: rect.bottom,
          visualScale,
          lastAppliedRect: {
            left: rect.left,
            top: rect.top,
            width: rect.width,
            height: rect.height,
            layoutWidth:rect.width / visualScale,
            layoutHeight:rect.height / visualScale,
            visualScale,
          },
        };
        panel.style.width = `${(rect.width / visualScale).toFixed(3)}px`;
        panel.style.height = `${(rect.height / visualScale).toFixed(3)}px`;
        onResizeStart?.({
          left:rect.left,
          top:rect.top,
          width:rect.width,
          height:rect.height,
          layoutWidth:rect.width / visualScale,
          layoutHeight:rect.height / visualScale,
          visualScale,
        });
        handle.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
      });

      handle.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
        if (!active || event.pointerId !== active.id || active.direction !== direction) return;
        const dx = event.clientX - active.startX;
        const dy = event.clientY - active.startY;
        const d = active.direction;

        let left = active.left;
        let top = active.top;
        let width = active.width;
        let height = active.height;

        const logicalMinWidth = typeof minWidth === 'function' ? minWidth() : minWidth;
        const visualMinWidth = Math.max(1, Number(logicalMinWidth) || 1) * active.visualScale;
        const visualMinHeight = Math.max(1, Number(minHeight) || 1) * active.visualScale;

        if (d.includes('e')) {
          const maxWidth = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth - active.left);
          const localMinWidth = Math.min(visualMinWidth, maxWidth);
          width = clamp(active.width + dx, localMinWidth, maxWidth);
        }
        if (d.includes('s')) {
          height = clamp(active.height + dy, Math.min(visualMinHeight, Math.max(1, window.innerHeight - active.top)), Math.max(1, window.innerHeight - active.top));
        }
        if (d.includes('w')) {
          const localMinWidth = Math.min(visualMinWidth, Math.max(1, active.right));
          left = clamp(active.left + dx, 0, Math.max(0, active.right - localMinWidth));
          width = active.right - left;
        }
        if (d.includes('n')) {
          const localMinHeight = Math.min(visualMinHeight, Math.max(1, active.bottom));
          top = clamp(active.top + dy, 0, Math.max(0, active.bottom - localMinHeight));
          height = active.bottom - top;
        }

        width = Math.min(width, window.innerWidth - left);
        height = Math.min(height, window.innerHeight - top);
        const appliedRect = {
          left,
          top,
          width,
          height,
          layoutWidth:width / active.visualScale,
          layoutHeight:height / active.visualScale,
          visualScale:active.visualScale,
        };
        active.lastAppliedRect = appliedRect;
        host.style.left = `${appliedRect.left.toFixed(3)}px`;
        host.style.top = `${appliedRect.top.toFixed(3)}px`;
        panel.style.width = `${appliedRect.layoutWidth.toFixed(3)}px`;
        panel.style.height = `${appliedRect.layoutHeight.toFixed(3)}px`;
        onResize?.(appliedRect);
      });

      const finish = event => {
        if (!active || event.pointerId !== active.id || active.direction !== direction) return;
        // Commit the last geometry actually applied by this gesture. A layout task
        // that touches the inline size between the last move and pointer release
        // must not become the user's newly saved preferred width.
        const rect = active.lastAppliedRect;
        host.style.left = `${rect.left.toFixed(3)}px`;
        host.style.top = `${rect.top.toFixed(3)}px`;
        panel.style.width = `${rect.layoutWidth.toFixed(3)}px`;
        panel.style.height = `${rect.layoutHeight.toFixed(3)}px`;
        active = null;
        onResizeEnd?.(rect);
      };
      handle.addEventListener('pointerup', finish);
      handle.addEventListener('pointercancel', finish);
    }

    return () => {
      for (const handle of handles) handle.remove();
    };
  }

  // Combat Training and Defense Reference intentionally use one header/settings
  // treatment. Both panels render in separate Shadow DOMs, so the shared CSS string is
  // inserted into each root rather than maintaining two almost-identical rule sets.
  const SHARED_HEADER_SETTINGS_CSS = `

    .settings-save, .settings-reset { width:100%; margin-top:7px; }
    .settings-save { background:rgba(221,177,94,.18); border-color:rgba(240,199,120,.5); }
    .settings-toggle { display:flex; align-items:flex-start; gap:7px; padding:4px 0; color:#e8e8e8; cursor:pointer; line-height:1.25; }
    .settings-toggle input { flex:0 0 auto; width:15px; height:15px; margin:1px 0 0; accent-color:#ddb15e; cursor:pointer; }
    .settings-toggle span { min-width:0; overflow-wrap:anywhere; }
  `;

  function getHeaderTechnicalMinWidth(header, shadowRoot, fallbackWidth, visualScale = 1) {
    try {
      const safeScale = Math.max(.01, Number(visualScale) || 1);
      const headerStyle = getComputedStyle(header);
      const padding = Number.parseFloat(headerStyle.paddingLeft || '0') + Number.parseFloat(headerStyle.paddingRight || '0');
      const toolMarkWidth = (shadowRoot.querySelector('.tool-mark')?.getBoundingClientRect?.().width ?? 0) / safeScale;
      const actions = [...shadowRoot.querySelectorAll('.head-action')].filter(action => !action.hidden);
      const controlsWidth = actions.reduce((sum, action) => sum + ((action.getBoundingClientRect?.().width || 0) / safeScale), 0);
      const actionRow = shadowRoot.querySelector('.header-actions');
      const actionStyle = actionRow ? getComputedStyle(actionRow) : null;
      const gap = Number.parseFloat(actionStyle?.columnGap || actionStyle?.gap || '0') || 0;
      const dragReserve = 14;
      const measured = Math.ceil(padding + toolMarkWidth + controlsWidth + Math.max(0, actions.length - 1) * gap + dragReserve);
      return Math.max(fallbackWidth, measured);
    } catch {
      return fallbackWidth;
    }
  }

  function createTransientSettingsPopover(settingsPopover, settingsButton) {
    function isInside(event) {
      // Shadow DOM clicks retarget to the host at document level. If the composed
      // path is unavailable, fail soft by keeping the popover open.
      if (typeof event?.composedPath !== 'function') return true;
      const path = event.composedPath();
      return path.includes(settingsPopover) || path.includes(settingsButton);
    }

    function close() {
      document.removeEventListener('click', handleOutsideClick, true);
      settingsPopover.hidden = true;
    }

    function handleOutsideClick(event) {
      if (!isInside(event)) close();
    }

    function reposition() {
      if (settingsPopover.hidden || typeof requestAnimationFrame !== 'function') return;
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (settingsPopover.hidden || typeof settingsPopover.getBoundingClientRect !== 'function') return;
        settingsPopover.style.transform = '';
        const rect = settingsPopover.getBoundingClientRect();
        const margin = 8;
        let deltaX = 0;
        let deltaY = 0;
        if (rect.left < margin) deltaX = margin - rect.left;
        else if (rect.right > window.innerWidth - margin) deltaX = window.innerWidth - margin - rect.right;
        if (rect.top < margin) deltaY = margin - rect.top;
        else if (rect.bottom > window.innerHeight - margin) deltaY = window.innerHeight - margin - rect.bottom;
        if (deltaX || deltaY) settingsPopover.style.transform = `translate(${Math.round(deltaX)}px, ${Math.round(deltaY)}px)`;
      });
    }

    function open() {
      if (!settingsPopover.hidden) return;
      settingsPopover.hidden = false;
      // The opening click has already passed document capture. Later outside clicks
      // are observed even if a page-level bubble handler stops propagation.
      document.addEventListener('click', handleOutsideClick, true);
      reposition();
    }

    function toggle() {
      if (settingsPopover.hidden) open();
      else close();
    }

    return { open, close, toggle, handleOutsideClick, reposition };
  }

  // Both panels use one launcher transition: move the snapped ⚔ launcher from the
  // expanded Minimize control, then briefly emphasize its arrival. Placement,
  // dragging, edge snapping, and reopen semantics remain owned by each panel.
  function createMiniLauncherTransition(host, miniButton) {
    let moveAnimation = null;
    let pulseAnimation = null;

    function cancel() {
      if (moveAnimation) {
        try { moveAnimation.cancel(); } catch (_) {}
        moveAnimation = null;
      }
      if (pulseAnimation) {
        try { pulseAnimation.cancel(); } catch (_) {}
        pulseAnimation = null;
      }
    }

    function start(originRect = null) {
      cancel();
      const hasValidOrigin = Boolean(
        originRect
        && Number.isFinite(originRect.left)
        && Number.isFinite(originRect.top)
        && (originRect.width > 0 || originRect.height > 0)
      );
      if (!hasValidOrigin || typeof host.animate !== 'function' || typeof miniButton.animate !== 'function') return;

      const finalRect = miniButton.getBoundingClientRect();
      const originCenterX = originRect.left + originRect.width / 2;
      const originCenterY = originRect.top + originRect.height / 2;
      const finalCenterX = finalRect.left + finalRect.width / 2;
      const finalCenterY = finalRect.top + finalRect.height / 2;
      const deltaX = Math.round(originCenterX - finalCenterX);
      const deltaY = Math.round(originCenterY - finalCenterY);

      try {
        moveAnimation = host.animate(
          [
            { transform: `translate(${deltaX}px, ${deltaY}px)` },
            { transform: 'translate(0, 0)' }
          ],
          {
            duration: 1500,
            easing: 'cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.8, 0.2, 1)',
            fill: 'none'
          }
        );
        moveAnimation.finished
          .then(() => {
            moveAnimation = null;
            if (!host.isConnected) return;
            try {
              pulseAnimation = miniButton.animate(
                [
                  {
                    transform: 'scale(1)',
                    boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.35)',
                    borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,.22)'
                  },
                  {
                    transform: 'scale(1.5)',
                    boxShadow: '0 0 0 2px rgba(227,182,95,.85), 0 0 16px rgba(227,182,95,.65), 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.6)',
                    borderColor: 'rgba(227,182,95,.95)',
                    offset: 0.5
                  },
                  {
                    transform: 'scale(1)',
                    boxShadow: '0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.35)',
                    borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,.22)'
                  }
                ],
                {
                  duration: 450,
                  easing: 'ease-out',
                  fill: 'none'
                }
              );
              return pulseAnimation.finished
                .then(() => { pulseAnimation = null; })
                .catch(() => {});
            } catch (_) {
              cancel();
            }
          })
          .catch(() => {});
      } catch (_) {
        cancel();
      }
    }

    return { start, cancel };
  }

           section body keeps the ordinary panel background. */
        .past-boundary .past-head { margin:-6px -5px 4px; padding:5px 5px 6px; background:var(--hwct-header-bg); }
        .hero-defeated .hero-sprite, .hero-defeated .titan-portrait-sprite { filter:grayscale(1) brightness(.52); opacity:.78; }
        .hero-defeated .unit-portrait-sprite { filter:grayscale(1) brightness(.52); opacity:.78; }
        /* A confirmed or unresolved Patron marker belongs to its defeated Hero: retain
           artwork / identity / question mark, but filter the entire badge so its yellow
           ring dims too. Before-attack unknown keeps its separately approved weak
           treatment, and Main Pet has no such descendant relationship. */
        .hero-defeated .patron-sprite:not(.patron-unknown) { filter:grayscale(1) brightness(.52); opacity:.78; }
        .defeated-cross, .defeated-label { position:absolute; z-index:10; pointer-events:none; display:none; }
        .defeated-cross { inset:0; align-items:center; justify-content:center; color:#fff; font:900 29px/1 Arial; text-shadow:0 1px 3px #000,0 0 5px #000; }
        .defeated-label { left:1px; right:1px; bottom:2px; padding:1px 0; border-radius:3px; background:rgba(20,20,20,.80); color:#fff;
          font:700 7px/1 sans-serif; letter-spacing:.15px; text-align:center; }
        .panel[data-defeated-style="cross"] .hero-defeated .defeated-cross { display:flex; }
        .panel[data-defeated-style="label"] .hero-defeated .defeated-label { display:block; }
        .titan-past-head { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:5px; margin-bottom:5px; }
        .titan-past-head .label { margin:0; }
        .titan-past-count { margin-left:auto; color:#bdbdbd; font-size:.90em; white-space:nowrap; }
        .titan-past-card { margin-top:7px; padding:7px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.13); border-radius:8px; background:rgba(255,255,255,.028); }
        .titan-past-card:first-of-type { margin-top:4px; }
        .titan-battle-head { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; margin-bottom:5px; min-width:0; }
        .titan-battle-date { font-weight:800; white-space:nowrap; }
        .titan-battle-place { min-width:0; flex:1; color:#c8c8c8; white-space:nowrap; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; }
        .titan-battle-result { flex:0 0 auto; padding:2px 5px; border-radius:4px; background:rgba(255,255,255,.09); font-weight:800; font-size:.84em; }
        .titan-side { margin-top:5px; padding:6px; border-radius:6px; background:rgba(255,255,255,.03); border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08); }
        .titan-side.match { border-color:rgba(227,182,95,.72); background:rgba(227,182,95,.07); }
        .titan-side-head { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; margin-bottom:4px; }
        .titan-side-title { font-weight:800; }
        .titan-side-player { min-width:0; flex:1; color:#c8c8c8; white-space:nowrap; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; }
        .titan-match-badge { flex:0 0 auto; padding:1px 5px; border-radius:4px; background:rgba(227,182,95,.20); color:#f3ce85; font-weight:900; font-size:.78em; }
        .titan-side .icons { min-height:calc(46px * var(--hwct-icon-scale, 1)); gap:calc(5px * var(--hwct-icon-scale, 1)); }
        .titan-side .iconbox.hero { width:calc(46px * var(--hwct-icon-scale, 1)); height:calc(46px * var(--hwct-icon-scale, 1)); }
        .titan-past-totems { margin-top:4px; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:3px; }
        .titan-past-totem { padding:4px 6px; border-radius:5px; background:rgba(0,0,0,.14); font-size:.90em; line-height:1.35; overflow-wrap:anywhere; }
        .titan-past-totem strong { color:#fff; }
        .titan-buff { margin-top:5px; padding:5px 6px; border-radius:5px; background:rgba(111,164,235,.08); color:#dceaff; font-size:.90em; overflow-wrap:anywhere; }
        .loading, .empty, .error { padding:10px 6px; color:#bdbdbd; }
        .error { color:#ffb6b6; }
        .resize-handle { position:absolute; z-index:20; touch-action:none; }
        .resize-n, .resize-s { left:12px; right:12px; height:7px; }
        .resize-n { top:-3px; cursor:ns-resize; } .resize-s { bottom:-3px; cursor:ns-resize; }
        .resize-e, .resize-w { top:12px; bottom:12px; width:7px; }
        .resize-e { right:-3px; cursor:ew-resize; } .resize-w { left:-3px; cursor:ew-resize; }
        .resize-ne, .resize-nw, .resize-se, .resize-sw { width:13px; height:13px; }
        .resize-ne { right:-4px; top:-4px; cursor:nesw-resize; } .resize-nw { left:-4px; top:-4px; cursor:nwse-resize; }
        .resize-se { right:-4px; bottom:-4px; cursor:nwse-resize; } .resize-sw { left:-4px; bottom:-4px; cursor:nesw-resize; }
      </style>
      <div id="panel" class="panel">
        <div id="head" class="panel-header">
          <span id="ref-title" class="header-title"><span class="tool-mark">⚔</span><span id="ref-title-text">${t('defenseReference')} <span class="version">· v${VERSION}</span></span></span>
          <div class="header-actions">
            <a id="ref-update" class="head-action update-action" href="${GREASY_FORK_PAGE_URL}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" hidden><span class="update-action-label">${t('updateShort')}</span><span class="update-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><span id="ref-update-tip" class="update-tip"></span></a>
            <a id="ref-greasyfork" class="head-action utility-action globe" href="${GREASY_FORK_PAGE_URL}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" title="${greasyForkLinkTitle()}" aria-label="${t('greasyForkPage')}">🌐</a>
            <button id="ref-settings" class="head-action utility-action" type="button" title="${t('settings')}">⚙</button>
            <button id="ref-minimize" class="head-action minimize-action" type="button" title="${t('minimize')}" aria-label="${t('minimize')}"><span class="minimize-bar" aria-hidden="true"></span></button>
            <div id="ref-settings-popover" class="settings" hidden>
              <div class="settings-title">${t('fontSize')}</div>
              <div class="font-controls"><button id="ref-font-minus" type="button">−</button><div id="ref-font-value" class="font-value"></div><button id="ref-font-plus" type="button">+</button></div>
              <button id="ref-reset-sizes" class="settings-reset" type="button">${t('resetSizes')}</button>
              <button id="ref-font-save" class="settings-save" type="button">${t('close')}</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div id="target" class="target"></div>
        <div id="content"><div class="loading">${t('loadingReference')}</div></div>
      </div>
      <div id="info-tip-layer" class="info-tip-layer"></div>`;

    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    syncNativeUiOcclusionVisibility();

    const panel = shadow.getElementById('panel');
    const head = shadow.getElementById('head');
    const titleLine = shadow.getElementById('ref-title');
    const titleText = shadow.getElementById('ref-title-text');
    const targetLine = shadow.getElementById('target');
    const content = shadow.getElementById('content');
    const infoTipLayer = shadow.getElementById('info-tip-layer');
    const settingsButton = shadow.getElementById('ref-settings');
    const settingsPopover = shadow.getElementById('ref-settings-popover');
    const fontMinus = shadow.getElementById('ref-font-minus');
    const fontPlus = shadow.getElementById('ref-font-plus');
    const fontSave = shadow.getElementById('ref-font-save');
    const fontValue = shadow.getElementById('ref-font-value');
    const resetSizesButton = shadow.getElementById('ref-reset-sizes');
    const refUpdate = shadow.getElementById('ref-update');
    const refUpdateTip = shadow.getElementById('ref-update-tip');
    const referenceSettingsController = createTransientSettingsPopover(settingsPopover, settingsButton);
    panel.dataset.defeatedStyle = defeatedDisplayStyle;
    let referenceUserMoved =
      (Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelOffsetXH) && Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelOffsetYH)) ||
      (Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelX) && Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelY));
    let legacyReferencePositionPending =
      !Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelOffsetXH) &&
      !Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelOffsetYH) &&
      Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelX) &&
      Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelY);
    let referenceVisualScale = 1;
    let referenceFontSize = clamp(Math.round(Number(uiState.fontSize) || UI_FONT_DEFAULT), UI_FONT_MIN, UI_FONT_MAX);
    let referenceResizeActive = false;
    let currentTargetKind = null;
    let currentReferenceType = 'hero';
    let appliedIconScale = 0;
    let renderSequence = 0;

    function getReferenceVisualScale(game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry()) {
      return Math.max(.05, game.height / REF_LAYOUT_REFERENCE_GAME_HEIGHT);
    }

    function syncReferenceInfoTipFontSize() {
      host.style.setProperty('--hwct-info-tip-font-size', `${referenceFontSize}px`);
    }

    function applyReferenceVisualScale(game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry()) {
      referenceVisualScale = getReferenceVisualScale(game);
      panel.style.zoom = String(referenceVisualScale);
      syncReferenceInfoTipFontSize();
      return referenceVisualScale;
    }

    function getReferenceTechnicalMinWidth() {
      return getHeaderTechnicalMinWidth(
        head,
        shadow,
        REF_PANEL_TECHNICAL_MIN_WIDTH_FALLBACK,
        referenceVisualScale
      );
    }

    function getReferenceAvailableLogicalWidth() {
      return Math.max(
        1,
        (window.innerWidth - PANEL_VIEWPORT_WIDTH_GUTTER) / Math.max(.01, referenceVisualScale)
      );
    }

    function ensureReferenceHeaderFits() {
      const minWidth = Math.min(getReferenceTechnicalMinWidth(), getReferenceAvailableLogicalWidth());
      const currentWidth = panel.offsetWidth || Number.parseFloat(panel.style.width) || REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH;
      if (currentWidth < minWidth) panel.style.width = `${minWidth.toFixed(3)}px`;
      return Math.max(currentWidth, minWidth);
    }

    applyReferenceVisualScale();
    {
