      const preferred = Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelWidth) ? uiState.refPanelWidth : REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH;
      const minWidth = Math.min(getReferenceTechnicalMinWidth(), getReferenceAvailableLogicalWidth());
      const width = Math.min(Math.max(minWidth, preferred), getReferenceAvailableLogicalWidth());
      panel.style.width = `${width.toFixed(3)}px`;
    }
    if (Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelHeight)) {
      const maxLogicalHeight = window.innerHeight / Math.max(.01, referenceVisualScale);
      panel.style.height = `${Math.min(uiState.refPanelHeight, Math.max(PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT, maxLogicalHeight)).toFixed(3)}px`;
    }

    // Usable inline space for one ordinary icon row, taken from the live section
    // geometry so the panel border, the section padding and any vertical scrollbar
    // are excluded. .icons / .current-row keep contain:inline-size, so this
    // measurement never depends on the icon scale it feeds.
    function measureReferenceUsableRowWidth(panelWidth) {
      const section = content.querySelector('.section');
      if (section) {
        const style = getComputedStyle(section);
        const inner = section.clientWidth
          - (Number.parseFloat(style.paddingLeft) || 0)
          - (Number.parseFloat(style.paddingRight) || 0);
        if (inner > 0) return inner;
      }
      const contentWidth = content.clientWidth;
      if (contentWidth > 0) return Math.max(1, contentWidth - REF_SECTION_PADDING_X_FALLBACK);
      const numeric = Number(panelWidth);
      if (!Number.isFinite(numeric) || numeric <= 0) return 0;
      return Math.max(1, numeric - REF_PANEL_BORDER_X_FALLBACK - REF_SECTION_PADDING_X_FALLBACK);
    }

    // Mark a bounded icon row as eligible to drive the shared scale. Only rows whose
    // item count is bounded by the game state are marked here; `content.innerHTML = ''`
    // at the start of each render retires the previous target's marks, so a row is
    // read as a driver only while it is actually rendered for the current target.
    function markReferenceScaleRow(row, iconCount) {
      if (!row) return;
      const naturalWidth = getBoundedIconRowNaturalWidth(iconCount);
      if (naturalWidth > 0) row.setAttribute(REF_SCALE_ROW_ATTRIBUTE, String(naturalWidth));
      else row.removeAttribute(REF_SCALE_ROW_ATTRIBUTE);
    }

    // One inline empty-state message for the whole Reference. A confirmed absence is
    // ordinary text next to the icon row it replaces, never a bolder or differently
    // spaced line of its own, so Hero and Titan cannot drift apart again.
    function createInlineEmptyMessage(message, title = message) {
      const text = document.createElement('div');
      text.className = 'current-inline-message empty';
      text.textContent = message;
      text.title = title;
      return text;
    }

    function readRenderedReferenceScaleRowWidths() {
      return [...content.querySelectorAll(`[${REF_SCALE_ROW_ATTRIBUTE}]`)]
        .map(row => Number(row.getAttribute(REF_SCALE_ROW_ATTRIBUTE)));
    }

    function updateReferenceIconScale(
      width = panel.offsetWidth || Number.parseFloat(panel.style.width) || REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH,
      { allowGrow = true } = {}
    ) {
      // Narrowing the panel removes the unused right-side space by following the
      // usable row width continuously. No full-scale plateau, no upper cap, and
      // below the 0.50 floor the content overflows horizontally instead of
      // shrinking further or wrapping.
      const naturalRowWidth = getReferenceScaleDriverNaturalWidth(
        readRenderedReferenceScaleRowWidths(),
        currentReferenceType
      );
      const scale = getReferenceIconScale(measureReferenceUsableRowWidth(width), naturalRowWidth);
      if (!allowGrow && scale > appliedIconScale) return;
      if (Math.abs(scale - appliedIconScale) < 0.001) return;
      appliedIconScale = scale;
      host.style.setProperty('--hwct-icon-scale', String(scale));
      host.style.setProperty('--hwct-totem-icon-scale', String(scale));
    }
    updateReferenceIconScale();

    // A vertical scrollbar appearing with newly rendered content also changes the
    // usable row width. Give that space back when it happens, but let only the
    // explicit resize / render paths grow the scale again so the scrollbar and the
    // scale cannot chase each other.
    const contentResizeObserver = typeof ResizeObserver === 'function'
      ? new ResizeObserver(() => updateReferenceIconScale(undefined, { allowGrow: false }))
      : null;
    contentResizeObserver?.observe(content);

    function setFontSize(size) {
      const normalized = clamp(Math.round(Number(size) || UI_FONT_DEFAULT), UI_FONT_MIN, UI_FONT_MAX);
      referenceFontSize = normalized;
      host.style.setProperty('--hwct-font-size', `${normalized}px`);
      syncReferenceInfoTipFontSize();
      fontValue.textContent = `${normalized}px`;
      const fittedWidth = ensureReferenceHeaderFits();
      updateReferenceIconScale(fittedWidth);
      if (host.style.display !== 'none') requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        clampReferenceToViewport();
        referenceSettingsController.reposition();
      });
    }

    function positionInfoTip(info, tip) {
      if (!info || !tip) return;
      const anchor = info.getBoundingClientRect();
      const margin = 8;
      const gap = 6;
      const wasDisplay = tip.style.display;
      const wasVisibility = tip.style.visibility;
      tip.style.visibility = 'hidden';
      tip.style.display = 'block';
      tip.style.left = `${margin}px`;
      tip.style.top = `${margin}px`;
      const tipRect = tip.getBoundingClientRect();
      let left = anchor.left;
      let top = anchor.bottom + gap;
      if (top + tipRect.height > window.innerHeight - margin) {
        top = anchor.top - tipRect.height - gap;
      }
      const maxLeft = Math.max(margin, window.innerWidth - tipRect.width - margin);
      const maxTop = Math.max(margin, window.innerHeight - tipRect.height - margin);
      left = clamp(left, margin, maxLeft);
      top = clamp(top, margin, maxTop);
      tip.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
      tip.style.top = `${Math.round(top)}px`;
      tip.style.visibility = wasVisibility;
      tip.style.display = wasDisplay;
    }

    function clearInfoTips() {
      infoTipLayer?.replaceChildren();
    }

    function bindInfoTip(info) {
      const tip = info?.querySelector?.('.info-tip');
      if (!tip || !infoTipLayer) return;
      // Move only the long-form custom tip out of the zoomed panel. The native
      // title hovers on Hero/Pet/Titan artwork remain browser-managed.
      infoTipLayer.appendChild(tip);
      let closeTimer = null;
      const cancelClose = () => {
        if (closeTimer != null) {
          clearTimeout(closeTimer);
          closeTimer = null;
        }
      };
      const open = () => {
        cancelClose();
        info.classList.add('tip-open');
        tip.classList.add('tip-open');
        requestAnimationFrame(() => positionInfoTip(info, tip));
      };
      const scheduleClose = () => {
        cancelClose();
        closeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          closeTimer = null;
          if (!info.matches(':focus-within') && !tip.matches(':hover')) {
            info.classList.remove('tip-open');
            tip.classList.remove('tip-open');
          }
        }, 250);
      };
      info.addEventListener('pointerenter', open);
      info.addEventListener('pointerleave', scheduleClose);
      info.addEventListener('focus', open);
      info.addEventListener('blur', scheduleClose);
      tip.addEventListener('pointerenter', open);
      tip.addEventListener('pointerleave', scheduleClose);
    }

    function positionUpdateTip(indicator, tip) {
      if (!indicator || !tip || indicator.hidden) return;
      const anchorRect = indicator.getBoundingClientRect();
      const margin = 8;
      const gap = 6;
      const previousDisplay = tip.style.display;
      const previousVisibility = tip.style.visibility;
      tip.style.visibility = 'hidden';
      tip.style.display = 'block';
      const tipRect = tip.getBoundingClientRect();
      let left = anchorRect.left;
      let top = anchorRect.bottom + gap;
      if (top + tipRect.height > window.innerHeight - margin) top = anchorRect.top - tipRect.height - gap;
      left = clamp(left, margin, Math.max(margin, window.innerWidth - tipRect.width - margin));
      top = clamp(top, margin, Math.max(margin, window.innerHeight - tipRect.height - margin));
      tip.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
      tip.style.top = `${Math.round(top)}px`;
      tip.style.visibility = previousVisibility;
      tip.style.display = previousDisplay;
    }

    const unsubscribeRefUpdate = subscribeUpdateAvailability(state => {
      const show = Boolean(state.updateAvailable && state.latestVersion);
      refUpdate.hidden = !show;
      refUpdate.setAttribute('aria-label', show ? t('updateAvailable') : '');
      refUpdateTip.textContent = show ? updateTooltipText(state.latestVersion) : '';
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const fittedWidth = ensureReferenceHeaderFits();
        updateReferenceIconScale(fittedWidth);
        clampReferenceToViewport();
        referenceSettingsController.reposition();
      });
      if (show && (refUpdate.matches(':hover') || refUpdate.matches(':focus'))) {
        requestAnimationFrame(() => positionUpdateTip(refUpdate, refUpdateTip));
      }
    });
    for (const eventName of ['pointerenter', 'focus']) {
      refUpdate.addEventListener(eventName, () => {
        if (refUpdate.hidden) return;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => positionUpdateTip(refUpdate, refUpdateTip));
      });
    }

    function setReferenceTarget(target, currentTermText = '') {
      currentTargetKind = target?.kind ?? null;
      currentReferenceType = target?.referenceType === 'titan' ? 'titan' : 'hero';
      titleText.childNodes[0].nodeValue = `${t('defenseReference')} `;
      targetLine.innerHTML = '';
      const primary = document.createElement('div');
      primary.className = 'target-primary';
      const building = target?.building || t('defense');
      const slot = target?.slotNumber != null ? ` #${target.slotNumber}` : '';
      primary.textContent = `${building}${slot}`;
      const secondary = document.createElement('div');
      secondary.className = 'target-secondary';
      secondary.textContent = target?.playerName || '—';
      targetLine.append(primary, secondary);
      // The current War Log term next to the target/location, so the current term
      // reading of Current Defense is distinguishable at a glance from the older term a
      // Past Setup record comes from. The resolver supplies the finished text: GW labels
      // a date, CoW states the term identity, and an unresolved term supplies nothing.
      const termText = String(currentTermText ?? '').trim();
      if (termText) {
        const term = document.createElement('div');
        term.className = 'target-term';
        term.textContent = termText;
        targetLine.appendChild(term);
      }
    }
    setFontSize(mainPanelController?.getFontSize?.() ?? uiState.fontSize);
    settingsButton.addEventListener('click', event => {
      event.stopPropagation();
      referenceSettingsController.toggle();
    });
    fontMinus.addEventListener('click', () => mainPanelController?.setFontSize?.((mainPanelController?.getFontSize?.() ?? UI_FONT_DEFAULT) - 1));
    fontPlus.addEventListener('click', () => mainPanelController?.setFontSize?.((mainPanelController?.getFontSize?.() ?? UI_FONT_DEFAULT) + 1));
    fontSave.addEventListener('click', () => { referenceSettingsController.close(); });
    resetSizesButton?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      resetUiSizes();
      referenceSettingsController.close();
    });

    function resetReferenceSize() {
      // Keep null in storage so this remains a default, not a user override.
      // The live controller still applies the tuned default dimensions immediately.
      uiState.refPanelWidth = REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH;
      uiState.refPanelHeight = REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_HEIGHT;
      saveUiState({
        refPanelWidth: null,
        refPanelHeight: null,
      });

      const availableLogicalWidth = getReferenceAvailableLogicalWidth();
      const minWidth = Math.min(getReferenceTechnicalMinWidth(), availableLogicalWidth);
      const width = Math.min(Math.max(minWidth, REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH), availableLogicalWidth);
      const maxLogicalHeight = Math.max(1, window.innerHeight / Math.max(.01, referenceVisualScale));
      const height = Math.min(
        Math.max(PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT, REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_HEIGHT),
        maxLogicalHeight
      );
      panel.style.width = `${width.toFixed(3)}px`;
      panel.style.height = `${height.toFixed(3)}px`;
      updateReferenceIconScale(width);

      if (host.style.display !== 'none') requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        clampReferenceToViewport();
        if (referenceUserMoved) {
          if (!placeSavedReferencePosition()) clampReferenceToViewport();
        } else {
          placeReferenceNearGamePopup();
        }
        referenceSettingsController.reposition();
      });
    }

    function migrateLegacyReferencePosition() {
      if (!legacyReferencePositionPending) return;
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const layoutWidth = panel.offsetWidth || REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH;
      const layoutHeight = panel.offsetHeight || 180;
      const oldMaxX = Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - layoutWidth);
      const oldMaxY = Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - layoutHeight);
      const oldLeft = oldMaxX * uiState.refPanelX;
      const oldTop = oldMaxY * uiState.refPanelY;
      uiState.refPanelOffsetXH = (oldLeft + layoutWidth / 2 - game.centerX) / game.height;
      uiState.refPanelOffsetYH = (oldTop + layoutHeight / 2 - game.centerY) / game.height;
      legacyReferencePositionPending = false;
      saveUiState({
        refPanelOffsetXH:uiState.refPanelOffsetXH,
        refPanelOffsetYH:uiState.refPanelOffsetYH,
      });
    }

    function saveReferencePosition() {
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
      uiState.refPanelOffsetXH =
        (rect.left + rect.width / 2 - game.centerX) / game.height;
      uiState.refPanelOffsetYH =
        (rect.top + rect.height / 2 - game.centerY) / game.height;
      saveUiState({
        refPanelOffsetXH:uiState.refPanelOffsetXH,
        refPanelOffsetYH:uiState.refPanelOffsetYH,
      });
    }

    function placeSavedReferencePosition() {
      migrateLegacyReferencePosition();
      if (!Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelOffsetXH) || !Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelOffsetYH)) return false;
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
      const centerX = game.centerX + uiState.refPanelOffsetXH * game.height;
      const centerY = game.centerY + uiState.refPanelOffsetYH * game.height;
      const left = clamp(centerX - rect.width / 2, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width));
      const top = clamp(centerY - rect.height / 2, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - rect.height));
      host.style.left = `${left.toFixed(3)}px`;
      host.style.top = `${top.toFixed(3)}px`;
      return true;
    }

    function placeReferenceNearGamePopup() {
      if (referenceUserMoved || host.style.display === 'none') return;
      const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const estimatedPopupHalfWidth = 390 * referenceVisualScale;
      const gap = 12 * referenceVisualScale;
      const rightCandidate = game.centerX + estimatedPopupHalfWidth + gap;
      const leftCandidate = game.centerX - estimatedPopupHalfWidth - gap - rect.width;
      let left;
      if (rightCandidate + rect.width <= window.innerWidth) left = rightCandidate;
      else if (leftCandidate >= 0) left = leftCandidate;
      else left = Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width - 8);
      left = clamp(left, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width));
      const top = clamp(
        game.centerY - rect.height / 2,
        0,
        Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - rect.height)
      );
      host.style.left = `${left.toFixed(3)}px`;
      host.style.top = `${top.toFixed(3)}px`;
    }

    function clampReferenceToViewport() {
      if (host.style.display === 'none') return;
      applyReferenceVisualScale();
      const availableLogicalWidth = getReferenceAvailableLogicalWidth();
      const minWidth = Math.min(getReferenceTechnicalMinWidth(), availableLogicalWidth);
      const preferredWidth = Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelWidth) ? uiState.refPanelWidth : REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH;
      const width = Math.min(Math.max(minWidth, preferredWidth), availableLogicalWidth);
      if (Math.abs((panel.offsetWidth || 0) - width) > 0.5) panel.style.width = `${width.toFixed(3)}px`;

      const maxLogicalHeight = Math.max(1, window.innerHeight / Math.max(.01, referenceVisualScale));
      if (Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelHeight)) {
        const height = Math.min(
          Math.max(PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT, uiState.refPanelHeight),
          Math.max(1, maxLogicalHeight)
        );
        panel.style.height = `${height.toFixed(3)}px`;
      } else {
        panel.style.height = '';
        const naturalHeight = panel.offsetHeight || 180;
        if (naturalHeight > maxLogicalHeight) panel.style.height = `${maxLogicalHeight.toFixed(3)}px`;
      }

      let rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
      updateReferenceIconScale(panel.offsetWidth || width);
      const rawLeft = Number.parseFloat(host.style.left);
      const rawTop = Number.parseFloat(host.style.top);
      const left = clamp(Number.isFinite(rawLeft) ? rawLeft : rect.left || 0, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width));
      const top = clamp(Number.isFinite(rawTop) ? rawTop : rect.top || 0, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - rect.height));
      host.style.left = `${left.toFixed(3)}px`;
      host.style.top = `${top.toFixed(3)}px`;
    }

    const removeReferenceResizeHandles = installEightWayResize({
      host,
      panel,
      shadow,
      minWidth:getReferenceTechnicalMinWidth,
      minHeight:PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT,
      getVisualScale:() => referenceVisualScale,
      onResizeStart:() => { referenceResizeActive = true; },
      onResize:rect => updateReferenceIconScale(rect.layoutWidth),
      onResizeEnd:rect => {
        referenceResizeActive = false;
        updateReferenceIconScale(rect.layoutWidth);
        uiState.refPanelWidth = rect.layoutWidth;
        uiState.refPanelHeight = rect.layoutHeight;
        saveUiState({
          refPanelWidth:uiState.refPanelWidth,
          refPanelHeight:uiState.refPanelHeight,
        });
        if (referenceUserMoved) saveReferencePosition();
        else requestAnimationFrame(placeReferenceNearGamePopup);
      },
    });

    let observedReferenceGameCanvas = null;
    const referenceGameResizeObserver = typeof ResizeObserver === 'function'
      ? new ResizeObserver(() => handleReferenceGeometryChange())
      : null;

    function syncReferenceGameObserverTarget() {
      if (!referenceGameResizeObserver) return;
      const nextCanvas = getLargestHeroWarsCanvasEntry()?.canvas ?? null;
      if (nextCanvas === observedReferenceGameCanvas) return;
      if (observedReferenceGameCanvas) referenceGameResizeObserver.unobserve(observedReferenceGameCanvas);
      observedReferenceGameCanvas = nextCanvas;
      if (observedReferenceGameCanvas) referenceGameResizeObserver.observe(observedReferenceGameCanvas);
    }

    function handleReferenceGeometryChange() {
      if (drag || referenceResizeActive) return;
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (drag || referenceResizeActive || host.style.display === 'none') return;
        syncReferenceGameObserverTarget();
        clampReferenceToViewport();
        if (referenceUserMoved) placeSavedReferencePosition();
        else placeReferenceNearGamePopup();
        referenceSettingsController.reposition();
      });
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', handleReferenceGeometryChange);
    syncReferenceGameObserverTarget();
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      clampReferenceToViewport();
      if (referenceUserMoved) {
        if (!placeSavedReferencePosition()) placeReferenceNearGamePopup();
      } else {
        placeReferenceNearGamePopup();
      }
    });

    let drag = null;
    head.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
      if (event.target.closest?.('.header-actions, .settings') || event.button !== 0) return;
      const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
      drag = { id: event.pointerId, dx: event.clientX - rect.left, dy: event.clientY - rect.top };
      head.classList.add('dragging');
      head.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
      event.preventDefault();
    });
    head.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
      if (!drag || event.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
      const rect = panel.getBoundingClientRect();
      const left = clamp(event.clientX - drag.dx, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width));
      const top = clamp(event.clientY - drag.dy, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - rect.height));
      host.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
      host.style.top = `${Math.round(top)}px`;
    });
    const endDrag = event => {
      if (!drag || event.pointerId !== drag.id) return;
      drag = null;
      head.classList.remove('dragging');
      referenceUserMoved = true;
      saveReferencePosition();
    };
    head.addEventListener('pointerup', endDrag);
    head.addEventListener('pointercancel', endDrag);

    shadow.getElementById('ref-minimize').addEventListener('click', event => {
      event.stopPropagation();
      const originRect = event.currentTarget?.getBoundingClientRect?.()
        || shadow.getElementById('ref-minimize')?.getBoundingClientRect?.()
        || null;
      minimizePatronReference(originRect);
    });

    // `idOnlyText` is the family's existing ID-only hover wording. The box puts the
    // resolved game name in front of it, so the hover reads `Name (ID)` whenever both
    // halves are known, and `state` follows the identity rather than replacing it. The
    // same text is used whether the artwork rendered or the placeholder did.
    async function addUnitBox(parent, id, sizeClass, idOnlyText, { name = '', state = '', frameSpec = null, family = ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait } = {}) {
      const numericId = Number(id);
      const identity = referenceIdentityWithState(
        referenceIdentityText(name || getUnitDisplayName(numericId), numericId, idOnlyText),
        state,
      );
      const box = document.createElement('div');
      box.className = `iconbox${sizeClass ? ` ${sizeClass}` : ''}`;
      box.title = identity || String(id ?? '');
      // With a native frame the portrait is inset to the game's own frame/portrait
      // ratio and keeps its square artwork, exactly as the Titan composition does.
      // Without one the icon is unchanged, so an unresolved frame costs nothing.
      const spriteSize = frameSpec ? UNIT_PORTRAIT_DISPLAY_SIZE : 46;
      const spriteClass = frameSpec
        ? 'unit-portrait-sprite'
        : (sizeClass === 'hero' ? 'hero-sprite' : 'unit-sprite');
      const sprite = Number.isFinite(numericId) && numericId > 0
        ? await createUnitSprite(numericId, spriteSize, spriteClass, identity, family)
        : null;

      if (frameSpec) {
        box.classList.add('unit-native');
        const background = document.createElement('span');
        background.className = 'unit-native-background';
        background.style.backgroundImage = `url("${frameSpec.backgroundDataUrl}")`;
        box.appendChild(background);
      }

      if (sprite) box.appendChild(sprite);
      else {
        const p = document.createElement('span');
        p.className = 'placeholder known-fallback';
        p.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(name || getUnitDisplayName(numericId), numericId);
        // Keep the fixed icon footprint and overlays; the hover already carries the
        // same identity it would have shown with artwork.
        box.appendChild(p);
      }

      if (frameSpec) {
        const frame = document.createElement('span');
        frame.className = 'unit-native-frame';
        frame.style.backgroundImage = `url("${frameSpec.frameDataUrl}")`;
        box.appendChild(frame);
      }

      parent.appendChild(box);
      return box;
    }

    async function addTitanUnitBox(parent, id, idOnlyText, { name = '', state = '' } = {}) {
      const numericId = Number(id);
      const identity = referenceIdentityWithState(
        referenceIdentityText(name || getUnitDisplayName(numericId), numericId, idOnlyText),
        state,
      );
      const box = document.createElement('div');
      box.className = 'iconbox hero';
      box.title = identity || String(id ?? '');
      const frameSpec = Number.isFinite(numericId) && numericId > 0
        ? await getTitanFrameSpriteSpec(numericId)
        : null;
      const portraitSize = frameSpec ? TITAN_PORTRAIT_DISPLAY_SIZE : TITAN_FRAME_DISPLAY_SIZE;
      const sprite = Number.isFinite(numericId) && numericId > 0
        ? await createUnitSprite(
          numericId,
          portraitSize,
          frameSpec ? 'titan-portrait-sprite' : 'hero-sprite',
          identity,
          ARTWORK_FAMILY.titanPortrait
        )
        : null;

      if (frameSpec) {
        box.classList.add('titan-native');
        if (frameSpec.backgroundDataUrl) {
          const background = document.createElement('span');
          background.className = 'titan-native-background';
          background.style.backgroundImage = `url("${frameSpec.backgroundDataUrl}")`;
          box.appendChild(background);
        }
      }

      if (sprite) {
        box.appendChild(sprite);
      } else {
        const placeholder = document.createElement('span');
        placeholder.className = 'placeholder known-fallback';
        placeholder.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(name || getUnitDisplayName(numericId), numericId);
        box.appendChild(placeholder);
      }

      if (frameSpec) {
        const frame = document.createElement('span');
        frame.className = 'titan-native-frame';
        frame.style.backgroundImage = `url("${frameSpec.frameDataUrl}")`;
        box.appendChild(frame);
      }

      parent.appendChild(box);
      return box;
    }

    return {
      host,
      savePosition() {
        referenceUserMoved = true;
        saveReferencePosition();
      },
      setLoading(target) {
        Promise.resolve(this.render(target, null, { loading: true }))
          .catch(error => warn('REFERENCE_LOADING_RENDER_FAILED', error));
      },
      setError(message, target = null) {
        if (!target) {
          clearInfoTips();
          content.innerHTML = '';
          const row = document.createElement('div');
          row.className = 'error';
          row.textContent = message || t('referenceCouldNotBeLoaded');
          content.appendChild(row);
          return;
        }
        Promise.resolve(this.render(target, null, { errorMessage: message || t('referenceCouldNotBeLoaded') }))
          .catch(error => warn('REFERENCE_ERROR_RENDER_FAILED', error));
      },
      async render(target, result, { errorMessage = '', loading = false } = {}) {
        const renderId = ++renderSequence;
        // The term text comes from the live term captured on the target, so it is shown
        // from the first render and never waits for a history read.
        setReferenceTarget(target, target?.currentTerm?.headerText ?? '');

        if (target?.referenceType === 'titan') {
          const defense = target.currentTitanDefense ?? { titans: [], totems: [] };
          await Promise.allSettled((defense.titans ?? []).map(row => getUnitIconSpriteSpec(row.id, ARTWORK_FAMILY.titanPortrait)));
          if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected) return;
          clearInfoTips();
          content.innerHTML = '';

          const titanSection = document.createElement('div');
          titanSection.className = 'section';
          const titanLabel = document.createElement('div');
          titanLabel.className = 'label';
          titanLabel.textContent = t('currentDefense');
          titanSection.appendChild(titanLabel);
          const titanIcons = document.createElement('div');
          titanIcons.className = 'icons';
          for (const titan of defense.titans ?? []) {
            const state = target?.titanStateById instanceof Map
              ? target.titanStateById.get(Number(titan.id))
              : null;
            const defeated = state?.alive === false;
            const box = await addTitanUnitBox(
              titanIcons,
              titan.id,
              `Titan ${titan.id}`,
              { name: titan.name, state: defeated ? t('defeatedUpper') : '' },
            );
            if (defeated) {
              box.classList.add('hero-defeated');
              const cross = document.createElement('span');
              cross.className = 'defeated-cross';
              cross.textContent = '×';
              const defeatedLabel = document.createElement('span');
              defeatedLabel.className = 'defeated-label';
              defeatedLabel.textContent = t('defeatedUpper');
              box.append(cross, defeatedLabel);
            }
          }
          titanSection.appendChild(titanIcons);
          markReferenceScaleRow(titanIcons, titanIcons.childElementCount);
          content.appendChild(titanSection);

          // The Totem / Fusion Skill group is current defense state of its own, so it
          // gets its own titled section the way Hero gives War Flag & Patterns one
          // directly after Current Defense.
          const totemSection = document.createElement('div');
          totemSection.className = 'section';
          const totemSectionLabel = document.createElement('div');
          totemSectionLabel.className = 'label';
          totemSectionLabel.textContent = t('totemFusionSkills');
          totemSection.appendChild(totemSectionLabel);

          const totems = defense.totems ?? [];
          const deferredTitanIconTasks = [];
          if (!totems.length) {
            // A resolved defense with no Totem is the same kind of confirmed absence as
            // a missing War Flag, so it uses the Hero inline empty-state message in the
            // ordinary current row rather than a Totem-specific line.
            const emptyRow = document.createElement('div');
            emptyRow.className = 'current-row';
            emptyRow.appendChild(createInlineEmptyMessage(t('noTotem')));
            totemSection.appendChild(emptyRow);
          } else {
            for (const totem of totems) {
              const card = document.createElement('div');
              card.className = 'totem-card';

              const row = document.createElement('div');
              row.className = 'totem-current-row';

              // Totem keeps its current metadata to the right of the icon, and names its
              // item type under the icon the way the Hero War Flag cell already does.
              const totemGroup = document.createElement('div');
              totemGroup.className = 'totem-main-group';
              const totemCell = document.createElement('div');
              totemCell.className = 'totem-icon-cell';
              const totemIcon = document.createElement('div');
              totemIcon.className = 'totem-icon';
              const totemName = referenceIdentityText(totem.name, totem.id, `Totem ${totem.id}`);
              totemIcon.title = totemName;
              totemIcon.setAttribute('aria-label', totemName);
              const totemTypeLabel = document.createElement('div');
              totemTypeLabel.className = 'totem-icon-label';
              totemTypeLabel.textContent = t('totem');
              totemCell.append(totemIcon, totemTypeLabel);
              const totemMeta = document.createElement('div');
              totemMeta.className = 'totem-meta-side';
              if (totem.level != null) {
                const levelLine = document.createElement('div');
                levelLine.textContent = t('levelShort', { level: totem.level });
                totemMeta.appendChild(levelLine);
              }
              if (totem.star != null) {
                const starLine = document.createElement('div');
                starLine.textContent = `★${totem.star}`;
                totemMeta.appendChild(starLine);
              }
              totemGroup.append(totemCell, totemMeta);
              row.appendChild(totemGroup);

              deferredTitanIconTasks.push(async () => {
                const totemSpec = await getTitanTotemIconSpec(totem);
                if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected || !card.isConnected) return;
                const totemSprite = totemSpec
                  ? await createSpriteFromSpec(totemSpec, 46, 'totem-native-sprite', totemName, {
                    family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemBody,
                    subject: titanTotemIconSubject(totem),
                  })
                  : null;
                if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected || !card.isConnected) return;
                // Issue #69: the approved fallback is applied first, so the icon is
                // never briefly frameless while the native frame resolves - resolving
                // it can mean warming a cold artifact atlas. The native frame then
                // replaces it if and when it arrives.
                const framePromise = getTitanTotemNativeFrameSpec(totem.color);
                applyTitanTierFrame(totemIcon, getTitanTotemTierByLevel(totem.level), 46);
                if (totemSprite) {
                  totemIcon.replaceChildren(totemSprite);
                } else {
                  const fallback = document.createElement('span');
                  fallback.className = 'totem-icon-fallback known-fallback';
                  fallback.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(totem.name, totem.id);
                  totemIcon.replaceChildren(fallback);
                }

                // The Totem body is already painted, so the atlas warm-up never delays
                // artwork, Lv / star or the Fusion Skill cells.
                const totemFrameSpec = await framePromise;
                if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected || !card.isConnected) return;
                if (totemFrameSpec) {
                  // Native frame resolved: drop the level-derived frame so the icon
                  // never shows two, then put the native one above the body, exactly
                  // as the Titan and Hero / Pet compositions order theirs.
                  clearTitanTierFrame(totemIcon);
                  totemIcon.classList.add('totem-native-framed');
                  const totemFrame = document.createElement('span');
                  totemFrame.className = 'totem-native-frame';
                  totemFrame.style.backgroundImage = `url("${totemFrameSpec.frameDataUrl}")`;
                  totemIcon.appendChild(totemFrame);
                }
              });

              // Fusion Skills follow Hero Current's icon + value-under-icon pattern.
              for (const skill of (totem.skills ?? []).slice(0, 2)) {
                const skillCell = document.createElement('div');
                skillCell.className = 'totem-skill-cell';
                const skillIcon = document.createElement('div');
                skillIcon.className = 'totem-skill-icon';
                const rankMeta = document.createElement('div');
                rankMeta.className = 'totem-skill-rank';

                // A confirmed absent Fusion Skill keeps the slot and states the absence
                // with the shared compact empty label, instead of leaving a blank box
                // that looks like a rendering failure. Only an explicitly empty row
                // reaches this branch; unresolved skill data never does.
                if (skill?.state === 'empty') {
                  const emptyLabel = t('emptyPatternShort');
                  skillIcon.classList.add('empty-skill-slot');
                  skillIcon.title = emptyLabel;
                  skillIcon.setAttribute('aria-label', emptyLabel);
                  const emptyText = document.createElement('span');
                  emptyText.className = 'placeholder';
                  emptyText.textContent = emptyLabel;
                  skillIcon.appendChild(emptyText);
                  rankMeta.textContent = '';
                  skillCell.append(skillIcon, rankMeta);
                  row.appendChild(skillCell);
                  continue;
                }

                const skillName = referenceIdentityText(
                  skill.name,
                  skill.id,
                  skill.id != null ? `#${skill.id}` : '−',
                );
                skillIcon.title = skillName;
                skillIcon.setAttribute('aria-label', skillName);
                rankMeta.textContent = skill.rank != null ? formatTitanRankShort(skill.rank) : '';

                skillCell.append(skillIcon, rankMeta);
                row.appendChild(skillCell);

                deferredTitanIconTasks.push(async () => {
                  const skillSpec = await getTitanSkillIconSpec(skill);
                  if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected || !skillCell.isConnected) return;
                  const skillSprite = skillSpec
                    ? await createSpriteFromSpec(skillSpec, 46, 'totem-skill-native-sprite', skillName, {
                      family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.totemSkill,
                      subject: titanSkillIconSubject(skill),
                    })
                    : null;
                  if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected || !skillCell.isConnected) return;
                  applyTitanTierFrame(skillIcon, getTitanSkillTierByRank(skill.rank), 46);
                  if (skillSprite) {
                    skillIcon.replaceChildren(skillSprite);
                  } else {
                    const fallback = document.createElement('span');
                    fallback.className = 'totem-icon-fallback known-fallback';
                    fallback.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(skill.name, skill.id);
                    skillIcon.replaceChildren(fallback);
                  }
                });
              }

              card.appendChild(row);
              totemSection.appendChild(card);
            }
          }
          content.appendChild(totemSection);
          updateReferenceIconScale(undefined, { allowGrow: false });

          // Let the browser paint the Current reference first. Hydrate all optional
          // images on the next frame; never block Totem name/Lv/★/skill text on icons.
          requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected) return;
            for (const task of deferredTitanIconTasks) {
              Promise.resolve()
                .then(task)
                .catch(error => warn('TITAN_DEFERRED_ICON_RENDER_FAILED', error));
            }
          });

          if (!TITAN_PAST_REFERENCE_ENABLED) {
            updateReferenceIconScale();
            requestAnimationFrame(placeReferenceNearGamePopup);
            return;
          }

          const pastSection = document.createElement('div');
          pastSection.className = 'section';
          const pastHead = document.createElement('div');
          pastHead.className = 'titan-past-head';
          const pastLabel = document.createElement('div');
          pastLabel.className = 'label';
          pastLabel.textContent = t('pastBattles');
          const pastInfo = document.createElement('button');
          pastInfo.type = 'button';
          pastInfo.className = 'info';
          pastInfo.setAttribute('aria-label', t('pastBattlesInfo'));
          pastInfo.textContent = 'ⓘ';
          const pastTip = document.createElement('span');
          pastTip.className = 'info-tip';
          pastTip.textContent = t('pastBattlesTip');
          pastInfo.appendChild(pastTip);
          bindInfoTip(pastInfo);
          pastHead.append(pastLabel, pastInfo);
          const battles = Array.isArray(result?.battles) ? result.battles : [];
          if (!loading && !errorMessage && battles.length) {
            const count = document.createElement('span');
            count.className = 'titan-past-count';
            count.textContent = t('sameTitanMatches', { count: battles.length });
            pastHead.appendChild(count);
          }
          pastSection.appendChild(pastHead);

          const renderPastTotems = (parent, totems) => {
            const rows = Array.isArray(totems) ? totems : [];
            if (!rows.length) return;
            const wrap = document.createElement('div');
            wrap.className = 'titan-past-totems';
            for (const totem of rows) {
              const row = document.createElement('div');
              row.className = 'titan-past-totem';
              const meta = [];
              if (totem.level != null) meta.push(t('levelShort', { level: totem.level }));
              if (totem.star != null) meta.push(`★${totem.star}`);
              // Issue #54 is scoped to Current Defense: the Past Setup one-line summary
              // keeps listing only real skills.
              const skillText = (totem.skills ?? []).filter(skill => skill?.state !== 'empty').map(skill => {
                const typeLabel = skill.type || t('skillShort', { index: skill.index });
                const skillName = skill.name || (skill.id != null ? `#${skill.id}` : '−');
                const rank = skill.rank != null ? formatTitanRankShort(skill.rank) : '';
                return `${typeLabel}: ${skillName}${rank ? ` · ${rank}` : ''}`;
              }).join(' / ');
              const strong = document.createElement('strong');
              strong.textContent = titanSpiritDisplayName(totem.name, totem.element);
              row.appendChild(strong);
              const detail = [...meta, skillText].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
              if (detail) row.appendChild(document.createTextNode(` · ${detail}`));
              wrap.appendChild(row);
            }
            parent.appendChild(wrap);
          };

          const renderPastSide = async (card, battle, sideName) => {
            const side = battle?.[sideName] ?? null;
            if (!side) return;
            const sideBox = document.createElement('div');
            const matched = Array.isArray(battle?.matchedSides) && battle.matchedSides.includes(sideName);
            sideBox.className = `titan-side${matched ? ' match' : ''}`;
            const sideHead = document.createElement('div');
            sideHead.className = 'titan-side-head';
            const sideTitle = document.createElement('span');
            sideTitle.className = 'titan-side-title';
            sideTitle.textContent = sideName === 'attacker' ? t('attacker') : t('defender');
            const player = document.createElement('span');
            player.className = 'titan-side-player';
            player.textContent = side.playerName || '';
            sideHead.append(sideTitle, player);
            if (matched) {
              const badge = document.createElement('span');
              badge.className = 'titan-match-badge';
              badge.textContent = t('matchUpper');
              sideHead.appendChild(badge);
            }
            sideBox.appendChild(sideHead);
            const icons = document.createElement('div');
            icons.className = 'icons';
            for (const titan of side.titans ?? []) {
              await addUnitBox(icons, titan.id, 'hero', `Titan ${titan.id}`, {
                name: titan.name,
                family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.titanPortrait,
              });
            }
            sideBox.appendChild(icons);
            renderPastTotems(sideBox, side.totems);

            if (sideName === 'defender' && Array.isArray(battle?.historicalBuffs) && battle.historicalBuffs.length) {
              const buff = document.createElement('div');
              buff.className = 'titan-buff';
              const values = battle.historicalBuffs.map(row => {
                const amount = `${Number(row.value) >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${row.value}${row.unit || ''}`;
                return `${row.name || row.effectKey}: ${amount}`;
              });
              buff.textContent = `${t('battleBuff')}: ${values.join(' / ')}`;
              sideBox.appendChild(buff);
            }
            card.appendChild(sideBox);
          };

          if (!loading && !errorMessage) {
            if (!battles.length) {
              const empty = document.createElement('div');
              empty.className = 'empty';
              empty.textContent = t('noMatchingTitanBattleLog');
              pastSection.appendChild(empty);
            } else {
              for (const battle of battles) {
                const card = document.createElement('div');
                card.className = 'titan-past-card';
                const battleHead = document.createElement('div');
                battleHead.className = 'titan-battle-head';
                const date = document.createElement('span');
                date.className = 'titan-battle-date';
                date.textContent = battle.dateString || '';
                const place = document.createElement('span');
                place.className = 'titan-battle-place';
                const position = battle.position != null && String(battle.position) !== '' ? ` #${battle.position}` : '';
                place.textContent = `${battle.building || ''}${position}`.trim();
                const resultBadge = document.createElement('span');
                resultBadge.className = 'titan-battle-result';
                resultBadge.textContent = battle.attackerWon === true
                  ? `${t('attacker')} ${t('winUpper')}`
                  : battle.attackerWon === false
                    ? `${t('defender')} ${t('winUpper')}`
                    : t('unknownResult');
                battleHead.append(date, place, resultBadge);
                card.appendChild(battleHead);
                await renderPastSide(card, battle, 'attacker');
                await renderPastSide(card, battle, 'defender');
                pastSection.appendChild(card);
              }
            }
          }
          if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected) return;
          content.appendChild(pastSection);

          if (loading) {
            const loadingRow = document.createElement('div');
            loadingRow.className = 'section loading';
            loadingRow.textContent = t('loadingReference');
            content.appendChild(loadingRow);
          } else if (errorMessage) {
            const error = document.createElement('div');
            error.className = 'section error';
            error.textContent = errorMessage;
            content.appendChild(error);
          }
          updateReferenceIconScale();
          requestAnimationFrame(placeReferenceNearGamePopup);
          return;
        }

        // The preload warms one shared ID cache for three different visuals, so each
        // ID carries the family it will actually be rendered as (Issue #47). Without
        // it the first resolution of a Pet ID would be recorded as a Hero portrait.
        // First writer wins, matching the order the Reference itself renders in.
        const preloadFamilyById = new Map();
        const addPreloadId = (value, family) => {
          const numericId = Number(value);
          if (!Number.isFinite(numericId) || numericId <= 0) return;
          if (!preloadFamilyById.has(numericId)) preloadFamilyById.set(numericId, family);
        };
        if (target?.kind === 'CoW') {
          for (const petId of result?.used?.petIds ?? []) addPreloadId(petId, ARTWORK_FAMILY.patronPet);
        }
        // Current Defense renders the live Hero lineup, its current Main Pet and
        // any current-term Patron evidence, so those icons must be preloaded even
        // when no matching history result exists.
        for (const heroId of target?.heroIds ?? []) {
          addPreloadId(heroId, ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait);
        }
        addPreloadId(target?.currentMainPet?.id, ARTWORK_FAMILY.mainPet);
        const reportedSetupState = String(result?.currentSetupState ?? '');
        const currentSetupState = errorMessage
          ? 'error'
          : loading
            ? 'loading'
            : ['confirmed', 'unknown', 'error'].includes(reportedSetupState)
              ? reportedSetupState
              : (result?.currentSetup ? 'confirmed' : 'unknown');
        const currentPatronByHeroId = new Map();
        const currentPatronStateByHeroId = new Map();
        for (const hero of currentSetupState === 'confirmed' ? (result?.currentSetup?.heroes ?? []) : []) {
          const heroId = Number(hero?.heroId);
          const petId = Number(hero?.patronPetId);
          if (!Number.isFinite(heroId) || heroId <= 0) continue;
          const patronState = ['known', 'none', 'unknown'].includes(hero?.patronPetState)
            ? hero.patronPetState
            : (petId > 0 ? 'known' : 'unknown');
          currentPatronStateByHeroId.set(heroId, patronState);
          if (patronState === 'known' && petId > 0) {
            currentPatronByHeroId.set(heroId, petId);
            addPreloadId(petId, ARTWORK_FAMILY.patronPet);
          }
        }
        const preloadRef = result?.reference ?? null;
        addPreloadId(preloadRef?.mainPetId, ARTWORK_FAMILY.mainPet);
        for (const hero of preloadRef?.heroes ?? []) {
          addPreloadId(hero?.heroId, ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait);
          addPreloadId(hero?.patronPetId, ARTWORK_FAMILY.patronPet);
        }
        const referenceFlagId = Number(preloadRef?.warFlag?.bannerId);
        const currentFlagId = Number(target?.currentDefense?.bannerId);
        const referenceFlagPromise = Number.isFinite(referenceFlagId) && referenceFlagId > 0
          ? getWarFlagSpriteSpec(referenceFlagId)
          : Promise.resolve(null);
        const currentFlagPromise = Number.isFinite(currentFlagId) && currentFlagId > 0
          ? getWarFlagSpriteSpec(currentFlagId)
          : Promise.resolve(null);
        const [, nativeFlagSpec, currentFlagSpec] = await Promise.all([
          Promise.allSettled([...preloadFamilyById].map(([id, family]) => getUnitIconSpriteSpec(id, family))),
          referenceFlagPromise,
          currentFlagPromise,
        ]);
        if (renderId !== renderSequence || !host.isConnected) return;

        clearInfoTips();

        content.innerHTML = '';

        // Current Defense means the currently known defense-team state, the same as
        // the Titan Reference: the live Hero lineup plus its current defeated state.
        // It reads only current-slot sources; Past Setup stays historical evidence
        // and no longer carries the current survivor / defeated presentation.
        const heroDefenseSection = document.createElement('div');
        heroDefenseSection.className = 'section';
        const heroDefenseLabel = document.createElement('div');
        heroDefenseLabel.className = 'label';
        heroDefenseLabel.textContent = t('currentDefense');
        heroDefenseSection.appendChild(heroDefenseLabel);
        const heroDefenseIcons = document.createElement('div');
        heroDefenseIcons.className = 'icons';

        // Main Pet is current-slot data, so it is always part of Current Defense
        // and never waits for a partial defeat or for history. A confirmed absent
        // Main Pet uses the shared compact empty label; unresolved state stays `?`.
        const mainPet = target?.currentMainPet ?? { state: 'unknown', id: null };
        if (mainPet.state === 'known') {
          await addUnitBox(heroDefenseIcons, mainPet.id, '', t('mainPetId', { id: mainPet.id }), {
            frameSpec: target?.mainPetFrame ?? null,
            family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.mainPet,
          });
        } else {
          const petBox = document.createElement('div');
          petBox.className = 'iconbox';
          const confirmedNone = mainPet.state === 'none';
          petBox.title = confirmedNone ? t('noMainPet') : t('stateUnknown');
          const placeholder = document.createElement('span');
          placeholder.className = 'placeholder';
          placeholder.textContent = confirmedNone ? t('emptyPatternShort') : '?';
          petBox.appendChild(placeholder);
          heroDefenseIcons.appendChild(petBox);
        }

        const currentHeroIds = (target?.heroIds ?? [])
          .map(Number)
          .filter(id => Number.isFinite(id) && id > 0)
          .sort((a, b) => getBattleOrder(b) - getBattleOrder(a));
        for (const heroId of currentHeroIds) {
          const state = target?.heroStateById instanceof Map
            ? target.heroStateById.get(heroId)
            : null;
          // Fail closed: unresolved state stays an ordinary living Hero.
          const defeated = state?.alive === false;
          const box = await addUnitBox(
            heroDefenseIcons,
            heroId,
            'hero',
            t('heroId', { id: heroId }),
            {
              state: defeated ? t('defeatedUpper') : '',
              frameSpec: target?.heroFrameById instanceof Map
                ? (target.heroFrameById.get(heroId) ?? null)
                : null,
              family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait,
            },
          );
          if (defeated) {
            box.classList.add('hero-defeated');
            const cross = document.createElement('span');
            cross.className = 'defeated-cross';
            cross.textContent = '×';
            const defeatedLabel = document.createElement('span');
            defeatedLabel.className = 'defeated-label';
            defeatedLabel.textContent = t('defeatedUpper');
            box.append(cross, defeatedLabel);
          }
          const currentPatronId = currentPatronByHeroId.get(heroId);
          const currentPatronState = currentPatronStateByHeroId.get(heroId) ?? 'unknown';
          if (currentSetupState === 'unknown') {
            // Before any reliable current-term battle evidence, the empty Patron ring
            // says "may be equipped, identity not known". It is not confirmed none.
            const marker = document.createElement('span');
            marker.className = 'patron-sprite patron-unknown';
            marker.title = t('stateUnknown');
            box.appendChild(marker);
          } else if (currentSetupState === 'error') {
            // A failed confirmation invalidates every assignment for this load. Keep
            // the badge visible but do not reuse an older readable record as current.
            const marker = document.createElement('span');
            marker.className = 'patron-sprite placeholder unresolved-fallback';
            marker.title = t('stateUnknown');
            marker.textContent = '?';
            box.appendChild(marker);
          } else if (currentPatronState === 'unknown') {
            // The replay was readable, but this Hero's Patron field/identity was not.
            const marker = document.createElement('span');
            marker.className = 'patron-sprite placeholder unresolved-fallback';
            marker.title = t('stateUnknown');
            marker.textContent = '?';
            box.appendChild(marker);
          } else if (Number(currentPatronId) > 0) {
            const patronIdentity = patronPetIdentityText(currentPatronId);
            const patron = await createUnitSprite(Number(currentPatronId), 22, 'patron-sprite', patronIdentity, ARTWORK_FAMILY.patronPet);
            if (patron) box.appendChild(patron);
            else {
              const marker = document.createElement('span');
              marker.className = 'patron-sprite placeholder known-fallback';
              marker.title = patronIdentity;
              marker.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(getUnitDisplayName(currentPatronId), currentPatronId);
              box.appendChild(marker);
            }
          }
        }
        if (currentHeroIds.length) {
          heroDefenseSection.appendChild(heroDefenseIcons);
          markReferenceScaleRow(heroDefenseIcons, heroDefenseIcons.childElementCount);
          if (!loading && (currentSetupState === 'unknown' || currentSetupState === 'error')) {
            const patronStatus = document.createElement('div');
            patronStatus.className = 'empty patron-status';
            patronStatus.textContent = t(
              currentSetupState === 'error'
                ? 'patronConfirmationError'
                : 'patronUnknownBeforeAttack'
            );
            heroDefenseSection.appendChild(patronStatus);
          }
          content.appendChild(heroDefenseSection);
          updateReferenceIconScale(undefined, { allowGrow: false });
        }

        // Current War Flag + Pattern slots 0..2 of the same live defense.
        const currentSection = document.createElement('div');
        currentSection.className = 'section';
        const currentLabel = document.createElement('div');
        currentLabel.className = 'label';
        currentLabel.textContent = t('warFlagPatterns');
        currentSection.appendChild(currentLabel);
        const currentRow = document.createElement('div');
        currentRow.className = 'current-row';
        const currentDefense = target?.currentDefense ?? null;

        const missingAssetText = [];

        const appendInlineCurrentMessage = (message, title = message) => {
          currentRow.appendChild(createInlineEmptyMessage(message, title));
        };

        const appendUnknownCurrentCell = (title = t('stateUnknown')) => {
          const cell = document.createElement('div');
          cell.className = 'current-cell';
          cell.title = title;
          const icon = document.createElement('div');
          icon.className = 'current-icon unknown-equipment';
          const p = document.createElement('span');
          p.className = 'placeholder';
          p.textContent = '?';
          icon.appendChild(p);
          const value = document.createElement('div');
          value.className = 'pattern-value';
          cell.append(icon, value);
          currentRow.appendChild(cell);
        };

        if (!currentDefense) {
          appendUnknownCurrentCell(t('referenceCouldNotBeLoaded'));
        } else if (!currentDefense.bannerId) {
          appendInlineCurrentMessage(t('noWarFlag'));
        } else {
          const flagCell = document.createElement('div');
          flagCell.className = 'current-cell';
          const resolvedFlagName = currentDefense.flagName || warFlagIdText(currentDefense.bannerId);
          // The icon hover carries the shared identity; the name on its own still
          // drives the in-icon short fallback and the asset-fallback text line below.
          flagCell.title = referenceIdentityText(
            currentDefense.flagName,
            currentDefense.bannerId,
            warFlagIdText(currentDefense.bannerId),
          );
          const flagIcon = document.createElement('div');
          // The native War Flag outer frame is only reachable inside a rendered
          // MiniBannerClipWithTooltip, never from BannerDescription (Issue #57 TEST2),
          // so this is the approved helper-owned silver frame.
          flagIcon.className = 'current-icon flag-frame';
          let flagRendered = false;
          if (currentFlagSpec) {
            const flagSprite = await createSpriteFromSpec(currentFlagSpec, 46, 'flag-sprite', flagCell.title, {
              family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.warFlag,
              subject: currentFlagId,
            });
            if (flagSprite) {
              flagIcon.appendChild(flagSprite);
              flagRendered = true;
            }
          }
          if (!flagRendered) {
            const p = document.createElement('span');
            p.className = 'placeholder known-fallback';
            p.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(currentDefense.flagName, currentDefense.bannerId);
            flagIcon.appendChild(p);
            missingAssetText.push(`${t('flag')}: ${resolvedFlagName}`);
          }
          const flagValue = document.createElement('div');
          flagValue.className = 'pattern-value';
          flagValue.textContent = t('flag');
          flagCell.append(flagIcon, flagValue);
          currentRow.appendChild(flagCell);

          const patternRows = Array.isArray(currentDefense.patterns) ? currentDefense.patterns : [];
          const knownPatternRows = patternRows.filter(item => Number.isInteger(Number(item?.slot)) && Number(item.slot) >= 0 && Number(item.slot) <= 2);
          const unknownPatternSlots = new Set(
            (currentDefense.patternSlotsUnknown ?? [])
              .map(Number)
              .filter(slot => Number.isInteger(slot) && slot >= 0 && slot <= 2)
          );

          if (!knownPatternRows.length && !unknownPatternSlots.size) {
            appendInlineCurrentMessage(t('noPatternsEquipped'));
          } else {
            for (let patternSlot = 0; patternSlot < 3; patternSlot += 1) {
              const row = knownPatternRows.find(item => Number(item.slot) === patternSlot) ?? null;
              const cell = document.createElement('div');
              cell.className = 'current-cell';
              const slotUnknown = unknownPatternSlots.has(patternSlot);
              const patternLabel = row?.name || (row ? `Pattern ${patternSlot + 1}` : t('patternSlotEmpty', { slot: patternSlot + 1 }));
              // An unresolved slot keeps `?` semantics and a confirmed empty slot keeps
              // its own wording; only an occupied slot carries item identity.
              const patternIdentity = row
                ? referenceIdentityText(row.name, row.id, `Pattern ${patternSlot + 1}`)
                : patternLabel;
              cell.title = slotUnknown ? t('stateUnknown') : patternIdentity;
              const icon = document.createElement('div');
              icon.className = 'current-icon';
              if (slotUnknown) {
                icon.classList.add('unknown-equipment');
                const p = document.createElement('span');
                p.className = 'placeholder';
                p.textContent = '?';
                icon.appendChild(p);
              } else if (!row) {
                icon.classList.add('empty-pattern-slot');
                const p = document.createElement('span');
                p.className = 'placeholder';
                p.textContent = t('emptyPatternShort');
                icon.appendChild(p);
              } else {
                if (row.pattern && row.colorTier) {
                  icon.classList.add('pattern-frame', `pattern-tier-${row.colorTier}`);
                }
                const nativePatternIcon = row.pattern ? await createPatternIconElement(row.pattern, 46) : null;
                if (nativePatternIcon) icon.appendChild(nativePatternIcon);
                else {
                  const p = document.createElement('span');
                  p.className = 'placeholder known-fallback';
                  p.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(row.name, row.id);
                  icon.appendChild(p);
                  const currentValue = row.currentValue;
                  missingAssetText.push(`Pattern ${patternSlot + 1}: ${patternLabel}${currentValue == null ? '' : ` (${currentValue}%)`}`);
                }
              }
              const value = document.createElement('div');
              value.className = 'pattern-value';
              const currentValue = row?.currentValue;
              value.textContent = currentValue == null ? '' : `${currentValue}%`;
              cell.append(icon, value);
              currentRow.appendChild(cell);
            }
          }
        }
        currentSection.appendChild(currentRow);
        // Only the fixed 46px cells count here: an inline "no War Flag" / "no Patterns"
        // message is ordinary text and is not part of the icon-row arithmetic.
        markReferenceScaleRow(currentRow, currentRow.querySelectorAll(':scope > .current-cell').length);

        // Usability fallback: if Hero Wars changes an asset resolver again, keep the
        // defense readable. Names come from the same live Banner/Pattern descriptions;
        // this is intentionally only shown for assets that failed to render.
        if (missingAssetText.length) {
          const fallback = document.createElement('div');
          fallback.className = 'asset-fallback-text';
          for (const line of missingAssetText) {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.textContent = line;
            fallback.appendChild(row);
          }
          currentSection.appendChild(fallback);
        }

        content.appendChild(currentSection);
        updateReferenceIconScale(undefined, { allowGrow: false });

        if (loading) {
          const loadingRow = document.createElement('div');
          loadingRow.className = 'section loading';
          loadingRow.textContent = t('loadingReference');
          content.appendChild(loadingRow);
          updateReferenceIconScale();
          requestAnimationFrame(placeReferenceNearGamePopup);
          return;
        }

        if (errorMessage) {
          const error = document.createElement('div');
          error.className = 'section error';
          error.textContent = errorMessage;
          content.appendChild(error);
          updateReferenceIconScale();
          requestAnimationFrame(placeReferenceNearGamePopup);
          return;
        }

        if (target?.kind === 'CoW') {
          // Used Patrons from the current matchup/day.
          const usedSection = document.createElement('div');
          usedSection.className = 'section';
          const usedHead = document.createElement('div');
          usedHead.className = 'used-head';
          const usedLabel = document.createElement('div');
          usedLabel.className = 'label-line';
          const usedText = document.createElement('span');
          usedText.textContent = t('usedPatrons');
          const usedInfo = document.createElement('button');
          usedInfo.type = 'button';
          usedInfo.className = 'info';
          usedInfo.setAttribute('aria-label', t('usedPatronsInfo'));
          usedInfo.textContent = 'ⓘ';
          const usedTip = document.createElement('span');
          usedTip.className = 'info-tip';
          usedTip.textContent = t('usedPatronsTip');
          usedInfo.appendChild(usedTip);
          bindInfoTip(usedInfo);
          usedLabel.append(usedText, usedInfo);
          usedHead.append(usedLabel);
          usedSection.appendChild(usedHead);
          const petIds = [...new Set((result?.used?.petIds ?? []).map(Number).filter(id => Number.isFinite(id) && id > 0))];
          // No `Last seen` row here. This section already means the current matchup, which
          // the header states, so a per-battle timestamp only read as historical noise.
          // `used.lastSeen` stays available internally for ordering / selection.
          if (result?.used?.resolved === false) {
            // The current term could not be identified, so there is no matchup to report
            // on. That is weaker than `noPatronUse`, which asserts no recorded use.
            const unknown = document.createElement('div');
            unknown.className = 'empty';
            unknown.textContent = t('stateUnknown');
            usedSection.appendChild(unknown);
          } else if (!petIds.length) {
            const none = document.createElement('div');
            none.className = 'empty';
            none.textContent = t('noPatronUse');
            usedSection.appendChild(none);
          } else {
            const usedIcons = document.createElement('div');
            usedIcons.className = 'icons';
            for (const petId of petIds) {
              await addUnitBox(usedIcons, petId, '', t('usedPatronId', { id: petId }), {
                family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.patronPet,
              });
            }
            usedSection.appendChild(usedIcons);
          }
          content.appendChild(usedSection);
        }

        // Historical exact-Hero-set reference.
        const refSection = document.createElement('div');
        // Everything above is current or current-matchup information; Past Setup is
        // historical evidence. The stronger rule marks that category boundary, and it
        // belongs to Past Setup itself, so it appears in GW too where there is no
        // Used Patron Pets section above it.
        refSection.className = 'section past-boundary';
        const pastHead = document.createElement('div');
        pastHead.className = 'past-head';
        const label = document.createElement('div');
        label.className = 'label-line';
        const labelText = document.createElement('span');
        labelText.textContent = t('pastSetup');
        const matchInfo = document.createElement('button');
        matchInfo.type = 'button';
        matchInfo.className = 'info';
        matchInfo.setAttribute('aria-label', t('pastSetupInfo'));
        matchInfo.textContent = 'ⓘ';
        const matchTip = document.createElement('span');
        matchTip.className = 'info-tip';
        matchTip.textContent = t('pastSetupTip');
        matchInfo.appendChild(matchTip);
        bindInfoTip(matchInfo);
        const ref = result?.reference ?? null;
        // The provenance a reader needs is which War Log term supplied the setup, not
        // the individual battle observation time. The battle timestamp stays internal,
        // for ordering records inside a term: if the term date cannot be resolved the
        // row is omitted rather than filled with a battle date that means something else.
        // CoW states the source term identity, GW its term date. Either way this is the
        // term that supplied the setup, never the individual battle observation time.
        const pastTermText = ref?.termDisplay || ref?.termDateString || '';
        const pastLastText = pastTermText ? t('lastSeen', { date: pastTermText }) : '';
        label.append(labelText, matchInfo);
        pastHead.append(label);
        refSection.appendChild(pastHead);
        if (ref) {
          const pastBuilding = ref.building || '';
          const pastPosition = ref.position != null && String(ref.position) !== '' ? ` #${ref.position}` : '';
          if (pastBuilding || pastPosition) {
            const meta = document.createElement('div');
            meta.className = 'meta';
            meta.textContent = `${pastBuilding || '—'}${pastPosition}`;
            refSection.appendChild(meta);
          }
        }
        if (pastLastText) {
          const pastLast = document.createElement('div');
          pastLast.className = 'last last-row';
          pastLast.textContent = pastLastText;
          refSection.appendChild(pastLast);
        }

        if (!ref) {
          const empty = document.createElement('div');
          empty.className = 'empty';
          empty.textContent = t('noMatchingBattleLog');
          refSection.appendChild(empty);
        } else {
          const icons = document.createElement('div');
          icons.className = 'icons';
          const flagBox = document.createElement('div');
          // Use the exact same border-box and silver frame presentation as Current
          // Defense. The frame marks the family, not the age of the evidence.
          flagBox.className = 'current-icon flag-frame';
          const pastWarFlagId = Number(ref.warFlag?.bannerId);
          const hasKnownPastWarFlag = Number.isFinite(pastWarFlagId) && pastWarFlagId > 0;
          if (!ref.warFlag) {
            flagBox.title = t('noWarFlag');
            const p = document.createElement('span'); p.className = 'placeholder'; p.textContent = t('pastSetupNoWarFlagShort'); flagBox.appendChild(p);
          } else if (!hasKnownPastWarFlag) {
            flagBox.title = t('stateUnknown');
            const p = document.createElement('span'); p.className = 'placeholder'; p.textContent = '?'; flagBox.appendChild(p);
          } else if (nativeFlagSpec) {
            flagBox.title = warFlagIdentityText(pastWarFlagId);
            const flagSprite = await createSpriteFromSpec(nativeFlagSpec, 46, 'flag-sprite', flagBox.title, {
              family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.warFlag,
              subject: pastWarFlagId,
            });
            if (flagSprite) flagBox.appendChild(flagSprite);
            else { const p = document.createElement('span'); p.className = 'placeholder known-fallback'; p.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(getWarFlagName(pastWarFlagId), pastWarFlagId); flagBox.appendChild(p); }
          } else {
            flagBox.title = warFlagIdentityText(pastWarFlagId);
            const p = document.createElement('span'); p.className = 'placeholder known-fallback'; p.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(getWarFlagName(pastWarFlagId), pastWarFlagId); flagBox.appendChild(p);
          }
          icons.appendChild(flagBox);
          await addUnitBox(icons, ref.mainPetId, '', ref.mainPetId == null ? t('noMainPet') : t('mainPetId', { id: ref.mainPetId }), {
            frameSpec: ref.mainPetId == null
              ? null
              : getHistoricalUnitNativeFrameSpec(ref.mainPetColor, { unitFamily: 'pet', subject: ref.mainPetId }),
            family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.mainPet,
          });

          // Historical Hero portraits identify the historical Patron assignments.
          // They are historical identities only: the current survivor / defeated
          // state belongs to Current Defense and is never re-derived here.
          const sortedHeroes = ref.heroes.slice().sort((a, b) => getBattleOrder(b.heroId) - getBattleOrder(a.heroId));
          for (const hero of sortedHeroes) {
            const box = await addUnitBox(icons, hero.heroId, 'hero', t('heroId', { id: hero.heroId }), {
              frameSpec: getHistoricalUnitNativeFrameSpec(hero.color, { unitFamily: 'hero', subject: hero.heroId }),
              family: ARTWORK_FAMILY.heroPortrait,
            });
            if (Number(hero.patronPetId) > 0) {
              const patronIdentity = patronPetIdentityText(hero.patronPetId);
              const patron = await createUnitSprite(Number(hero.patronPetId), 22, 'patron-sprite', patronIdentity, ARTWORK_FAMILY.patronPet);
              if (patron) box.appendChild(patron);
              else {
                const marker = document.createElement('span');
                marker.className = 'patron-sprite placeholder known-fallback';
                marker.title = patronIdentity;
                marker.textContent = shortIdentityFallback(getUnitDisplayName(hero.patronPetId), hero.patronPetId);
                box.appendChild(marker);
              }
            }
          }
          refSection.appendChild(icons);
          markReferenceScaleRow(icons, icons.childElementCount);
        }
        content.appendChild(refSection);

        updateReferenceIconScale();
        requestAnimationFrame(placeReferenceNearGamePopup);
      },
      setFontSize,
      resetSize: resetReferenceSize,
      setDefeatedStyle(style) {
        defeatedDisplayStyle = ['cross', 'label', 'gray'].includes(style) ? style : 'cross';
        panel.dataset.defeatedStyle = defeatedDisplayStyle;
      },
      getKind() { return currentTargetKind; },
      setScopeVisible(visible) {
        const nextVisible = Boolean(visible);
        if (nextVisible) ensureUpdateAvailabilityCheck();
        const currentlyVisible = host.style.display !== 'none';
        // GW polls its scope every REFRESH_MS. Reapplying the same visible state
        // used to schedule a position/size correction on every poll, which fought
        // active pointer drag/resize and made the Reference panel flicker or snap.
        if (currentlyVisible === nextVisible) return;
        host.style.display = nextVisible ? '' : 'none';
        // A hidden Reference must not come back with a stale settings popover open,
        // and must not keep its outside-click listener while it cannot be clicked.
        if (!nextVisible) referenceSettingsController.close();
        if (nextVisible) requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          const fittedWidth = ensureReferenceHeaderFits();
          updateReferenceIconScale(fittedWidth);
          if (referenceUserMoved) {
            if (!placeSavedReferencePosition()) clampReferenceToViewport();
          } else {
            clampReferenceToViewport();
          }
        });
      },
      remove() {
        renderSequence += 1;
        referenceSettingsController.close();
        window.removeEventListener('resize', handleReferenceGeometryChange);
        referenceGameResizeObserver?.disconnect();
        removeReferenceResizeHandles?.();
        contentResizeObserver?.disconnect();
        host.remove();
      },
    };
  }

  function ensurePatronReferenceView() {
    if (!patronReferenceView?.host?.isConnected) patronReferenceView = createPatronReferenceView();
    ensureUpdateAvailabilityCheck();
    return patronReferenceView;
  }

  function getCurrentReferenceSession() {
    if (nativeCowSession?.target) {
      try {
        const popupOpen = nativeCowSession.popup &&
          getOpenPopupsByClass(CLASS.demoPopup).includes(nativeCowSession.popup);
        if (popupOpen || (nativeCowSession.attackEditorSeen && hasOpenTrainingUi())) {
          return { session: nativeCowSession, target: nativeCowSession.target };
        }
      } catch {}
    }
    if (activeDefenseSession?.patronTarget && hasOpenTrainingUi()) {
      return { session: activeDefenseSession, target: activeDefenseSession.patronTarget };
    }
    return null;
  }

  function hideReferenceReopenLauncher() {
    referenceReopenLauncher?._hwctCleanup?.();
    referenceReopenLauncher?.remove?.();
    referenceReopenLauncher = null;
    const stale = document.getElementById(REFERENCE_REOPEN_HOST_ID);
    stale?._hwctCleanup?.();
    stale?.remove();
  }

  function showReferenceReopenLauncher(originRect = null) {
    const current = getCurrentReferenceSession();
    if (!current || current.session?.referenceHiddenForAttackEditor) {
      hideReferenceReopenLauncher();
      return null;
    }
    if (referenceReopenLauncher?.isConnected) return referenceReopenLauncher;

    document.getElementById(REFERENCE_REOPEN_HOST_ID)?.remove();
    const uiState = loadUiState();
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.id = REFERENCE_REOPEN_HOST_ID;

    function getLegacyMiniSide() {
      if (uiState.refMiniSide === 'left' || uiState.refMiniSide === 'right') return uiState.refMiniSide;
      if (Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelOffsetXH)) return uiState.refPanelOffsetXH < 0 ? 'left' : 'right';
      return Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelX) && uiState.refPanelX < 0.5 ? 'left' : 'right';
    }

    function getLegacyMiniY() {
      if (Number.isFinite(uiState.refMiniY)) return clamp(uiState.refMiniY, 0, 1);
      return Number.isFinite(uiState.refPanelY) ? clamp(uiState.refPanelY, 0, 1) : 0.25;
    }

    function getMiniVisualScale(game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry()) {
      return Math.max(.05, game.height / REF_LAYOUT_REFERENCE_GAME_HEIGHT);
    }

    function migrateLegacyMiniPosition() {
      if (Number.isFinite(uiState.refMiniOffsetXH) && Number.isFinite(uiState.refMiniOffsetYH)) return;
