      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const width = 44;
      const height = 44;
      const side = getLegacyMiniSide();
      const yRatio = getLegacyMiniY();
      const left = side === 'left' ? 0 : Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - width);
      const top = Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - height) * yRatio;
      uiState.refMiniOffsetXH = (left + width / 2 - game.centerX) / game.height;
      uiState.refMiniOffsetYH = (top + height / 2 - game.centerY) / game.height;
      saveUiState({
        refMiniOffsetXH:uiState.refMiniOffsetXH,
        refMiniOffsetYH:uiState.refMiniOffsetYH,
      });
    }

    function placeMini() {
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const button = shadow?.querySelector?.('button');
      if (!button) return;
      const visualScale = getMiniVisualScale(game);
      button.style.zoom = String(visualScale);
      migrateLegacyMiniPosition();
      const rect = button.getBoundingClientRect();
      const centerX = game.centerX + uiState.refMiniOffsetXH * game.height;
      const centerY = game.centerY + uiState.refMiniOffsetYH * game.height;
      const left = clamp(centerX - rect.width / 2, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width));
      const top = clamp(centerY - rect.height / 2, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - rect.height));
      host.style.left = `${left.toFixed(3)}px`;
      host.style.right = 'auto';
      host.style.top = `${top.toFixed(3)}px`;
    }

    host.style.cssText = [
      'position:fixed',
      'left:0',
      'top:0',
      'z-index:2147483647',
      'pointer-events:auto',
      'user-select:none'
    ].join(';') + ';';

    const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    shadow.innerHTML = `
      <style>
        :host { all: initial; }
        .mini {
          position:relative; box-sizing:border-box; width:44px; height:44px;
          border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.22);
          border-radius:9px;
          background:rgba(24,27,33,.96);
          color:#f1f1f1;
          cursor:grab;
          touch-action:none;
          padding:3px;
          box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.35);
          display:flex;
          flex-direction:column;
          align-items:center;
          justify-content:center;
          gap:0;
          font-family:sans-serif;
        }
        .mini:hover { background:rgba(40,44,52,.98); color:#fff; }
        .mini.dragging { cursor:grabbing; }
        .mini-icon { font:700 27px/27px Arial; color:#e3b65f; text-shadow:0 0 4px rgba(227,182,95,.28); }
        .update-dot-mini { position:absolute; right:3px; top:3px; width:9px; height:9px; border-radius:50%; background:#f04455; border:1px solid #ffd6dc; box-shadow:0 0 5px rgba(240,68,85,.85); pointer-events:none; }
        .update-tip-mini { position:fixed; left:8px; top:8px; z-index:2147483647; width:min(260px, calc(100vw - 16px)); padding:8px 9px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2); border-radius:7px; background:rgb(16,18,23); color:#eee; font:12px/1.42 sans-serif; white-space:pre-line; box-shadow:0 5px 17px rgba(0,0,0,.55); pointer-events:none; display:none; text-align:left; }
        .mini.update-available:hover .update-tip-mini, .mini.update-available:focus .update-tip-mini { display:block; }
      </style>
      <button class="mini" type="button" aria-label="${t('openDefenseReference')}" title="${t('defenseReference')} v${VERSION}">
        <span class="mini-icon">⚔</span><span class="update-dot-mini" hidden></span><span class="update-tip-mini"></span>
      </button>
    `;
    const miniButton = shadow.querySelector('button');
    const miniUpdateDot = shadow.querySelector('.update-dot-mini');
    const miniUpdateTip = shadow.querySelector('.update-tip-mini');
    const launcherTransition = createMiniLauncherTransition(host, miniButton);
    const unsubscribeMiniUpdate = subscribeUpdateAvailability(state => {
      const show = Boolean(state.updateAvailable && state.latestVersion);
      miniButton.classList.toggle('update-available', show);
      miniUpdateDot.hidden = !show;
      miniUpdateTip.textContent = show ? updateTooltipText(state.latestVersion) : '';
    });
    host.__hwctUpdateUnsubscribe = unsubscribeMiniUpdate;
    const positionMiniUpdateTip = () => {
      if (!miniButton.classList.contains('update-available')) return;
      const anchorRect = miniButton.getBoundingClientRect();
      const margin = 8, gap = 6;
      const previousDisplay = miniUpdateTip.style.display;
      const previousVisibility = miniUpdateTip.style.visibility;
      miniUpdateTip.style.visibility = 'hidden';
      miniUpdateTip.style.display = 'block';
      const rect = miniUpdateTip.getBoundingClientRect();
      let left = anchorRect.left;
      let top = anchorRect.bottom + gap;
      if (top + rect.height > window.innerHeight - margin) top = anchorRect.top - rect.height - gap;
      left = clamp(left, margin, Math.max(margin, window.innerWidth - rect.width - margin));
      top = clamp(top, margin, Math.max(margin, window.innerHeight - rect.height - margin));
      miniUpdateTip.style.left = `${Math.round(left)}px`;
      miniUpdateTip.style.top = `${Math.round(top)}px`;
      miniUpdateTip.style.visibility = previousVisibility;
      miniUpdateTip.style.display = previousDisplay;
    };
    miniButton.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => requestAnimationFrame(positionMiniUpdateTip));
    miniButton.addEventListener('focus', () => requestAnimationFrame(positionMiniUpdateTip));
    ensureUpdateAvailabilityCheck();
    let miniDrag = null;

    miniButton.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
      if (event.button !== 0) return;
      launcherTransition.cancel();
      const rect = miniButton.getBoundingClientRect();
      host.style.left = `${rect.left.toFixed(3)}px`;
      host.style.right = 'auto';
      host.style.top = `${rect.top.toFixed(3)}px`;
      miniDrag = {
        id: event.pointerId,
        dx: event.clientX - rect.left,
        dy: event.clientY - rect.top,
        startX: event.clientX,
        startY: event.clientY,
        moved: false,
      };
      miniButton.classList.add('dragging');
      miniButton.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
      event.preventDefault();
    });

    miniButton.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
      if (!miniDrag || event.pointerId !== miniDrag.id) return;
      if (Math.hypot(event.clientX - miniDrag.startX, event.clientY - miniDrag.startY) >= 4) miniDrag.moved = true;
      if (!miniDrag.moved) return;
      const rect = miniButton.getBoundingClientRect();
      const left = clamp(event.clientX - miniDrag.dx, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - rect.width));
      const top = clamp(event.clientY - miniDrag.dy, 0, Math.max(0, window.innerHeight - rect.height));
      host.style.left = `${left.toFixed(3)}px`;
      host.style.right = 'auto';
      host.style.top = `${top.toFixed(3)}px`;
    });

    function endMiniDrag(event, cancelled = false) {
      if (!miniDrag || event.pointerId !== miniDrag.id) return;
      const moved = miniDrag.moved;
      miniDrag = null;
      miniButton.classList.remove('dragging');
      if (!moved) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          hideReferenceReopenLauncher();
          showActivePatronReference();
        } else {
          placeMini();
        }
        return;
      }

      const rect = miniButton.getBoundingClientRect();
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      uiState.refMiniOffsetXH =
        (rect.left + rect.width / 2 - game.centerX) / game.height;
      uiState.refMiniOffsetYH =
        (rect.top + rect.height / 2 - game.centerY) / game.height;
      // Keep the legacy edge fields synchronized for rollback/backward compatibility.
      uiState.refMiniSide = rect.left + rect.width / 2 < window.innerWidth / 2 ? 'left' : 'right';
      uiState.refMiniY = clamp(rect.top / Math.max(1, window.innerHeight - rect.height), 0, 1);
      saveUiState({
        refMiniOffsetXH:uiState.refMiniOffsetXH,
        refMiniOffsetYH:uiState.refMiniOffsetYH,
        refMiniSide:uiState.refMiniSide,
        refMiniY:uiState.refMiniY,
      });
      placeMini();
    }

    miniButton.addEventListener('pointerup', event => endMiniDrag(event, false));
    miniButton.addEventListener('pointercancel', event => endMiniDrag(event, true));

    let observedMiniGameCanvas = null;
    const miniGameResizeObserver = typeof ResizeObserver === 'function'
      ? new ResizeObserver(() => handleMiniGeometryChange())
      : null;

    function syncMiniGameObserverTarget() {
      if (!miniGameResizeObserver) return;
      const nextCanvas = getLargestHeroWarsCanvasEntry()?.canvas ?? null;
      if (nextCanvas === observedMiniGameCanvas) return;
      if (observedMiniGameCanvas) miniGameResizeObserver.unobserve(observedMiniGameCanvas);
      observedMiniGameCanvas = nextCanvas;
      if (observedMiniGameCanvas) miniGameResizeObserver.observe(observedMiniGameCanvas);
    }

    function handleMiniGeometryChange() {
      if (miniDrag) return;
      window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (miniDrag || !host.isConnected) return;
        syncMiniGameObserverTarget();
        placeMini();
      });
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', handleMiniGeometryChange);
    syncMiniGameObserverTarget();
    host._hwctCleanup = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleMiniGeometryChange);
      miniGameResizeObserver?.disconnect();
      launcherTransition.cancel();
    };

    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    syncNativeUiOcclusionVisibility();
    placeMini();
    referenceReopenLauncher = host;

    launcherTransition.start(originRect);

    return host;
  }

  function minimizePatronReference(originRect = null) {
    patronRequestToken += 1;
    patronReferenceView?.savePosition?.();
    patronReferenceView?.remove?.();
    patronReferenceView = null;
    hideReferenceReopenLauncher();
    if (getCurrentReferenceSession()) {
      mainPanelController?.hideForReference?.();
      showReferenceReopenLauncher(originRect);
    }
  }

  function hidePatronReference({ restoreMain = true, offerReopen = false } = {}) {
    patronRequestToken += 1;
    patronReferenceView?.remove?.();
    patronReferenceView = null;
    hideReferenceReopenLauncher();
    if (restoreMain) {
      const keepGwSelectionHidden = Boolean(activeDefenseSession && hasOpenTrainingUi());
      if (!keepGwSelectionHidden) mainPanelController?.showAfterReference?.();
      else mainPanelController?.hideForReference?.();
    }
    if (offerReopen && getCurrentReferenceSession()) showReferenceReopenLauncher();
  }

  function showActivePatronReference() {
    const current = getCurrentReferenceSession();
    if (!current) return false;
    const { session, target } = current;
    hideReferenceReopenLauncher();
    if (session.referenceHiddenForAttackEditor) return false;
    if (target?.kind === 'GW') mainPanelController?.hideForReference?.();
    const view = ensurePatronReferenceView();
    if (session.referenceResult) {
      Promise.resolve(view.render(target, session.referenceResult)).catch(error => {
        warn('PATRON_REFERENCE_RENDER_FAILED', error);
        view.setError(t('failedDisplayDefenseReference'), target);
      });
      return true;
    }
    if (session.referenceError) {
      view.setError(t('couldNotLoadBattleLogReference'), target);
      return true;
    }
    if (session.referenceLoading) {
      view.setLoading(target);
      return true;
    }
    startPatronReferenceLoad(target, session);
    return true;
  }

  function getReferenceViewForRender(session, token) {
    if (token !== patronRequestToken) return null;
    if (session && (!isReferenceSessionActive(session) || session.referenceHiddenForAttackEditor)) return null;
    if (!patronReferenceView?.host?.isConnected) return null;
    return patronReferenceView;
  }

  function startPatronReferenceLoad(target, session = activeDefenseSession) {
    hideReferenceReopenLauncher();
    const token = ++patronRequestToken;
    if (target?.kind === 'GW') mainPanelController?.hideForReference?.();
    const view = ensurePatronReferenceView();

    if (target?.referenceType === 'titan') {
      if (!TITAN_PAST_REFERENCE_ENABLED) {
        // Initial Titan release intentionally stops at the live Current Defense.
        // Do not touch war-history APIs/replays until Past Battles returns in a
        // separately tested UI redesign.
        const result = {
          currentTitanDefense: target.currentTitanDefense ?? null,
          battles: [],
          stats: { source: 'current-titan-defense', pastReferenceEnabled: false },
        };
        if (session && isReferenceSessionActive(session)) {
          session.referenceResult = result;
          session.referenceError = null;
          session.referenceLoading = false;
        }
        const renderView = getReferenceViewForRender(session, token);
        if (!renderView) return;
        log('Titan Current Reference', target);
        Promise.resolve(renderView.render(target, result)).catch(error => {
          warn('TITAN_CURRENT_REFERENCE_RENDER_FAILED', error);
          renderView.setError(t('failedDisplayDefenseReference'), target);
        });
        return;
      }

      view.setLoading(target);
      if (session) session.referenceLoading = true;
      Promise.resolve()
        .then(() => loadTitanPastReference(target))
        .then(async past => {
          const result = {
            currentTitanDefense: target.currentTitanDefense ?? null,
            battles: Array.isArray(past?.battles) ? past.battles : [],
            stats: past?.stats ?? { source: 'titan-past-reference' },
          };
          if (session && isReferenceSessionActive(session)) {
            session.referenceResult = result;
            session.referenceError = null;
            session.referenceLoading = false;
          }
          const renderView = getReferenceViewForRender(session, token);
          if (!renderView) return;
          log('Titan Past Reference', target, result.stats);
          await renderView.render(target, result);
        })
        .catch(async error => {
          const result = {
            currentTitanDefense: target.currentTitanDefense ?? null,
            battles: [],
            stats: { source: 'current-titan-defense', pastReferenceError: String(error?.message ?? error) },
          };
          if (session && isReferenceSessionActive(session)) {
            // Current Reference remains usable even if historical log lookup fails.
            session.referenceResult = result;
            session.referenceError = null;
            session.referenceLoading = false;
          }
          const renderView = getReferenceViewForRender(session, token);
          if (!renderView) return;
          warn('TITAN_PAST_REFERENCE_FAILED', error);
          await renderView.render(target, result, { errorMessage: t('couldNotLoadBattleLogReference') });
        });
      return;
    }

    view.setLoading(target);
    if (session) session.referenceLoading = true;
    Promise.resolve()
      .then(() => target?.kind === 'GW'
        ? loadGwPatronReference(target)
        : target?.kind === 'CoW'
          ? loadCowPatronReference(target)
          : fail('PATRON_TARGET_KIND_UNSUPPORTED'))
      .then(async result => {
        if (session && isReferenceSessionActive(session)) {
          session.referenceResult = result;
          session.referenceError = null;
          session.referenceLoading = false;
        }
        const renderView = getReferenceViewForRender(session, token);
        if (!renderView) return;
        log('Patron Reference', target, result.stats);
        await renderView.render(target, result);
      })
      .catch(error => {
        if (session && isReferenceSessionActive(session)) {
          session.referenceError = error;
          session.referenceLoading = false;
        }
        const renderView = getReferenceViewForRender(session, token);
        if (!renderView) return;
        warn('PATRON_REFERENCE_FAILED', error);
        renderView.setError(t('couldNotLoadBattleLogReference'), target);
      });
  }

  async function launchTraining(slotNumber, mode, snapshotHint = null) {
    if (hasOpenDemoBattle()) fail('DEMO_BATTLE_ALREADY_OPEN');

    // Prefer the already-rendered live context. A fresh PopupManager lookup is unnecessary
    // and can briefly fail while Hero Wars mutates popup state.
    const snapshot = snapshotHint?.kind ? snapshotHint : detectContextSnapshot();
    const data = getLaunchData(snapshot, slotNumber);
    // Capture target metadata before opening Combat Training. Reference failures must
    // never block MAX itself. Hero history is loaded later; Titan Current Reference
    // is captured from the live target slot now.
    let referenceTarget = null;
    if (mode === MODES.MAX && (snapshot.kind === 'GW' || snapshot.kind === 'CoW')) {
      try {
        referenceTarget = data.isHero
          ? getPatronTarget(snapshot, data.item)
          : buildTitanReferenceTargetFromSlot(snapshot.kind, data.item.slot, {
              slotNumber: data.item.slotNumber,
              building: data.item.building,
              mediator: snapshot.mediator,
              popup: snapshot.popup,
            });
      } catch (error) {
        warn(data.isHero ? 'PATRON_TARGET_SKIPPED' : 'TITAN_REFERENCE_TARGET_SKIPPED', error);
      }
    }
    const Presets = findClass(CLASS.demoPresets);
    const Demo = findClass(CLASS.demoMediator);

    const currentPresets = new Presets(
      data.buffs,
      data.banner,
      data.pet,
      data.team.slice()
    );

    let demo;
    let demoOpened = false;
    let viewBanner = data.banner;

    if (mode === MODES.MAX) {
      // CoW keeps the proven helper-side MAX banner clone.
      // GW uses Hero Wars' native MAX conversion only; do not independently
      // replace the enemy's Pattern items with Absolute MAX Patterns here.
      viewBanner = snapshot.kind === 'CoW' ? buildMaxBanner(data.banner) : null;

      if (snapshot.kind === 'CoW') {
        // CoW native MAX path: a non-zero slot/fortification entry id triggers the game's MAX conversion.
        demo = new Demo(
          snapshot.mediator.player,
          data.isHero,
          data.battleMode,
          0,
          data.descId,
          currentPresets
        );
        const prepared = await waitForCoWMaxDisplay(demo, data.team.length);
        if (viewBanner) setBattleTeamBanner(prepared.team, viewBanner);
      } else {
        // GW has no native Combat Training button. In the current Hero Wars build,
        // applying the MAX preset before open() is overwritten by popup initialization.
        // Open the native Create Hero battle popup first, then apply MAX to its live model.
        demo = new Demo(
          snapshot.mediator.player,
          data.isHero,
          data.battleMode,
          0,
          data.descId,
          currentPresets
        );
        log('GW launch base preset', {
          slotNumber,
          descId: data.descId,
          teamCount: data.team.length,
          hasPet: Boolean(data.pet),
          hasBanner: Boolean(data.banner),
        });

        demo.open();
        demoOpened = true;
        await waitFor(() => hasOpenDemoBattle(), { code: 'GW_DEMO_OPEN_TIMEOUT' });

        const prepared = await forceGwMaxDisplay(demo, data.team.length);
        // Keep the banner produced by the game's native MAX preset path.
        if (!demo[prepared.control.getterName]()) fail('GW_MAX_NOT_ACTIVE');

        log('GW post-open MAX ready', {
          slotNumber,
          teamCount: data.team.length,
          defenderMax: Boolean(demo[prepared.control.getterName]()),
        });
      }
    } else if (mode === MODES.MAX_CURR) {
      viewBanner = buildMaxBanner(data.banner);

      if (snapshot.kind === 'CoW') {
        // Build a normal CoW MAX visual team first for MAX(CURR).
        demo = new Demo(
          snapshot.mediator.player,
          data.isHero,
          data.battleMode,
          0,
          data.descId,
          currentPresets
        );
        const prepared = await waitForCoWMaxDisplay(demo, data.team.length);
        if (viewBanner) setBattleTeamBanner(prepared.team, viewBanner);
        // Then change only the internal battle condition back to CURRENT. The UI selection stays MAX.
        setInternalCurrentWithoutChangingView(demo, prepared.control);
      } else {
        // GW MAX(CURR): create the MAX view using the game MAX conversion, then retain that view while returning the internal flag to CURRENT.
        demo = new Demo(
          snapshot.mediator.player,
          data.isHero,
          data.battleMode,
          0,
          data.descId,
          currentPresets
        );
        const prepared = await forceGwMaxDisplay(demo, data.team.length);
        if (viewBanner) setBattleTeamBanner(prepared.team, viewBanner);
        setInternalCurrentWithoutChangingView(demo, prepared.control);
      }

      setDefenderUser(demo, data.targetUser);
      const defenseControl = resolvePowerControl(demo, 'get_defenderMaxPowerMode');
      const attackControl = resolvePowerControl(demo, 'get_attackerMaxPowerMode');
      if (demo[defenseControl.getterName]()) fail('MAX_CURR_INTERNAL_DEFENSE_NOT_CURRENT');
      if (demo[attackControl.getterName]()) fail('MAX_CURR_INTERNAL_ATTACK_NOT_CURRENT');
    } else {
      fail('INVALID_MODE', String(mode));
    }

    if (!demoOpened) demo.open();

    // GW formal UX: stop on Create Hero battle after the MAX defense is ready.
    // The user opens the native Defense editor manually with the pencil button
    // only when adjustments (for example Patron selection) are needed.
    const defenseEditorOpened = false;

    return {
      kind: snapshot.kind,
      slotNumber,
      mode,
      teamCount: data.team.length,
      userName: data.item.userName,
      userId: data.item.userId,
      building: data.item.building || referenceTarget?.building || '',
      teamKey: data.item.teamKey || getSlotTeamKey(data.team),
      defenseEditorOpened,
      patronTarget: referenceTarget,
      demoMediator: demo,
      battleLaunchBaseline: captureBattleLaunchBaseline(demo),
    };
  }

  function humanizeError(error) {
    const code = error?.code;
    switch (code) {
      case 'HAXE_NOT_READY':
        return t('errorWaitGameLoad');
      case 'ATTACK_CONTEXT_NOT_FOUND':
      case 'SLOT_LIST_NOT_FOUND':
        return t('openGwAttackTarget');
      case 'DEMO_BATTLE_ALREADY_OPEN':
        return t('errorCloseCombatTraining');
      case 'SLOT_NOT_FOUND':
        return t('errorSlotNotCurrentBuilding');
      case 'SLOT_NOT_READY':
        return t('errorSlotNotAvailable');
      case 'SLOT_EMPTY':
        return t('errorSlotNoTeam');
      case 'TARGET_USER_NOT_FOUND':
        return t('errorTargetUser');
      case 'MAX_PATTERN_MATCH_FAILED':
      case 'ABSOLUTE_PATTERN_LIST_NOT_FOUND':
        return t('errorMaxPattern');
      case 'GW_MAX_TEAM_TIMEOUT':
      case 'GW_MAX_TEAM_FIRST_APPLY_TIMEOUT':
      case 'GW_MAX_TEAM_SECOND_APPLY_TIMEOUT':
      case 'GW_MAX_TEAM_LATE_RESTORE_TIMEOUT':
      case 'COW_MAX_TEAM_TIMEOUT':
        return t('errorMaxTeamTimeout');
      default:
        return t('errorStopped', { detail: code ?? error?.message ?? 'unknown' });
    }
  }

  async function copyText(text) {
    if (!text) return false;
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(String(text));
        return true;
      }
    } catch {}

    try {
      const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
      textarea.value = String(text);
      textarea.style.position = 'fixed';
      textarea.style.opacity = '0';
      textarea.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      document.body.appendChild(textarea);
      textarea.focus();
      textarea.select();
      const ok = document.execCommand('copy');
      textarea.remove();
      return ok;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function loadUiState() {
    try {
      const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_STORAGE_KEY) || '{}');
      return {
        // Brand-new installs use the release-tuned live layout; persisted user choices still win.
        minimized: Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw, 'minimized') ? Boolean(raw.minimized) : true,
        expandedX: Number.isFinite(raw.expandedX) ? clamp(raw.expandedX, 0, 1) : MAIN_PANEL_DEFAULT_EXPANDED_X,
        expandedY: Number.isFinite(raw.expandedY) ? clamp(raw.expandedY, 0, 1) : MAIN_PANEL_DEFAULT_EXPANDED_Y,
        miniSide: raw.miniSide === 'left' ? 'left' : 'right',
        miniY: Number.isFinite(raw.miniY) ? clamp(raw.miniY, 0, 1) : MAIN_PANEL_DEFAULT_MINI_Y,
        fontSize: Number.isFinite(raw.fontSize) ? clamp(Math.round(raw.fontSize), UI_FONT_MIN, UI_FONT_MAX) : UI_FONT_DEFAULT,
        mainPanelWidth: Number.isFinite(raw.mainPanelWidth) ? Math.max(MAIN_PANEL_MIN_WIDTH, Math.round(raw.mainPanelWidth)) : MAIN_PANEL_INITIAL_WIDTH,
        mainPanelHeight: Number.isFinite(raw.mainPanelHeight) ? Math.max(PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT, Math.round(raw.mainPanelHeight)) : MAIN_PANEL_INITIAL_HEIGHT,
        refPanelWidth: Number.isFinite(raw.refPanelWidth) ? Math.max(1, Math.round(raw.refPanelWidth)) : REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH,
        refPanelHeight: Number.isFinite(raw.refPanelHeight) ? Math.max(PANEL_MIN_HEIGHT, Math.round(raw.refPanelHeight)) : REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_HEIGHT,
        refPanelX: Number.isFinite(raw.refPanelX) ? clamp(raw.refPanelX, 0, 1) : null,
        refPanelY: Number.isFinite(raw.refPanelY) ? clamp(raw.refPanelY, 0, 1) : null,
        refPanelOffsetXH: Number.isFinite(raw.refPanelOffsetXH) ? raw.refPanelOffsetXH : REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_OFFSET_X_H,
        refPanelOffsetYH: Number.isFinite(raw.refPanelOffsetYH) ? raw.refPanelOffsetYH : REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_OFFSET_Y_H,
        refMiniSide: raw.refMiniSide === 'left' ? 'left' : raw.refMiniSide === 'right' ? 'right' : 'right',
        refMiniY: Number.isFinite(raw.refMiniY) ? clamp(raw.refMiniY, 0, 1) : 0.5200939723876578,
        refMiniOffsetXH: Number.isFinite(raw.refMiniOffsetXH) ? raw.refMiniOffsetXH : REF_MINI_DEFAULT_OFFSET_X_H,
        refMiniOffsetYH: Number.isFinite(raw.refMiniOffsetYH) ? raw.refMiniOffsetYH : REF_MINI_DEFAULT_OFFSET_Y_H,
        battleModeIndicator: Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw, 'battleModeIndicator') ? Boolean(raw.battleModeIndicator) : true,
        liveWarningAnimation: Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(raw, 'liveWarningAnimation') ? Boolean(raw.liveWarningAnimation) : true,
        battleModeX: Number.isFinite(raw.battleModeX) ? clamp(raw.battleModeX, 0, 1) : null,
        battleModeY: Number.isFinite(raw.battleModeY) ? clamp(raw.battleModeY, 0, 1) : null,
        battleModeOffsetXH: Number.isFinite(raw.battleModeOffsetXH) ? raw.battleModeOffsetXH : null,
        battleModeOffsetYH: Number.isFinite(raw.battleModeOffsetYH) ? raw.battleModeOffsetYH : null,
        battleModeWidthH: Number.isFinite(raw.battleModeWidthH) ? Math.max(BATTLE_MODE_MIN_WIDTH_H, raw.battleModeWidthH) : null,
        battleModeWidthPct: Number.isFinite(raw.battleModeWidthPct) ? Math.max(BATTLE_MODE_MIN_WIDTH_PCT, raw.battleModeWidthPct) : BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT,
        battleModeScale: Number.isFinite(raw.battleModeScale) ? Math.max(0.05, raw.battleModeScale) : null,
      };
    } catch {
      return {
        minimized: true,
        expandedX: MAIN_PANEL_DEFAULT_EXPANDED_X,
        expandedY: MAIN_PANEL_DEFAULT_EXPANDED_Y,
        miniSide: 'right',
        miniY: MAIN_PANEL_DEFAULT_MINI_Y,
        fontSize: UI_FONT_DEFAULT,
        mainPanelWidth: MAIN_PANEL_INITIAL_WIDTH,
        mainPanelHeight: MAIN_PANEL_INITIAL_HEIGHT,
        refPanelWidth: REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_WIDTH,
        refPanelHeight: REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_HEIGHT,
        refPanelX: null,
        refPanelY: null,
        refPanelOffsetXH: REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_OFFSET_X_H,
        refPanelOffsetYH: REF_PANEL_DEFAULT_OFFSET_Y_H,
        refMiniSide: 'right',
        refMiniY: 0.5200939723876578,
        refMiniOffsetXH: REF_MINI_DEFAULT_OFFSET_X_H,
        refMiniOffsetYH: REF_MINI_DEFAULT_OFFSET_Y_H,
        battleModeIndicator: true, liveWarningAnimation: true,
        battleModeX: null, battleModeY: null, battleModeOffsetXH: null, battleModeOffsetYH: null, battleModeWidthH: null,
        battleModeWidthPct: BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT, battleModeScale: null,
      };
    }
  }

  function saveUiState(patch) {
    let latest = {};
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_STORAGE_KEY) || '{}');
      if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) latest = parsed;
    } catch {}
    const changed = patch && typeof patch === 'object' && !Array.isArray(patch) ? patch : {};
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(UI_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...latest, ...changed }));
    } catch {}
  }

  function getBattleModePreferences() {
    // Legacy ON/OFF values may still exist in localStorage, but the sign and its
    // approved animation are now normal always-enabled product behavior.
    return { indicator: true, animation: true };
  }

  function resetUiSizes() {
    // Reset size-only preferences. Position and other persisted UI choices stay intact.
    saveUiState({
      fontSize: UI_FONT_DEFAULT,
      battleModeWidthH: BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H,
      battleModeWidthPct: BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT,
      battleModeScale: 1,
      refPanelWidth: null,
      refPanelHeight: null,
    });
    mainPanelController?.setFontSize?.(UI_FONT_DEFAULT);
    battleModeIndicatorController?.resetSize?.();
    patronReferenceView?.resetSize?.();
  }

  function getOpenPopupObjectsForBattleMode() {
    try {
      const manager = getPopupManager();
      return uniqueRefs(
        Object.values(manager)
          .filter(Array.isArray)
          .flat()
          .filter(Boolean)
      );
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }

  function getOpenPopupClassNamesForBattleMode() {
    return [...new Set(
      getOpenPopupObjectsForBattleMode()
        .map(value => className(value))
        .filter(Boolean)
    )];
  }

  function isPotentialLiveAttackTeamGatherClass(name) {
    const value = String(name ?? '');
    if (!value || value === CLASS.demoAttackGatherPopup || value.includes('.demoBattle.')) return false;
    const short = value.split('.').pop() ?? '';
    return /TeamGather/i.test(short);
  }

  function getOpenGwLiveAttackChoiceMediator() {
    const popups = getOpenPopupObjectsForBattleMode();
    if (!popups.some(popup => className(popup) === CLASS.gwAttackPopup)) return null;

    const candidates = popups.filter(popup => isPotentialLiveAttackTeamGatherClass(className(popup)));
    if (candidates.length !== 1) return null;

    const mediators = directValuesByClass(candidates[0], CLASS.gwAttackTeamGatherMediator);
    if (mediators.length !== 1) return null;
    return typeof mediators[0]?.close === 'function' ? mediators[0] : null;
  }

  function hasOpenGwLiveAttackEditor() {
    return Boolean(getOpenGwLiveAttackChoiceMediator());
  }

  function resolveGwLiveAttackChoiceContext(snapshot = null) {
    try {
      const current = snapshot?.kind ? snapshot : detectContextSnapshot();
      if (current?.kind !== 'GW') return null;

      const matches = [];
      for (const popup of getOpenPopupObjectsForBattleMode()) {
        if (!isPotentialLiveAttackTeamGatherClass(className(popup))) continue;

        const mediators = directValuesByClass(popup, CLASS.gwAttackTeamGatherMediator);
        if (mediators.length !== 1) continue;
        const mediator = mediators[0];
        if (typeof mediator?.close !== 'function') continue;

        const slots = directValuesByClass(mediator, CLASS.gwSlotVO);
        if (slots.length !== 1) continue;
        const slot = slots[0];

        const slotMatches = (current.slots ?? []).filter(row => row?.slot === slot);
        if (slotMatches.length !== 1) continue;
        const slotInfo = slotMatches[0];
        if (!slotInfo.canLaunch) continue;

        matches.push({ popup, mediator, slot, slotInfo, snapshot:current });
      }

      return matches.length === 1 ? matches[0] : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  function classifyBattleModePopupClasses(
    popupClasses,
    { trainingSessionOpen = false, trainingWarContextOpen = false } = {}
  ) {
    const names = [...new Set((popupClasses ?? []).map(String).filter(Boolean))];
    const trainingCreateOpen = names.includes(CLASS.demoPopup);
    const trainingEditorOpen = names.includes(CLASS.demoAttackGatherPopup);
    const trainingOpen = trainingCreateOpen || trainingEditorOpen;
    const liveCandidates = names.filter(isPotentialLiveAttackTeamGatherClass);
    const attackContextOpen = names.includes(CLASS.gwAttackPopup) || names.includes(CLASS.cowAttackPopup);

    // Green requires a verified Demo Battle screen plus either HWCT's tracked war
    // training session or a directly verified war-specific popup stack. Tracking
    // allows the Create Battle screen to stay visibly Training before Attack Team
    // Gather opens, without allowing unrelated Demo Battle modes to turn green.
    if (trainingOpen) {
      if ((!trainingSessionOpen && !trainingWarContextOpen) || liveCandidates.length) return null;
      return 'training';
    }

    // Red requires both pieces of evidence: one non-Demo Team Gather and an open,
    // verified GW / CoW attack popup.
    return attackContextOpen && liveCandidates.length === 1 ? 'live' : null;
  }

  function getBattleModeDiagnostics() {
    const popupClasses = getOpenPopupClassNamesForBattleMode();
    const trainingSessionOpen = Boolean(activeDefenseSession || nativeCowSession);
    const attackContextOpen = popupClasses.includes(CLASS.gwAttackPopup) || popupClasses.includes(CLASS.cowAttackPopup);
    const trainingWarContextOpen =
      attackContextOpen &&
      popupClasses.includes(CLASS.demoPopup) &&
      popupClasses.includes(CLASS.demoAttackGatherPopup);
    return {
      mode: classifyBattleModePopupClasses(popupClasses, { trainingSessionOpen, trainingWarContextOpen }),
      popupClasses,
      trainingSessionOpen,
      trainingWarContextOpen,
      trainingCreateOpen: popupClasses.includes(CLASS.demoPopup),
      trainingEditorOpen: popupClasses.includes(CLASS.demoAttackGatherPopup),
      attackContextOpen,
      candidateGatherClasses: popupClasses.filter(isPotentialLiveAttackTeamGatherClass),
    };
  }


  function isNativeUiOcclusionOpen() {
    return Boolean(
      document.querySelector('section.sidebar.sidebar_open, #meya_support_wrapper')
    );
  }

  function syncNativeUiOcclusionVisibility() {
    const visibility = isNativeUiOcclusionOpen() ? 'hidden' : '';
    for (const id of [BATTLE_MODE_HOST_ID, PATRON_HOST_ID, REFERENCE_REOPEN_HOST_ID]) {
      const element = document.getElementById(id);
      if (element) element.style.visibility = visibility;
    }
  }

      </div>
    `;
    document.documentElement.appendChild(host);
    syncNativeUiOcclusionVisibility();

    const sign = shadow.getElementById('sign');
    const title = shadow.getElementById('title');
    const resizeHandles = [...shadow.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle')];
    const moveZone = shadow.getElementById('move-zone');
    const uiState = loadUiState();

    // rc11 moves only the sign's OUTER geometry to the shared game-relative
    // coordinate model. The existing title/sword/flame layout is intentionally
    // left unchanged for this stage.
    let sizeScale = Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeScale)
      ? Math.max(.05, uiState.battleModeScale)
      : Math.max(.05, (Number(uiState.battleModeWidthPct) || BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT) / BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT);
    let widthH = Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeWidthH)
      ? Math.max(BATTLE_MODE_MIN_WIDTH_H, uiState.battleModeWidthH)
      : Math.max(
          BATTLE_MODE_MIN_WIDTH_H,
          BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H *
            ((Number(uiState.battleModeWidthPct) || BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT) / BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT)
        );
    let offsetXH = Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeOffsetXH) ? uiState.battleModeOffsetXH : null;
    let offsetYH = Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeOffsetYH) ? uiState.battleModeOffsetYH : null;
    let legacyPositionPending =
      !Number.isFinite(offsetXH) &&
      !Number.isFinite(offsetYH) &&
      Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeX) &&
      Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeY);
    let userMoved =
      (Number.isFinite(offsetXH) && Number.isFinite(offsetYH)) ||
      legacyPositionPending;
    if (!Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeScale)) {
      uiState.battleModeScale = sizeScale;
      saveUiState({ battleModeScale:sizeScale });
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(uiState.battleModeWidthH)) {
      uiState.battleModeWidthH = widthH;
      saveUiState({ battleModeWidthH:widthH });
    }

    let currentMode = null;
    let actualMode = null;
    let suppressed = false;
    let drag = null;
    let resize = null;
    let lastPlaqueWidth = 0;
    let lastAppliedScale = sizeScale;
    let lastAppliedWidthH = widthH;

    function currentLocaleSpec(mode) {
      return {
        training:nativeUiText('combatTraining', t('battleModeTrainingTitle')),
        live:nativeUiText('attack', t('battleModeLiveTitle')),
        mode,
      };
    }

    function referenceVisualScale(game) {
      const referencePlaqueWidth = game.height * BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H;
      return referencePlaqueWidth / BATTLE_MODE_REFERENCE_WIDTH_U;
    }

    function applyScale(
      requestedScale,
      game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry(),
      maxWidth = window.innerWidth - 12,
      requestedWidthH = widthH
    ) {
      let safeScale = Math.max(.01, Number(requestedScale) || 1);
      let safeWidthH = Math.max(
        BATTLE_MODE_MIN_WIDTH_H,
        Number(requestedWidthH) || BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H
      );
      const minWidth = game.height * BATTLE_MODE_MIN_WIDTH_H;
      const widthCap = Math.max(minWidth, maxWidth);
      const requestedWidth = game.height * safeWidthH;
      const width = clamp(requestedWidth, minWidth, widthCap);

      // If the viewport clamps a requested size, shrink the internal artwork by
      // the same ratio. Outer geometry still remains game-height based.
      if (Math.abs(width - requestedWidth) > .01) {
        const clampRatio = width / Math.max(1, requestedWidth);
        safeScale *= clampRatio;
        safeWidthH = width / game.height;
      }

      const visualScale = referenceVisualScale(game) * safeScale;
      host.style.setProperty('--hwct-sign-scale', String(visualScale));
      host.style.width = width + 'px';
      lastPlaqueWidth = width;
      lastAppliedScale = safeScale;
      lastAppliedWidthH = safeWidthH;
      return width;
    }



    function place() {
      if (host.style.display === 'none' || drag || resize) return;
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const width = applyScale(sizeScale, game);
      const signHeight = sign.getBoundingClientRect().height || 1;
      const maxLeft = Math.max(6, window.innerWidth - width - 6);
      const maxTop = Math.max(6, window.innerHeight - signHeight - 6);

      // One-time migration of the old viewport-relative saved position. This
      // preserves the user's current placement, then stores it in game-relative
      // center offsets so future canvas changes move it with Hero Wars.
      if (legacyPositionPending) {
        const legacyLeft = 6 + uiState.battleModeX * Math.max(0, maxLeft - 6);
        const legacyTop = 6 + uiState.battleModeY * Math.max(0, maxTop - 6);
        offsetXH = (legacyLeft + width / 2 - game.centerX) / game.height;
        offsetYH = (legacyTop + signHeight / 2 - game.centerY) / game.height;
        uiState.battleModeOffsetXH = offsetXH;
        uiState.battleModeOffsetYH = offsetYH;
        legacyPositionPending = false;
        saveUiState({ battleModeOffsetXH:offsetXH, battleModeOffsetYH:offsetYH });
      }

      const effectiveOffsetXH = Number.isFinite(offsetXH) ? offsetXH : BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_OFFSET_X_H;
      const effectiveOffsetYH = Number.isFinite(offsetYH) ? offsetYH : BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_OFFSET_Y_H;
      const desiredCenterX = game.centerX + effectiveOffsetXH * game.height;
      const desiredCenterY = game.centerY + effectiveOffsetYH * game.height;
      const left = clamp(desiredCenterX - width / 2, 6, maxLeft);
      const top = clamp(desiredCenterY - signHeight / 2, 6, maxTop);

      host.style.left = left + 'px';
      host.style.top = top + 'px';
    }

    function savePosition() {
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      offsetXH = (rect.left + rect.width / 2 - game.centerX) / game.height;
      offsetYH = (rect.top + rect.height / 2 - game.centerY) / game.height;
      uiState.battleModeOffsetXH = offsetXH;
      uiState.battleModeOffsetYH = offsetYH;
      userMoved = true;
      saveUiState({
        battleModeOffsetXH:offsetXH,
        battleModeOffsetYH:offsetYH,
      });
    }

    function saveSize() {
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      sizeScale = lastAppliedScale;
      widthH = lastAppliedWidthH;
      uiState.battleModeScale = sizeScale;
      uiState.battleModeWidthH = widthH;
      // Keep the legacy percentage in sync for rollback/backward compatibility,
      // but rc11 no longer uses it as the sign's runtime scale source.
      uiState.battleModeWidthPct =
        BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT * (widthH / BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H);
      saveUiState({
        battleModeScale:sizeScale,
        battleModeWidthH:widthH,
        battleModeWidthPct:uiState.battleModeWidthPct,
      });
      lastPlaqueWidth = rect.width;
    }

    moveZone.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
      if (event.button !== 0) return;
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      drag = {
        pointerId:event.pointerId,
        startX:event.clientX,
        startY:event.clientY,
        left:rect.left,
        top:rect.top,
      };
      moveZone.classList.add('dragging');
      moveZone.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    });

    moveZone.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
      if (!drag || event.pointerId !== drag.pointerId) return;
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      const maxLeft = Math.max(6, window.innerWidth - rect.width - 6);
      const maxTop = Math.max(6, window.innerHeight - rect.height - 6);
      const left = clamp(drag.left + event.clientX - drag.startX, 6, maxLeft);
      const top = clamp(drag.top + event.clientY - drag.startY, 6, maxTop);
      host.style.left = Math.round(left) + 'px';
      host.style.top = Math.round(top) + 'px';
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    });

    function finishDrag(event) {
      if (!drag || event.pointerId !== drag.pointerId) return;
      drag = null;
      moveZone.classList.remove('dragging');
      try { moveZone.releasePointerCapture?.(event.pointerId); } catch {}
      savePosition();
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    }

    moveZone.addEventListener('pointerup', finishDrag);
    moveZone.addEventListener('pointercancel', finishDrag);

    function startResize(side, event) {
      if (event.button !== 0) return;
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      resize = {
        side,
        pointerId:event.pointerId,
        startX:event.clientX,
        startY:event.clientY,
        width:rect.width,
        height:Math.max(1, rect.height),
        left:rect.left,
        top:rect.top,
        right:rect.right,
        bottom:rect.bottom,
        centerX:rect.left + rect.width / 2,
        centerY:rect.top + rect.height / 2,
        scale:lastAppliedScale,
        widthH:lastAppliedWidthH,
      };
      event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    }

    function resizeRatioForPointer(event) {
      const dx = event.clientX - resize.startX;
      const dy = event.clientY - resize.startY;
      const sx = resize.side.includes('e') ? 1 : resize.side.includes('w') ? -1 : 0;
      const sy = resize.side.includes('s') ? 1 : resize.side.includes('n') ? -1 : 0;
      const basisX = sx * resize.width;
      const basisY = sy * resize.height;
      const denominator = basisX * basisX + basisY * basisY;
      if (denominator <= 0) return 1;
      return 1 + (dx * basisX + dy * basisY) / denominator;
    }

    function resizeMaxWidth() {
      const ratioWH = resize.width / Math.max(1, resize.height);
      let horizontal;
      if (resize.side.includes('w')) {
        horizontal = resize.right - 6;
      } else if (resize.side.includes('e')) {
        horizontal = window.innerWidth - resize.left - 6;
      } else {
        horizontal = 2 * Math.min(resize.centerX - 6, window.innerWidth - 6 - resize.centerX);
      }

      let verticalHeight;
      if (resize.side.includes('n')) {
        verticalHeight = resize.bottom - 6;
      } else if (resize.side.includes('s')) {
        verticalHeight = window.innerHeight - resize.top - 6;
      } else {
        verticalHeight = 2 * Math.min(resize.centerY - 6, window.innerHeight - 6 - resize.centerY);
      }
      return Math.max(1, Math.min(horizontal, verticalHeight * ratioWH));
    }

    function moveResize(event) {
      if (!resize || event.pointerId !== resize.pointerId) return;
      const game = getHeroWarsGameGeometry();
      const minWidth = game.height * BATTLE_MODE_MIN_WIDTH_H;
      const maxWidth = Math.max(minWidth, resizeMaxWidth());
      const minRatio = minWidth / Math.max(1, resize.width);
      const maxRatio = maxWidth / Math.max(1, resize.width);
      const ratio = clamp(resizeRatioForPointer(event), minRatio, maxRatio);
      const requestedScale = resize.scale * ratio;
      const requestedWidthH = resize.widthH * ratio;
      const nextWidth = applyScale(requestedScale, game, maxWidth, requestedWidthH);
      sizeScale = lastAppliedScale;
      widthH = lastAppliedWidthH;

      const nextRect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      const nextHeight = Math.max(1, nextRect.height);

      let nextLeft;
      if (resize.side.includes('w')) nextLeft = resize.right - nextWidth;
      else if (resize.side.includes('e')) nextLeft = resize.left;
      else nextLeft = resize.centerX - nextWidth / 2;

      let nextTop;
      if (resize.side.includes('n')) nextTop = resize.bottom - nextHeight;
      else if (resize.side.includes('s')) nextTop = resize.top;
      else nextTop = resize.centerY - nextHeight / 2;

      host.style.left = clamp(nextLeft, 6, Math.max(6, window.innerWidth - nextWidth - 6)) + 'px';
      host.style.top = clamp(nextTop, 6, Math.max(6, window.innerHeight - nextHeight - 6)) + 'px';
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    }

    function finishResize(event) {
      if (!resize || event.pointerId !== resize.pointerId) return;
      try { event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture?.(event.pointerId); } catch {}
      resize = null;
      saveSize();
      savePosition();
      event.preventDefault();
      event.stopPropagation();
    }

    for (const handle of resizeHandles) {
      const side = handle.dataset.side;
      handle.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => startResize(side, event));
      handle.addEventListener('pointermove', moveResize);
      handle.addEventListener('pointerup', finishResize);
      handle.addEventListener('pointercancel', finishResize);
    }

    function render(mode) {
      const prefs = getBattleModePreferences();
      actualMode = prefs.indicator && (mode === 'training' || mode === 'live') ? mode : null;
      if (!actualMode) {
        currentMode = null;
        host.style.display = 'none';
        return;
      }

      const spec = currentLocaleSpec(actualMode);
      const visibleMode = actualMode;
      const visibleTitle = visibleMode === 'training' ? spec.training : spec.live;
      currentMode = visibleMode;
      sign.className = `sign ${visibleMode}${prefs.animation ? ' animated' : ''}`;
      title.textContent = visibleTitle;
      host.style.display = '';
      requestAnimationFrame(place);
    }

    function refresh() {
      syncNativeUiOcclusionVisibility();
      const diagnostics = getBattleModeDiagnostics();
      const gwChoiceMediator = diagnostics.mode === 'live'
        ? getOpenGwLiveAttackChoiceMediator()
        : null;
      const pendingGwModeChoice = Boolean(
        gwChoiceMediator &&
        !gwModeChoiceAcknowledgedMediators.has(gwChoiceMediator)
      );
      render((suppressed || pendingGwModeChoice) ? null : diagnostics.mode);
      return diagnostics;
    }

    const timer = window.setInterval(refresh, BATTLE_MODE_POLL_MS);
    const handleResize = () => requestAnimationFrame(place);
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);
    refresh();

    return {
      host,
      refresh,
      diagnostics:getBattleModeDiagnostics,
      getMode:() => currentMode,
      setSuppressed(value) {
        suppressed = Boolean(value);
        refresh();
      },
      resetSize() {
        sizeScale = 1;
        widthH = BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H;
        uiState.battleModeScale = 1;
        uiState.battleModeWidthH = BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H;
        uiState.battleModeWidthPct = BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT;
        saveUiState({
          battleModeScale:1,
          battleModeWidthH:BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_H,
          battleModeWidthPct:BATTLE_MODE_DEFAULT_WIDTH_PCT,
        });
        requestAnimationFrame(place);
      },
      destroy() {
        window.clearInterval(timer);
        window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);
        host.remove();
      },
    };
  }

  function createPanel() {
    if (document.getElementById(HOST_ID)) return null;

    const uiState = loadUiState();
    const host = document.createElement('div');
    host.id = HOST_ID;
    host.style.position = 'fixed';
    host.style.left = '0px';
    host.style.top = '0px';
    host.style.zIndex = '2147483647';
    host.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
    host.style.userSelect = 'none';
    host.style.display = 'none';

    const shadow = host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
    shadow.innerHTML = `
      <style>
        :host { all: initial; }
        * { box-sizing: border-box; }
        .panel {
          position: relative;
          width: 330px;
          border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.20);
          border-radius: 9px;
          background: rgba(24,27,33,.95);
          color: #f6f6f6;
          font: var(--hwct-font-size, 13px)/1.35 sans-serif;
          box-shadow: 0 5px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.42);
          overflow: visible;
          display:flex;
          flex-direction:column;
        }
        ${SHARED_HEADER_SETTINGS_CSS}
        .context { margin-left:7px; color:#c9c9c9; font-size:.92em; font-weight:400; }
        .body { padding: 9px; min-height:0; overflow:auto; }
        .slots { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: calc(4px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1)); }
        .slot-row {
          display: grid;
          grid-template-columns: calc(34px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1)) minmax(0, 1fr);
          align-items: center;
          min-height: calc(29px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          gap: calc(5px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          padding: calc(2px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1)) calc(4px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          border-radius: calc(5px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          background: rgba(255,255,255,.035);
        }
        .slot-row.inactive { opacity: .48; }
        .slot-btn {
          position: relative;
          width: calc(32px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          height: calc(25px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          padding: 0; border: 0;
          border-radius: calc(5px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));

          font:700 var(--hwct-slot-font-size, .95em) sans-serif;

        .slot-btn:disabled { background: #777; color: #ddd; cursor: default; }
        .slot-btn.my-target::after {
          content: ''; position: absolute;
          right: calc(-4px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          top: calc(-4px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          width: calc(9px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          height: calc(9px * var(--hwct-main-scale, 1));
          border-radius: 50%; background: #f22626; border: 1px solid #ffe1c9;
          box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px rgba(0,0,0,.45), 0 0 5px rgba(242,38,38,.8);
          pointer-events: none;
        }
        .player-name {
          min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap;
          color: #f1f1f1;
        }
        .player-name.launchable { cursor:pointer; }
        .state-hint { color: #a8a8a8; }
        .empty-message { padding: 8px 4px; color: #bbb; text-align: center; }
        .status {
          min-height: 18px; margin-top: 8px; padding-top: 6px;
          border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.09); color: #cfcfcf; font-size:.90em;
          word-break:break-word;
        }
        .status.error { color: #ffb3b3; }
        .status.ok { color: #bde8bd; }
        .mini {
          position:relative; width:44px; height:44px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.22); border-radius:9px;
          background:rgba(24,27,33,.96); color:#f1f1f1; cursor:grab; touch-action:none; padding:3px;
          box-shadow:0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,.35); display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:0;
        }
        .mini.dragging { cursor:grabbing; }
        .mini-icon { font:700 27px/27px Arial; color:#e3b65f; text-shadow:0 0 4px rgba(227,182,95,.28); }
        .update-dot-mini { position:absolute; right:3px; top:3px; width:9px; height:9px; border-radius:50%; background:#f04455; border:1px solid #ffd6dc; box-shadow:0 0 5px rgba(240,68,85,.85); pointer-events:none; }
        .update-tip-mini { position:fixed; left:8px; top:8px; z-index:2147483647; width:min(260px, calc(100vw - 16px)); padding:8px 9px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.2); border-radius:7px; background:rgb(16,18,23); color:#eee; font:12px/1.42 sans-serif; white-space:pre-line; box-shadow:0 5px 17px rgba(0,0,0,.55); pointer-events:none; display:none; text-align:left; }
        .mini.update-available:hover .update-tip-mini, .mini.update-available:focus .update-tip-mini { display:block; }
        .modal-backdrop {
          position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 2147483647; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center;
          background: rgba(0,0,0,.38); pointer-events:auto;
        }
        .modal-box {
          width:min(390px, calc(100vw - 36px)); padding:14px 15px 12px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.25);
          border-radius:9px; background:rgba(24,27,33,.985); color:#f6f6f6; box-shadow:0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.58);
          font:12px/1.5 sans-serif; user-select:text;
        }
        .modal-brand { margin-bottom:7px; color:#e3b65f; font:700 12px/1.2 sans-serif; letter-spacing:.2px; }
        .modal-title { font-weight:700; font-size:14px; margin-bottom:7px; }
        .modal-message { white-space:pre-line; color:#ededed; }
        .modal-actions { display:flex; justify-content:flex-end; margin-top:12px; }
        .modal-ok { min-width:72px; padding:6px 13px; border:1px solid #f0c778; border-radius:5px; background:#ddb15e;
          color:#21180a; cursor:pointer; font:700 12px sans-serif; }
        .modal-box.gw-entry-notice { font-size:13px; line-height:1.55; }
        .modal-box.gw-entry-notice .modal-title { font-size:15px; margin-bottom:8px; }
        .modal-box.gw-entry-notice .modal-ok { font-size:13px; }
        .mode-choice-box {
          --hwct-choice-u:1px;
          position:fixed;
          left:50%;
          top:50%;
          transform:translate(-50%, -50%);
          width:390px;
          padding:calc(14 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) calc(15 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) calc(13 * var(--hwct-choice-u));
          border:max(1px, calc(1.4 * var(--hwct-choice-u))) solid rgba(221,184,105,.68);
          border-radius:calc(10 * var(--hwct-choice-u));
          background:linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(67,54,38,.99) 0%, rgba(34,31,29,.995) 18%, rgba(22,24,28,.995) 100%);
          color:#f6f2e8;
          box-shadow:inset 0 calc(1 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) 0 rgba(255,238,190,.18), inset 0 calc(-2 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) 0 rgba(0,0,0,.42), 0 calc(8 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) calc(28 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) rgba(0,0,0,.60);
          font:calc(12 * var(--hwct-choice-u))/1.45 sans-serif;
          user-select:text;
        }
        .mode-choice-title {
          display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; text-align:center;
          gap:calc(7 * var(--hwct-choice-u)); margin-bottom:calc(6 * var(--hwct-choice-u));
          font-size:calc(16 * var(--hwct-choice-u)); color:#f0dfb2;
          text-shadow:0 calc(1 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) calc(2 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) rgba(0,0,0,.75);
        }
        .mode-choice-sword {
          color:#e3b65f; text-shadow:0 0 calc(4 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) rgba(227,182,95,.34);
          font-size:calc(17 * var(--hwct-choice-u)); line-height:1;
        }
        .mode-choice-actions {
          display:grid; grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) minmax(0,1fr); align-items:stretch;
          gap:calc(9 * var(--hwct-choice-u)); margin-top:calc(12 * var(--hwct-choice-u));
        }
        .mode-choice-button {
          --hwct-choice-rim:#b59050;
          --hwct-choice-inner:rgba(255,239,184,.34);
          min-width:0; min-height:calc(42 * var(--hwct-choice-u));
          padding:calc(8 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) calc(10 * var(--hwct-choice-u));
          border:max(1px, calc(2 * var(--hwct-choice-u))) solid #2d1b10;
          border-radius:calc(7 * var(--hwct-choice-u));
          cursor:pointer; color:#fffaf0; font:700 calc(13 * var(--hwct-choice-u))/1.22 sans-serif;
          text-align:center; white-space:normal; overflow-wrap:break-word; word-break:normal; hyphens:auto;
          text-shadow:0 calc(1 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) calc(2 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) rgba(0,0,0,.78);
          box-shadow:
            0 0 0 max(1px, calc(1.1 * var(--hwct-choice-u))) var(--hwct-choice-rim),
            inset 0 0 0 max(1px, calc(1.1 * var(--hwct-choice-u))) var(--hwct-choice-inner),
            inset 0 calc(3 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) 0 rgba(255,255,255,.20),
            inset 0 calc(-4 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) 0 rgba(0,0,0,.36),
            0 calc(4 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) calc(6 * var(--hwct-choice-u)) rgba(0,0,0,.48);
          transition:filter .12s ease, transform .08s ease, box-shadow .08s ease;
        }
        .mode-choice-button:hover:not(:disabled) { filter:brightness(1.08); }
        .mode-choice-button:active:not(:disabled) {
          transform:translateY(calc(1 * var(--hwct-choice-u)));
          box-shadow:
            0 0 0 max(1px, calc(1.1 * var(--hwct-choice-u))) var(--hwct-choice-rim),

        .mode-choice-close:active:not(:disabled) { transform:translateY(calc(1 * var(--hwct-choice-u))); }

        .resize-handle { position:absolute; z-index:20; touch-action:none; }
